=== add test/test-pii-revoke-rewrites-e2e.mjs
new 7eb03cc78a00420ac682f17c
+/**
+ * A REVOKED EXCEPTION is put back behind its token in what was ALREADY RECORDED.
+ *
+ * Stored results are never filtered a second time (a result is filtered once, when it comes back). So a value the
+ * developer cleared, and then un-cleared, would stay readable in every result recorded in between - on screen, in
+ * conversation.jsonl, and in what the model is sent next. Revoking it rewrites the recorded conversation.
+ *
+ * Through the real server, the shipped filter and the panel's own Revoke button (its event route):
+ *   [1] while cleared, the value is in the recorded result and goes to the model
+ *   [2] Revoke: the panel says how many recorded messages were rewritten
+ *   [3] the events, the file on disk and the next request to the model no longer hold it - a token is in its place
+ *   [4] and the rest of the conversation is exactly as it was
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-pii-revoke-rewrites-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile, readdir, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-revoke-home-'));
+const PROJ = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-revoke-proj-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const PORT = 28467;
+const B = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const VALUE = 'Zqhavisham';
+const TOK = /#Xxxxxxxxxx#[0-9a-z]{7}/;
+
+await mkdir(path.join(PROJ, 'conf'), { recursive: true });
+await writeFile(path.join(PROJ, 'conf', 'app.properties'), 'support.contact_name=' + VALUE + '\nbatch.retries=3\n');
+// the developer has cleared it for this project - in the store OUTSIDE the container, where the server's piishape

+][Math.min(i++, 2)];

+  async complete(req) { sent.push(String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content)); return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+};

+const j = async (p, opt) => (await fetch(B + p, opt)).json();
+const post = (p, body) => j(p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
+const ask = async (text, need) => { const n = sent.length; await post('/api/message', { text }); for (let k = 0; k < 100; k++) { await sleep(200); const s = await j('/api/state?limit=20'); if (s.status && s.status !== 'running' && sent.length >= n + need) break; } await sleep(600); };
+const events = async () => (await j('/api/state?limit=100000')).events || [];

+    const out = [];

+  };
+  const heldBefore = await filesWith();

+  const toast = String((r && (r.toast && (r.toast.text || r.toast))) || JSON.stringify(r)).slice(0, 700);
+  ok(/masked again, here and in \d+ recorded message/.test(toast), `the panel says what it did (${toast})`);
+  await sleep(1500);
+
+  console.log('\n[3] it is gone from what was recorded - a token is in its place');
+  const after = await events();
+  const afterText = JSON.stringify(after);
+  ok(!afterText.includes(VALUE) && TOK.test(afterText), 'the events no longer hold it');
+  let disk = ''; for (let k = 0; k < 30; k++) { disk = await onDisk(); if (disk && !disk.includes(VALUE)) break; await sleep(300); }
+  ok(disk.length > 200 && !disk.includes(VALUE) && TOK.test(disk), `nor does conversation.jsonl (${disk.length} chars)`);
+  const n = sent.length;
+  await ask('anything else?', 1);
+  const next = sent.slice(n).join('\n');
+  ok(next.includes('support.contact_name=#') && !next.includes(VALUE), 'and the next request to the model has the token, not the value');

+  ok(/saved file/.test(toast) && /left as it was/.test(toast) && /pii-revoke\.log/.test(toast), `and the panel said both (${toast})`);

=== add test/test-pii-testdata-plugin.mjs
new 80a4c52f0ba9373657c01b5d
+/**
+ * The PII / test-data plugin (examples/plugins/pii-testdata__1.mjs), exercised through the REAL machinery it runs in:
+ * the plugin manager (load + validate + blockedPaths + tools + host.tokens), the token store and the filter pipeline
+ * (the platform masks by the tokens the plugin issues; fail-closed), and the worker (H2/H3 round-trip). Model: a case
+ * is a FAMILY of DEERS beneficiaries — a family-level deers_family_id + members, each with person fields. Tokens
+ * (one grammar, src/tokens.mjs): #@family.member.field#id and #@family.deers_family_id#id, the id the value's.
+ * Proves the guarantee: the model handles only tokens, tools act on real values, real values never enter the transcript.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-pii-testdata-plugin.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, mkdir, rm, copyFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { createPluginManager } from '../src/plugins/index.mjs';
+import { createFilterPipeline } from '../src/plugins/filters.mjs';
+import { createTokenStore, parseToken } from '../src/tokens.mjs';
+import { execute, extractContent } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+import { runMinimalWorker } from '../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const REPO = process.cwd();
+const PLUGIN_SRC = path.join(REPO, 'examples', 'plugins', 'pii-testdata__1.mjs');
+
+const SMITHS = {
+  label: 'smiths', description: 'retiree + dependents',
+  family: { deers_family_id: '20240001' },
+  members: {
+    sponsor: { first_name: 'Gwendolyn', last_name: 'Okonkwo', ssn: '482-11-9037', phone: '555-0142', edi: '1002003004', deers_beneficiary_id: '01', address: { street: '1 Main St', city: 'Lynchburg', zip: '24501' } },
+    spouse:  { first_name: 'Harold', last_name: 'Okonkwo', ssn: '333-22-1111', address: { zip: '73104' } },
+  },
+};
+
+// recursively find the first component node with a given id
+function findNode(node, id) {
+  if (!node || typeof node !== 'object') return null;
+  if (node.id === id) return node;
+  for (const c of (node.children || [])) { const f = findNode(c, id); if (f) return f; }
+  return null;
+}
+
+// ITS OWN HOME: the test data is in the store OUTSIDE the container (vault.mjs) - for a test, a local folder under
+// CB_HOME. Without one the store would be nowhere (vault.mjs refuses the developer's real home).
+process.env.CB_HOME = (await import('node:fs')).mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-pii-home-'));
+const STORE = path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'secure', 'plugins', 'pii-testdata');
+const FAM = path.join(STORE, 'families');
+// this test writes the store's files itself, so it tells the store (whose version is otherwise up to 250 ms old)
+const { _vaultTouch } = await import('../src/vault.mjs');
+const writeFam = async (p, c) => { await writeFile(p, c); _vaultTouch(); };
+const rmFam = async (p, o) => { await rm(p, o); _vaultTouch(); };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+async function setup() {
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-pii-proj-'));
+  const plugDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-pii-plug-'));
+  await copyFile(PLUGIN_SRC, path.join(plugDir, 'pii-testdata__1.mjs'));
+  await mkdir(FAM, { recursive: true });
+  await writeFam(path.join(FAM, 'smiths.json'), JSON.stringify(SMITHS, null, 2));
+
+  // the tokens are code_boss's: the store the server makes, given to the manager (host.tokens) and the pipeline
+  const store = createTokenStore({ secret: 'k' });
+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, tokens: store });
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(plugDir);
+  await mgr.reconcile();
+  const filter = createFilterPipeline(() => mgr.enabledFilters(), { ownNames: () => mgr.exemptNames(), namesVersion: () => mgr.namesVersion(), tokens: store });
+  return { proj, plugDir, mgr, filter, store, cleanup: async () => { await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); await rm(plugDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); } };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const { proj, mgr, filter, store, cleanup } = await setup();
+  const tools = mgr.enabledTools();
+  const ctx = { cwd: proj };
+  const id = (v) => store.idOf(v);
+  // a named token carries its value's format (2026-10-14) - built here by the same function the store uses
+  const { namedTokenFor } = await import('../src/tokens.mjs');
+  const tok = (p, v) => namedTokenFor(p, id(v), v);
+  const T = {
+    ssn: tok('smiths.sponsor.ssn', '482-11-9037'), spouseSsn: tok('smiths.spouse.ssn', '333-22-1111'), fam: tok('smiths.deers_family_id', '20240001'),
+    zip: tok('smiths.sponsor.address.zip', '24501'), last: tok('smiths.sponsor.last_name', 'Okonkwo'), edi: tok('smiths.sponsor.edi', '1002003004'),
+  };
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[A] the plugin LOADS with its tools, blockedPaths, panel - and a read-only filter for the old tokens');
+    const snap = mgr.snapshot().find((r) => r.name === 'pii-testdata');
+    ok(snap && snap.enabled, 'A1: pii plugin enabled');
+    ok(mgr.enabledFilters().some((f) => f.name === 'pii-testdata-legacy' && typeof f.isToken === 'function'), 'A2: its filter vouches for legacy tokens (isToken) - it masks nothing itself');
+    ok(mgr.enabledBlockedPaths().includes('testdata'), 'A3: testdata is blocked');
+    ok(mgr.enabledVerbs().testdata_get === 'testdata_get' && mgr.enabledVerbs().testdata_fields === 'testdata_fields' && mgr.enabledVerbs().testdata_expand === 'testdata_expand', 'A4: tools registered (get/list/fields/expand)');
+    ok(snap && snap.panel && snap.panel.title === 'Test data', 'A5: the plugin advertises its editor panel');
+    ok(store.resolve(T.ssn) && store.resolve(T.ssn).value === '482-11-9037' && store.resolve(T.ssn).kind === 'family' && store.resolve(T.ssn).plugin === 'pii-testdata', `A6: on load it ISSUED a token for every value through host.tokens (${T.ssn})`);
+    ok(store.entries().filter((e) => e.k === 'family').length === 14, `A7: one per stored value, every one hidden (${store.entries().length} entries)`);
+
+    console.log('\n[B] promptAddition lists FAMILIES + members with each field\'s TOKEN, never VALUES');
+    const pa = mgr.promptAdditions(proj).join('\n');
+    ok(/smiths \(retiree \+ dependents\)/.test(pa) && /sponsor:/.test(pa) && /spouse:/.test(pa), 'B1: lists the family + its members');
+    ok(pa.includes('ssn=' + T.ssn) && pa.includes('deers_family_id=' + T.fam) && pa.includes('address.zip=' + T.zip), 'B2: every field with its token');
+    ok(/family-level field: deers_family_id/.test(pa) && /per-member fields:.*ssn/.test(pa) && /edi/.test(pa), 'B3: the schema field names');
+    ok(!pa.includes('482-11-9037') && !pa.includes('Gwendolyn') && !pa.includes('20240001'), 'B4: NO real values leak into the prompt');
+    ok(/EXACTLY as written/.test(pa) && /never ask for a raw value/.test(pa), 'B5: tells the agent how to use a token');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] testdata_get — member + family-level tokens, never values');
+    const m1 = extractContent(await tools.testdata_get.impl({ family: 'smiths', member: 'sponsor', field: 'ssn' }, ctx));
+    ok(m1 === T.ssn, `C1: a member field → its token (${m1})`);
+    const m2 = extractContent(await tools.testdata_get.impl({ family: 'smiths', member: 'sponsor', field: 'address.zip' }, ctx));
+    ok(m2 === T.zip, `C2: a dotted member field (${m2})`);
+    const fam = extractContent(await tools.testdata_get.impl({ family: 'smiths', field: 'deers_family_id' }, ctx));
+    ok(fam === T.fam, `C3: the family-level field (no member) (${fam})`);
+    ok(/no member/i.test(extractContent(await tools.testdata_get.impl({ family: 'smiths', member: 'nobody', field: 'ssn' }, ctx))), 'C4: an unknown member is a clear error');
+    ok(/no family/i.test(extractContent(await tools.testdata_get.impl({ family: 'nope', field: 'ssn' }, ctx))), 'C5: an unknown family is a clear error');
+    ok(/no filled field/i.test(extractContent(await tools.testdata_get.impl({ family: 'smiths', member: 'sponsor', field: 'nope' }, ctx))), 'C6: an unknown field lists what IS available');
+    ok(parseToken(m1).id === id('482-11-9037'), 'C7: the id is the value\'s - what piishape would give the same value');
+
+    console.log('\n[D] the round trip (the core guarantee), through the pipeline the server runs');
+    const fctx = { projectDir: proj, hook: 'request' };
+    const masked = await filter.mask('SSN is 482-11-9037 and family 20240001 and ZIP 24501.', fctx);
+    ok(masked === `SSN is ${T.ssn} and family ${T.fam} and ZIP ${T.zip}.`, `D1: member + family values → their tokens (${masked})`);
+    ok(!masked.includes('482-11-9037') && !masked.includes('20240001'), 'D2: no real value survives the mask');
+    const un = await filter.unmask(masked, { projectDir: proj, hook: 'tool-args' });
+    ok(un === 'SSN is 482-11-9037 and family 20240001 and ZIP 24501.', `D3: unmask restores the real values (${un})`);
+    const twoMembers = await filter.unmask(`sponsor ${T.ssn} spouse ${T.spouseSsn}`, { projectDir: proj, hook: 'tool-args' });
+    ok(twoMembers === 'sponsor 482-11-9037 spouse 333-22-1111', `D4: the same field on two members resolves independently (${twoMembers})`);
+    const seps = await filter.mask('482119037 and 482.11.9037 and 482 11 9037 and 1002003004', { projectDir: proj, hook: 'tool-result' });
+    // each token shows the spelling that was there (2026-10-14) - same name and id, so the same value
+    const ssnId = parseToken(T.ssn).id;
+    ok(['000000000', '000.00.0000', '000␣00␣0000'].every((f) => seps.includes(`#@smiths.sponsor.ssn~${f}#${ssnId}`)) && seps.includes(T.edi) && !/482|9037/.test(seps), `D5: a numeric value masks in any separator layout, its token showing that layout (${seps})`);
+    const cased = await filter.mask('OKONKWO, okonkwo, Okonkwo; but Okonkwos and xOkonkwo', { projectDir: proj, hook: 'tool-result' });
+    const lastId = parseToken(T.last).id;
+    ok(['XXXXXXX', 'xxxxxxx', 'Xxxxxxx'].every((f) => cased.includes(`#@smiths.sponsor.last_name~${f}#${lastId}`)) && /Okonkwos and xOkonkwo/.test(cased), `D6: a name masks in any case, whole words only, its case shown as written (${cased})`);
+    ok((await filter.mask(masked, fctx)) === masked, 'D7: masking a masked text again changes nothing (idempotent)');
+    const shared = await filter.mask('the surname Okonkwo', { projectDir: proj, hook: 'tool-result' });
+    ok(shared === 'the surname ' + T.last, `D8: a value two members share masks to ONE token - the first path by name (${shared})`);
+    const exp = extractContent(await tools.testdata_expand.impl({ id: T.last }, ctx));
+    ok(/2 path\(s\) hold this value/.test(exp) && /smiths: sponsor\.last_name, spouse\.last_name/.test(exp) && !/Okonkwo/.test(exp), `D9: testdata_expand lists every path holding that id, no value (${exp.replace(/\n/g, ' | ')})`);
+    ok(/does not match/.test(extractContent(await tools.testdata_expand.impl({ id: 'zzzzzzz' }, ctx))), 'D10: an id no value has is a clear error');
+    const num = await filter.mask('Beneficiary 01 and 555-0142 here', { projectDir: proj, hook: 'tool-result' });

+      ok(star.a.startsWith('#@a~') && star.b.c.startsWith('#@c~') && star.d.startsWith('#@d~'), 'K18: "*" masks every leaf');
+      const pred = ex.maskFields({ projectDir: proj, data: { keep: 'Halloway', drop_me: 'Ferncastle' }, fields: (k) => k.endsWith('_me') });
+      ok(pred.keep === 'Halloway' && pred.drop_me.startsWith('#@drop_me~'), 'K19: a predicate picks the keys');
+      const dotted = ex.maskFields({ projectDir: proj, data: { address: { zip: '24599', city: 'Lynchburg' } }, fields: ['address.zip'] });
+      ok(dotted.address.zip.startsWith('#@zip~') && dotted.address.city === 'Lynchburg', 'K20: a dotted, index-free path matches just that leaf');
+      const tbl = ex.maskFields({ projectDir: proj, data: [{ id: 9, email: 'zara.quintero@example.mil' }], fields: ['email'], label: 'enrollees' });
+      ok(tbl[0].email.startsWith('#@enrollees.email~'), `K20b: label as a string prefixes the leaf key (${tbl[0].email})`);
+      ok((await filter.unmask(`mail ${tbl[0].email}`, { projectDir: proj, hook: 'tool-args' })) === 'mail zara.quintero@example.mil', 'K20c: a prefixed token still resolves');
+      const fn = ex.maskFields({ projectDir: proj, data: [{ id: 10, phone: '540-555-0199' }], fields: ['phone'], label: (key) => 'providers.' + key.toUpperCase() });
+      ok(fn[0].phone.startsWith('#@providers.phone~'), `K20d: label as a function names the field (lower-cased) (${fn[0].phone})`);
+      const badLabel = ex.maskFields({ projectDir: proj, data: [{ id: 11, note: 'Halberd-Quince' }], fields: ['note'], label: 'bad label with spaces!' });
+      ok(badLabel[0].note.startsWith('#@value~'), `K20e: a label the token grammar cannot hold falls back to "value" (${badLabel[0].note})`);
+      let e2 = ''; try { ex.maskFields({ projectDir: proj, data: rows, fields: [] }); } catch (e) { e2 = e.message; }
+      let e3 = ''; try { ex.maskFields({ projectDir: proj, data: rows, fields: ['ssn'], ttlDays: 0 }); } catch (e) { e3 = e.message; }
+      ok(/fields/.test(e2) && /ttlDays/.test(e3), 'K21: bad calls are clean errors');
+      ok(store.resolve(out[0].ssn).kind === 'value' && store.resolve(out[0].ssn).hidden === true && store.resolve(out[0].ssn).plugin === 'pii-testdata', 'K22: issued as a hidden value, by this plugin');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[L] the LEGACY tokens an old transcript carries: still resolved while this store can vouch for them');
+    {
+      const u = await filter.unmask('old: @@@(smiths.sponsor.ssn) and @@@(smiths.deers_family_id)', { projectDir: proj, hook: 'tool-args' });
+      ok(u === 'old: 482-11-9037 and 20240001', `L1: an old family token resolves (${u})`);
+      const kept = await filter.mask('see @@@(smiths.sponsor.ssn) and @@@(nobody.home) here', { projectDir: proj, hook: 'tool-result' });
+      ok(kept.includes('@@@(smiths.sponsor.ssn)') && !kept.includes('@@@(nobody.home)'), `L2: in a text, one this store vouches for is kept, one it does not is unframed (${kept})`);
+      const refuse = async (t) => { try { await filter.unmask(t, { projectDir: proj, hook: 'tool-args' }); return ''; } catch (e) { return e.message; } };
+      ok(/does not have/.test(await refuse('@@@(nobody.sponsor.ssn)')), 'L3: an old token for an unknown family is refused');
+      ok(/REFERENCE reached a tool/.test(await refuse('@@@{smiths.sponsor}')), 'L4: an old reference reaching a tool is refused');
+      ok(/read-only descriptor/.test(await refuse('###{first_name: sponsor, spouse in smiths}')), 'L5: an old fuzzy descriptor reaching a tool is refused');
+      ok(/no longer holds/.test(await refuse('@@@(email#0123456789abcdef)')), 'L6: an old ad-hoc token nobody holds is refused');
+    }
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await cleanup(); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main();

+/**
+ * The test-data PII plugin (pii-testdata__1), SHIPPED and used end-to-end through the real UI: the agent uses a
+ * stored value by TOKEN without ever seeing it.
+ *   - it auto-installs on a fresh install (bundled, enabled by default) and its "Test data" panel tab appears;
+ *   - the agent writes #@smiths.sponsor.ssn#<id> to a file → the FILE holds the REAL value (H2 unmask on tool args);
+ *   - no request to the model ever carries the real value (H1), the tool-call event keeps the token, and the chat
+ *     never shows the value (H3 re-masks the diff/result);
+ *   - a developer who TYPES the real value into the chat still never sends it to the model (H1 on user input).
+ * Scripted model (no LLM); the store is a JSON file under <project>/testdata/. Run: node test/test-pii-testdata-use-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';

+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29255;
+const REAL = '482-11-9037';
+const TOKEN = () => '#@smiths.sponsor.ssn#' + tokenIdOf(REAL, tokenSecret());
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const TURNS = () => [
+  `Writing the sponsor's SSN by token. <remote-workspace><js>return await write("out.txt", "sponsor ssn: ${TOKEN()}\\n");</js></remote-workspace>`,
+  'Wrote it. TESTDATA_FINAL',
+  'Confirmed. TESTDATA_FINAL2',
+];
+const requests = [];   // every request the model received — the real value must never appear in any of them
+let call = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) { requests.push(req); const T = TURNS(); const t = T[Math.min(call++, T.length - 1)]; yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t } }] }; },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+const contentOf = (m) => typeof m?.content === 'string' ? m.content : Array.isArray(m?.content) ? m.content.map((b) => b?.text || '').join('\n') : '';
+const reqText = (r) => ((r || {}).messages || []).map(contentOf).join('\n') + '\n' + String((r || {}).system || '');
+
+async function main() {

+  // the test data is in the store OUTSIDE the container (vault.mjs) - for a test, a folder under its own CB_HOME
+  const FAM = path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'secure', 'plugins', 'pii-testdata', 'families');

+    // real values on screen is the default now (build 1981); this test asserts what the SERVER sends is masked, by reading the page, so it switches the display swap off first
+    await page.evaluate(async () => { if (revealOn()) await toggleReveal(); });
+
+    console.log('\n[A] it ships: auto-installed on a fresh install, enabled, with its panel tab');
+    await page.waitForFunction(async () => ((await (await fetch('/api/state')).json()).plugins || []).some((p) => p.name === 'pii-testdata' && p.enabled), { timeout: 12000 }).catch(() => {});
+    ok(existsSync(path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'plugins', 'pii-testdata__1.mjs')), 'pii-testdata__1.mjs was written into the plugins folder on boot');

+    ok(plugins.some((p) => p.name === 'piishape' && p.enabled), 'alongside piishape (the mandatory one)');

+
+    console.log('\n[B] the agent USES a stored value by token: the file gets the REAL value, the model never does');
+    await page.type('#input', "write the sponsor's ssn to out.txt");
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('TESTDATA_FINAL'), { timeout: 25000 });
+    await sleep(500);
+    const outFile = path.join(projectDir, 'out.txt');
+    const written = existsSync(outFile) ? await readFile(outFile, 'utf8') : '';
+    ok(written.includes(REAL), `out.txt holds the REAL value — the token was unmasked for the tool (H2) (${JSON.stringify(written.trim())})`);
+    ok(!written.includes('#@'), 'and not the token');
+    const leaked = requests.map(reqText).filter((t) => t.includes(REAL)).length;
+    ok(requests.length >= 2 && leaked === 0, `NONE of the ${requests.length} requests to the model contained the real value (H1/H3)`);
+    // the snippet's tool-call carries the code (the token inside it); its write is a js-step whose label + diff go through the masker
+    const callEv = await page.evaluate(() => (session.events || []).find((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'js' && /write\(/.test(String(e.args?.code || ''))));
+    ok(callEv && String(callEv.args?.code || '').includes(TOKEN()) && !String(callEv.args?.code || '').includes('482-11-9037'), 'the write tool-call event keeps the TOKEN (the real value existed only inside the tool)');
+    const stepEv = await page.evaluate(() => (session.events || []).find((e) => e.type === 'js-step' && e.fn === 'write'));
+    ok(stepEv && !JSON.stringify(stepEv).includes('482-11-9037') && String(stepEv.diff || '').includes(TOKEN()), `the write step's diff shows the TOKEN, never the value (${JSON.stringify(stepEv && stepEv.diff)})`);
+    ok(await page.evaluate((v) => !document.body.innerText.includes(v), REAL), 'the chat never shows the real value');
+    ok(await page.evaluate((v) => !JSON.stringify(session.events || []).includes(v), REAL), 'nor does any persisted chat event (the diff/result were re-masked)');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] a developer who TYPES the real value still never sends it to the model');
+    await page.type('#input', `is the sponsor ssn ${REAL}? confirm`);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('TESTDATA_FINAL2'), { timeout: 25000 });
+    const last = reqText(requests[requests.length - 1]);
+    ok(!last.includes(REAL), 'the typed value was masked on the way out (H1 on user input)');
+    // Look at the developer's OWN message (the last user turn), not the whole request — the plugin's prompt addition
+    // documents the token FORMAT, which would satisfy a request-wide search for the wrong reason.
+    const userMsgs = ((requests[requests.length - 1] || {}).messages || []).filter((m) => m && m.role === 'user').map(contentOf);
+    const typed = userMsgs.reverse().find((t) => /confirm/.test(t)) || '';
+    // (The live tail pins the plugin's format doc into this same message, so display the SPECIFIC replacement, not
+    // the first @@@(…) that happens to appear.)
+    ok(typed.includes(TOKEN()) && !typed.includes(REAL), `the developer's message reached the model with the value's TOKEN in its place (${(typed.match(/#@smiths\.sponsor\.ssn#[0-9a-z]{7}/) || ['none'])[0]})`);
+
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-pii-wall-ui.mjs
new 0de5cc3485a5415e66c53154
+/**
+ * The PII WALL review screen, end to end through the REAL platform path:
+ *
+ *   plugin host.request('ask-panel') → server requestApproval → SSE approval-request → the modal in the REAL UI
+ *   → the developer answers each choice → POST /api/approval with the values → back to the plugin.
+ *
+ * The platform is deliberately PII-BLIND: it renders a component tree and returns the answers. Every PII word on
+ * this screen comes from the plugin's tree, which A6b asserts.
+ *
+ * The wall's whole value is that nothing goes out until a human decides, so the assertions are about the GATE,
+ * not the pixels: Send is disabled until every match is decided, cancelling returns NO decisions (the plugin then
+ * fails closed and withholds the result), and a real click round-trips the per-match answers to the plugin.
+ *
+ * A fixture plugin stands in for browserdebug — it calls the same host request with the same payload shape and
+ * writes whatever comes back to a file, so the test reads the plugin's own view of the answer.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-pii-wall-ui.mjs   (skips if Chrome is not installed)
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 17993;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const chrome = [
+  path.join(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '', 'Google', 'Chrome', 'Application', 'chrome.exe'),
+  path.join('C:', 'Program Files', 'Google', 'Chrome', 'Application', 'chrome.exe'),
+  '/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/usr/bin/chromium-browser',
+].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+if (!chrome) { console.log('Chrome not installed — skipping PII-wall UI test (not a failure).'); process.exit(0); }
+
+const ITEMS = [
+  { id: 'm0', shape: 'ssn', label: 'SSN', value: '482-11-9037',
+    context: { line: 2, before: ['Member record'], hit: 'SSN 482-11-9037 on file', after: ['Status: active'] } },
+  { id: 'm1', shape: 'zip', label: 'ZIP', value: '20191',
+    context: { line: 7, before: [], hit: 'Reston VA 20191', after: [] } },
+];
+
+// A fixture plugin that makes the SAME host request browserdebug's wall makes, then records the answer.
+const TREE = {
+  type: 'stack', gap: 12, children: [
+    { type: 'note', tone: 'muted', text: '2 values in a browser_snapshot result look like PII.' },
+    { type: 'note', tone: 'muted', text: 'browser:probe@http://app.example/patient' },
+    { type: 'section', id: 'sec0', title: 'SSN  -  482-11-9037', children: [
+      { type: 'note', mono: true, segments: [
+        { text: 'Member record\nSSN ' },
+        { text: '482-11-9037', mark: true },
+        { text: ' on file\nStatus: active' } ] },
+      { type: 'choice', id: 'm0', label: 'This value:', options: [{ value: 'mask', label: 'Mask' }, { value: 'allow', label: 'Allow' }] } ] },
+    { type: 'text', id: 'note', label: 'Why:', value: 'seed' },
+    { type: 'section', id: 'sec1', title: 'ZIP  -  20191', children: [
+      { type: 'note', mono: true, text: 'Reston VA 20191' },
+      { type: 'choice', id: 'm1', label: 'This value:', options: [{ value: 'mask', label: 'Mask' }, { value: 'allow', label: 'Allow' }] } ] },
+  ],
+};
+
+// The fixture makes the same GENERIC request the wall makes. Nothing here is platform-side PII knowledge — the
+// vocabulary all lives in the tree the plugin supplies.
+const FIXTURE = (outPath) => `
+import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+const OUT = ${JSON.stringify(outPath)};
+export default {
+  description: 'blocking-panel probe',
+  init(host) {
+    host.request('ask-panel', {
+      title: 'Possible PII - review before sending',
+      tree: ${JSON.stringify(TREE)},
+      requireAll: true, submitLabel: 'Send', cancelLabel: 'Cancel (withhold)',
+    }).then((r) => writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify(r)))
+      .catch((e) => writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify({ threw: String(e && e.message) })));
+  },
+  tools: [],
+};
+`;
+
+async function runOnce({ cancel, offset }) {
+  const portOff = offset != null ? offset : (cancel ? 1 : 0);
+  const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-wallui-home-'));
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-wallui-proj-'));
+  const out = path.join(home, 'answer.json');
+  await mkdir(path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'plugins'), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'plugins', 'wallprobe__1.mjs'), FIXTURE(out));
+  process.env.CB_HOME = home;
+
+  const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs?wallui=' + encodeURIComponent(home));
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: html.replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'mock', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>'),
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'mock',
+    port: PORT + portOff, initialProject: proj, modelsList: [{ id: 'mock' }],
+    adapter: { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'done.' } }] }; },
+      async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'done.' } }] }; },
+      async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock' }] }; } },
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 900 });
+  const errors = [];
+  page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(String(e.message)));
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT + portOff}/`, { waitUntil: 'networkidle2', timeout: 30000 });
+  // The plugin fires its request as it loads (~5s reconcile), so wait for the modal rather than racing it.
+  await page.waitForSelector('#approvalModalBg.show', { timeout: 25000 });
+  return { page, browser, server, out, errors, port: PORT + portOff, cleanup: async () => { try { await browser.close(); } catch {} try { server.close(); } catch {} } };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  // ── [A] the screen, and the gate on Send ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+  console.log('\n[A] the review screen blocks until every match is decided');
+  const r = await runOnce({ cancel: false });
+  try {
+    const shown = await r.page.evaluate(() => {
+      const body = document.getElementById('panelAskBody');
+      return {
+        title: document.getElementById('approvalTitle')?.textContent || '',
+        cards: body ? body.querySelectorAll('[data-pp-choice]').length : 0,
+        text: body ? body.innerText : '',
+        sendDisabled: !!document.getElementById('approvalApproveBtn')?.disabled,
+        count: document.getElementById('panelAskCount')?.textContent || '',
+      };
+    });
+    ok(/PII/i.test(shown.title), `A1: the wall screen is shown (${shown.title})`);
+    ok(shown.cards === 2, `A2: one card per match (${shown.cards})`);
+    ok(/482-11-9037/.test(shown.text) && /20191/.test(shown.text), 'A3: both values are shown');
+    ok(/Member record/.test(shown.text) && /Status: active/.test(shown.text), 'A4: each match shows the lines above and below it');
+    // Context without highlighting is a guessing game: several lines, and no clue which characters are the value.
+    const marked = await r.page.evaluate(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('#panelAskBody .pp-mark')].map((e) => e.textContent));
+    ok(marked.includes('482-11-9037'), `A4b: the matched value is HIGHLIGHTED inside the excerpt (${JSON.stringify(marked)})`);
+    ok(marked.length === 1, 'A4c: only the match is highlighted, not the whole line');
+    ok(shown.sendDisabled === true, 'A5: Send is DISABLED while matches are undecided — nothing goes out by default');
+    ok(/2 still to decide/.test(shown.count), `A6: the undecided count is visible (${shown.count})`);
+    // The platform must carry no PII VOCABULARY of its own. Plugin-supplied TEXT may of course say "PII" — that is
+    // the plugin's word. So assert on the platform's own surface: element ids, classes and data-attributes.
+    const platformIdents = await r.page.evaluate(() => {
+      const body = document.getElementById('panelAskBody');
+      const names = [];
+      const walk = (el) => { names.push(el.id || '', el.className || '');
+        for (const a of el.attributes) names.push(a.name);
+        for (const c of el.children) walk(c); };
+      if (body) walk(body);
+      return names.join(' ');
+    });
+    ok(!/pii|wall/i.test(platformIdents), `A6b: the platform's own ids/classes/attributes carry NO pii or wall vocabulary (${platformIdents.slice(0, 90)})`);
+
+    // Decide ONE — still blocked.
+    await r.page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('#panelAskBody [data-pp-choice="m0"] [data-pp-choice-val="mask"]')?.click());
+    ok(await r.page.evaluate(() => !!document.getElementById('approvalApproveBtn')?.disabled), 'A7: one of two decided → still blocked');
+
+    // Decide the second — now sendable.
+    await r.page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('#panelAskBody [data-pp-choice="m1"] [data-pp-choice-val="allow"]')?.click());
+    await r.page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.querySelector('#panelAskBody input[data-ppid="note"]'); if (i) { i.value = 'edited'; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); } });
+    ok(await r.page.evaluate(() => !document.getElementById('approvalApproveBtn')?.disabled), 'A8: all decided → Send enabled');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] the decisions round-trip to the PLUGIN');
+    await r.page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('approvalApproveBtn')?.click());
+    let answer = null;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !answer; i++) { await sleep(250); try { answer = JSON.parse(await readFile(r.out, 'utf8')); } catch {} }
+    ok(!!answer, 'B1: the plugin received an answer');
+    const d = (answer && answer.values) || {};
+    ok(answer && answer.submitted === true, 'B1b: submitted');
+    ok(d.m0 === 'mask', `B2: the first choice came back as MASK, addressed by the PLUGIN's own id (${JSON.stringify(d)})`);
+    ok(d.m1 === 'allow', 'B3: the second came back as ALLOW');
+    // A component that RENDERS in a blocking panel but never reports is silent data loss for the plugin.
+    ok(d.note === 'edited', `B3b: a TEXT field in a blocking tree returns its value too (${JSON.stringify(d.note)})`);
+    ok(await r.page.evaluate(() => !document.getElementById('approvalModalBg')?.classList.contains('show')), 'B4: the screen closes after sending');
+    ok(await r.page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('approvalModal')?.style.maxWidth === '520px'), 'B5: the widened modal is restored, so the next ordinary approval is not oversized');
+    ok(r.errors.length === 0, `B6: no page errors (${r.errors.join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness[A/B]:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally { await r.cleanup(); }
+
+  // ── [C] cancelling returns NO decisions → the plugin fails closed ─────────────────────────────────────
+  console.log('\n[C] cancelling withholds (fail-closed)');
+  const r2 = await runOnce({ cancel: true });
+  try {
+    await r2.page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('#panelAskBody [data-pp-choice="m0"] [data-pp-choice-val="allow"]')?.click());
+    await r2.page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('approvalCancelBtn')?.click());
+    let answer = null;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !answer; i++) { await sleep(250); try { answer = JSON.parse(await readFile(r2.out, 'utf8')); } catch {} }
+    ok(!!answer, 'C1: the plugin was answered (not left hanging)');
+    ok(answer && answer.submitted === false && answer.values == null, `C2: cancel returns submitted:false with NO values — what that MEANS is the plugin's call (${JSON.stringify(answer)})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness[C]:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally { await r2.cleanup(); }
+
+  // ── [D] a pending review survives a RELOAD ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+  // The blocking panel rides requestApproval, which re-shows a pending request after a refresh. That path
+  // predates this feature and only ever carried a yes/no payload; it now has to carry a whole component TREE.
+  console.log('\n[D] the review screen survives a page reload');
+  const r3 = await runOnce({ offset: 2 });
+  try {
+    await r3.page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('#panelAskBody [data-pp-choice="m0"] [data-pp-choice-val="mask"]')?.click());
+    await r3.page.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle2' });
+    await r3.page.waitForSelector('#approvalModalBg.show', { timeout: 20000 });
+    const after = await r3.page.evaluate(() => {
+      const body = document.getElementById('panelAskBody');
+      return { cards: body ? body.querySelectorAll('[data-pp-choice]').length : 0,
+        text: body ? body.innerText : '',
+        sendDisabled: !!document.getElementById('approvalApproveBtn')?.disabled };
+    });
+    ok(after.cards === 2, `D1: the tree re-renders after reload, not an empty modal (${after.cards} cards)`);
+    ok(/482-11-9037/.test(after.text) && /Member record/.test(after.text), 'D2: values AND context excerpts survive the round-trip through the snapshot');
+    ok(after.sendDisabled === true, 'D3: answers do NOT survive — the reloaded screen re-gates, so a half-answered form cannot be sent by accident');
+    // ...and it is still answerable, i.e. the re-shown modal is wired to the SAME pending request.
+    await r3.page.evaluate(() => { for (const g of document.querySelectorAll('#panelAskBody [data-pp-choice]')) g.querySelector('[data-pp-choice-val="allow"]')?.click(); });
+    await r3.page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('approvalApproveBtn')?.click());
+    let ans = null;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !ans; i++) { await sleep(250); try { ans = JSON.parse(await readFile(r3.out, 'utf8')); } catch {} }
+    ok(ans && ans.submitted === true && ans.values && ans.values.m0 === 'allow',
+      `D4: answering the RE-SHOWN modal still reaches the plugin (${JSON.stringify(ans && ans.values)})`);
+    ok(r3.errors.length === 0, `D5: no page errors across the reload (${r3.errors.join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness[D]:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally { await r3.cleanup(); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

=== add test/test-piiscan-boundaries.mjs
new c209ce477d62830f572a5458
+/**
+ * WHERE the redaction happens, and what it is told when it happens there.
+ *
+ * piiscan decides how hard to look from the FILE it is looking at: a stylesheet gets the strong patterns only, a
+ * .java file gets them plus whatever is inside a string or a comment, and a fixture gets everything. That
+ * judgement is worth nothing if the two boundaries that actually call it never say which file they are holding.
+ *
+ *   H3  a tool result, before it enters the transcript   (minimal-worker)
+ *   H1  every message, on its way to the model           (server → maskByFileContext)
+ *
+ * H1 used to mask each message as one anonymous blob, which meant the strictest possible reading of every line of
+ * every file, every turn — and it silently undid the per-file judgement H3 had already made. This test pins both
+ * halves: the file is passed, and passing it changes the answer.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-piiscan-boundaries.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-piiscan-b-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const P = await import('../examples/plugins/piiscan__1.mjs');
+const { createFilterPipeline } = await import('../src/plugins/filters.mjs');
+const { maskByFileContext, blockFilePath, maskSearchLines } = await import('../src/agent/mask-segments.mjs');
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);
+const pipeline = createFilterPipeline(() => [{ ...P.default.filters[0], plugin: 'piiscan' }]);
+const TOKEN = /\[PII:[A-Z]+#[0-9a-f]{8}\]/;
+
+// A line that is data in a data file and an assignment in a source file. The whole point of the file classes.
+const AMBIGUOUS = 'contact_name = Rutherford Havisham';
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[A] the file changes the answer — otherwise none of the rest matters');
+  {
+    const asData = await pipeline.mask(AMBIGUOUS, { hook: 'request', homeDir: HOME, filePath: 'seed.properties' });
+    const asCode = await pipeline.mask(AMBIGUOUS, { hook: 'request', homeDir: HOME, filePath: 'Registry.java' });
+    ok(TOKEN.test(asData), 'a key/value line in a .properties file is data, and the name is redacted');
+    ok(asCode === AMBIGUOUS, 'the same characters in a .java file are an assignment, and are left alone');
+    const unknown = await pipeline.mask(AMBIGUOUS, { hook: 'request', homeDir: HOME, filePath: null });
+    ok(TOKEN.test(unknown), 'told nothing, it takes the stricter reading — which is why being told matters');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[B] H1 reads the path off each result block');
+  {
+    ok(blockFilePath('<remote-workspace-result op="read" path="src/a.java">x</remote-workspace-result>') === 'src/a.java', 'a path attribute is the file');
+    ok(blockFilePath('<remote-workspace-result op="run" status="ok">BUILD SUCCESS</remote-workspace-result>') === null, 'a shell run names no file');
+    ok(blockFilePath('<remote-workspace-result op="read" path="…Person.java">x</remote-workspace-result>') === null, 'an elided display path is not a file');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[C] H1 masks each block AS its file, and the prose between them as prose');
+  {
+    const convo = [
+      'Here is what I found.',
+      `<remote-workspace-result op="read" status="ok" path="src/Registry.java">${NL}${AMBIGUOUS}${NL}</remote-workspace-result>`,
+      `<remote-workspace-result op="read" status="ok" path="src/seed.properties">${NL}${AMBIGUOUS}${NL}</remote-workspace-result>`,
+      'Both files mention the same person.',
+    ].join(NL);
+    const out = await maskByFileContext(pipeline, convo, { hook: 'request', homeDir: HOME });
+    const [, java, props] = /Registry\.java">([\s\S]*?)<\/remote-workspace-result>[\s\S]*?properties">([\s\S]*?)<\/remote-workspace-result>/.exec(out);
+    ok(!TOKEN.test(java), 'the .java block survives intact');
+    ok(TOKEN.test(props), 'the .properties block beside it is redacted');
+    ok(out.startsWith('Here is what I found.'), 'and the prose around them is still prose');
+
+    // The strong patterns do not care what file they are in — that is what makes them strong.
+    const ssn = `<remote-workspace-result op="read" status="ok" path="src/Registry.java">ssn = "482-11-9037";</remote-workspace-result>`;
+    ok(/\[PII:SSN#/.test(await maskByFileContext(pipeline, ssn, { hook: 'request', homeDir: HOME })), 'an SSN in a .java file is still redacted');
+
+    // A message with no result blocks at all takes the single-call path unchanged.
+    ok(TOKEN.test(await maskByFileContext(pipeline, AMBIGUOUS, { hook: 'request', homeDir: HOME })), 'a plain message is masked as before');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[C2] a search result names a file per LINE, and is masked a line at a time');
+  {
+    // The one result where a stylesheet, a Java class and a fixture arrive interleaved. Masked as one blob they
+    // all get the fixture treatment and the reviewer loses the line they searched for.
+    const hits = [
+      `src/Registry.java:41:  ${AMBIGUOUS}`,
+      `src/seed.properties:7:${AMBIGUOUS}`,
+      '(no matches in 1 other file)',
+    ].join(NL);
+    const out = await maskSearchLines(pipeline, hits, { hook: 'tool-result', homeDir: HOME });
+    const lines = out.split(NL);
+    ok(lines[0] === `src/Registry.java:41:  ${AMBIGUOUS}`, 'the hit in a .java file comes back readable');
+    ok(TOKEN.test(lines[1]) && lines[1].startsWith('src/seed.properties:7:'), 'the hit in a .properties file is redacted, prefix intact');
+    ok(lines[2] === '(no matches in 1 other file)', 'a line that is not a hit passes through');
+    ok(out.split(NL).length === 3, 'and the line count is unchanged, so the result still parses as a search result');
+
+    // A strong pattern does not care what file it is in, and the PATH is still scanned as strictly as ever —
+    // narrowing what we claim about the CONTENT must not narrow what we look for in the path.
+    const risky = [
+      'src/Registry.java:9:  String ssn = "482-11-9037";',
+      'fixtures/601-44-2288/notes.txt:1:see attached',
+    ].join(NL);
+    const out2 = await maskSearchLines(pipeline, risky, { hook: 'tool-result', homeDir: HOME });
+    ok(/\[PII:SSN#/.test(out2.split(NL)[0]), 'an SSN in a .java hit is still redacted');
+    ok(!out2.includes('601-44-2288'), 'and an SSN in the PATH is redacted too');
+    // A bare NAME in a path is not, and that is the documented limit, not an oversight: a capitalised pair with
+    // nothing to confirm it is indistinguishable from two directory names. See PIISCAN-SPEC section 9.
+    const named = await maskSearchLines(pipeline, 'fixtures/Rutherford Havisham/notes.txt:1:see attached', { hook: 'tool-result', homeDir: HOME });
+    ok(named.includes('Rutherford Havisham'), 'a bare name in a path is NOT caught — asserted so it stays a decision');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[D] a filter that throws still rejects the whole message (fail-closed is not weakened)');
+  {
+    const angry = createFilterPipeline(() => [{ name: 'boom', plugin: 'boom', mask: async () => { throw new Error('nope'); } }]);
+    let threw = null;
+    try { await maskByFileContext(angry, `a<remote-workspace-result op="read" path="x.json">b</remote-workspace-result>c`, { hook: 'request' }); }
+    catch (e) { threw = e; }
+    ok(threw && /nope|refus|filter/i.test(threw.message), `a throwing filter rejects the segmented call too (${threw && threw.message})`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[E] H3 tells the filter which file a tool result is the contents of');
+  {
+    const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-piiscan-b-proj-'));
+    await mkdir(path.join(proj, 'src'), { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'src', 'Registry.java'), AMBIGUOUS + NL, 'utf8');
+    await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'src', 'seed.properties'), AMBIGUOUS + NL, 'utf8');
+
+    const seen = [];
+    const spy = {
+      active: () => true,
+      unmaskDeep: async (a) => a,
+      mask: async (text, ctx) => { seen.push({ filePath: ctx.filePath || null, toolName: ctx.toolName }); return pipeline.mask(text, { ...ctx, homeDir: HOME }); },
+    };
+    let turn = 0;
+    const adapter = {
+      async complete() {
+        const i = turn++;
+        if (i === 0) return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'Reading. <remote-workspace><js>return await read("src/Registry.java");</js></remote-workspace>' } }] };
+        if (i === 1) return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'And the other. <remote-workspace><js>return await read("src/seed.properties");</js></remote-workspace>' } }] };
+        return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'Done.' } }] };
+      },
+    };
+    const events = [];
+    await runMinimalWorker({
+      adapter, model: 'm', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'read both files' }], projectDir: proj,
+      requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true,
+      onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {},
+      contentFilter: spy,
+    });
+    const paths = seen.filter((s) => s.filePath).map((s) => s.filePath.replace(/\\/g, '/'));
+    ok(paths.some((p) => p.endsWith('src/Registry.java')), `the read of the .java file names it (${JSON.stringify(paths)})`);
+    ok(paths.some((p) => p.endsWith('src/seed.properties')), 'and so does the read of the .properties file');
+
+    const blob = JSON.stringify(events);
+    ok(/Rutherford Havisham/.test(blob), 'so the .java result reaches the transcript unredacted');
+    ok(/\[PII:NAME#[0-9a-f]{8}\]/.test(blob), 'while the .properties result beside it is redacted');
+    await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+
+  await rm(HOME, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });
