+/**
+ * host.exemptNames(names): a plugin's own vocabulary - the names of the system it works with - is never masked.
+ * The developer (2026-10-10): names a plugin works with came back to the agent as tokens, whole or in pieces; a plugin
+ * must be able to register its names so the filters show them.
+ *
+ *   [1] a plugin declares names in init(); the manager hands every enabled plugin's names together
+ *   [2] through the filter pipeline the server builds (ctx.ownNames), piishape shows them - whole, in any case
+ *   [3] a name only PART of a longer word is not shown on its strength
+ *   [4] each call replaces the plugin's list; bad input is refused
+ *   [5] the authoring brief tells a plugin author about it
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-exempt-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = home;
+const { createPluginManager } = await import('../src/plugins/index.mjs');
+const { createFilterPipeline } = await import('../src/plugins/filters.mjs');
+const { authoringBrief } = await import('../src/plugins/authoring.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const dir = path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'plugins');
+await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'piishape__1.mjs'), await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'examples', 'plugins', 'piishape__1.mjs'), 'utf8'));
+const VOCAB = (names) => `let H;
+export default {
+  description: 'fixture: declares the names it works with',
+  init(h) { H = h; h.exemptNames(${JSON.stringify(names)}); },
+  tools: [{ verb: 'vocab_set', name: 'vocab_set', schema: { description: 'x', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { names: { type: 'array' } } } },
+    impl: async (args) => ({ content: String(H.exemptNames(args.names)) }) }],
+};
+`;
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'vocab__1.mjs'), VOCAB(['ZQASG_HCDP', 'ZQHCDP_PLN_TYP_CD', 'ZQDEERS_FAM_ID']));

+ok(pm.exemptNames().join() === 'ZQASG_HCDP,ZQHCDP_PLN_TYP_CD,ZQDEERS_FAM_ID', `the manager has them (${pm.exemptNames().join(', ')})`);

+const out = await mask(text);
+ok(out === text, `the text comes back as it went (${out})`);
+const control = 'look at a.ZQHCDP_PLN_TYQ_CD in ZQASG_HCDQ a';
+ok(await mask(control) !== control, `the same shape of name NOT declared is still masked (${await mask(control)})`);
+
+console.log('\n[3] part of a longer word is not enough');

+ok(pm.exemptNames().join() === 'ZQONLY_THIS,two words', `the old list is gone (${pm.exemptNames().join()})`);

+ok(/must be an array/.test(said), `a non-array is refused, saying so (${said})`);
+
+console.log('\n[5] the authoring brief tells a plugin author');

+ok(/At an end that is NOT a letter or digit, anything may follow/.test(b) && /EXEMPT ONLY WHAT IS MASKED/.test(b) && /"DAY\("/.test(b), 'and how a name is matched: the edge rule, and to exempt only the word that is masked');
+
+console.log('\n[6] the edge rule: at an end that is not a letter or digit, anything may follow (the developer, 2026-10-11)');
+// "INTERVAL DAY(3) TO SECOND(0)": only DAY is masked (a surname). The agent added the whole types one by one; the
+// one name "DAY(" covers them all, and a bare Day stays masked.
+const before6 = await mask('INTERVAL DAY(3) TO SECOND(0) and Mr Day');
+ok(/INTERVAL #XXX#\w{7}\(3\) TO SECOND\(0\)/.test(before6), `without it, the DAY of the type is masked (${before6})`);
+ok((await tool.impl({ names: ['DAY(', '(ZQPRE', 'TIME ZONE'] }, {})).content === '3', 'names that start or end with a bracket, and one with a space, are kept');

+ok(/Mr #Xxx#\w{7}/.test(types), 'a bare Day - a surname - is still masked');
+ok(!/ZQPRE7/.test(types), `"(ZQPRE" does not show a word it ends inside of: "(ZQPRE7" stays masked (${(types.match(/\(\S+ and/) || [''])[0]})`);
+ok(/WITH TIME ZONE/.test(types), 'a name with a space is shown');
+const notInside = await mask('QZXDAY(3)');
+ok(!/QZXDAY/.test(notInside), `"DAY(" still has to stand apart where it starts with a letter: "QZXDAY(3)" is not shown (${notInside})`);
+
+console.log('\n[7] exempt words make an exempt compound, in any case (the developer, 2026-10-15: "if hi and ho are exempt, hi_ho should be too")');
+// Gold and Silver are surnames: without the names each piece of gold_silver is masked
+const compounds = ['gold_silver', 'GOLD_SILVER', 'Gold_Silver', 'GoldSilver', 'gold-silver', 'gold.silver', 'the GOLD_SILVER plan', '{"plan": "GOLD_SILVER"}', '{"GOLD_SILVER": 1}'];
+ok(!/^GOLD_SILVER$/.test(await mask('GOLD_SILVER')), 'without the names, GOLD_SILVER is masked');
+await tool.impl({ names: ['Gold', 'Silver'] }, {});
+await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2100));
+for (const c of compounds) { const o = await mask(c); ok(o === c, `${c} is shown${o === c ? '' : ' (' + o + ')'}`); }
+const half = await mask('GOLD_SMITH and gold_smith');
+ok(!/SMITH/.test(half) && !/smith/.test(half), `a compound with a piece that is not exempt still masks that piece (${half})`);
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-plugin-failed-row-persists.mjs
new 2b6220740ecbf5ae48906c62
+/**
+ * A BROKEN PLUGIN STAYS VISIBLE. The reconcile loop memoises a file whose bytes failed so it is not re-imported
+ * every 5-second poll — but the issue list is rebuilt each cycle, so the memo hit used to drop the error row: a
+ * plugin with a syntax error showed as failed for exactly one cycle and then vanished, badge and all (found by
+ * _bughunt-r2). Now the remembered row is re-pushed on every cycle until the bytes change.
+ *   [1] a broken file → an error row on the first reconcile, and STILL there on the second and third
+ *   [2] fixing the file → the row goes, the plugin loads, its tool is enabled
+ *   [3] breaking it again with DIFFERENT bad bytes → a fresh error row (the memo is per hash)
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-failed-row-persists.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-failedrow-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = home;
+const { createPluginManager } = await import('../src/plugins/index.mjs');
+const dir = path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'plugins');
+await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
+const file = path.join(dir, 'brk__1.mjs');
+const rows = (m) => m.snapshot().filter((p) => p.name === 'brk').map((p) => ({ enabled: p.enabled, error: String(p.error || '').slice(0, 60) }));
+
+try {
+  const m = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, onHostRequest: async () => null, pluginDataRoot: path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'plugin-data') });
+  m.setPluginsDir(dir);
+
+  console.log('\n[1] a broken file shows as failed on the first reconcile — and stays failed on later polls');
+  await writeFile(file, 'export default { description: "broken", tools: [ this is not javascript', 'utf8');
+  await m.detectAll(); await m.reconcile();
+  const r1 = rows(m);
+  ok(r1.length === 1 && r1[0].enabled === false && /load failed/.test(r1[0].error), `first reconcile: an error row (${JSON.stringify(r1[0])})`);
+  await m.reconcile(); await m.reconcile();
+  const r3 = rows(m);
+  ok(r3.length === 1 && r3[0].enabled === false && /load failed/.test(r3[0].error), `third reconcile: the row is STILL there (${JSON.stringify(r3[0] || null)})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[2] fixing the file clears the row and loads the plugin');
+  await writeFile(file, 'export default { description: "fixed", tools: [{ verb: "brk_hi", name: "brk_hi", schema: { description: "hi", parameters: { type: "object", properties: {} } }, impl: async () => ({ content: "hi" }) }] };', 'utf8');
+  await m.reconcile();
+  const r4 = rows(m);
+  ok(r4.length === 1 && r4[0].enabled === true, `the plugin is loaded and enabled (${JSON.stringify(r4[0] || null)})`);
+  ok(Object.keys(m.enabledTools() || {}).includes('brk_hi'), 'its tool is on');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] breaking it again with different bad bytes → a fresh error row');
+  await writeFile(file, 'export default { description: "broken again", tools: [ still not javascript', 'utf8');
+  await m.reconcile(); await m.reconcile();
+  const r5 = rows(m);
+  ok(r5.length === 1 && r5[0].enabled === false && /load failed/.test(r5[0].error), `failed again, and the row persists across a second poll (${JSON.stringify(r5[0] || null)})`);
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { await cleanupDir(home); }
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-plugin-host-request.mjs
new 8ce545dc7f6e246d70db9c12
+/**
+ * Plugin↔host channel (Layer 1): a plugin's init(host) can request things from the SYSTEM unsolicited, via a
+ * generic host.request(type, payload) → Promise, and the result round-trips back into the plugin — no agent
+ * involved. Also: unloading the plugin aborts an in-flight request. Run: node test/test-plugin-host-request.mjs
+ */
+import { createPluginManager } from '../src/plugins/index.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-plughost-'));
+
+// [1] init(host) round-trips a request to the system, and the RESULT comes back into the plugin.
+{
+  // A plugin that, on init, pings the system and then reports the result back via a second request.
+  const src = [
+    'export default {',
+    '  async init(host) {',
+    '    const r = await host.request("ping", { hello: "world" });',
+    '    await host.request("observed", { pong: r, myName: host.pluginName });',
+    '  },',
+    '};',
+  ].join('\n');
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'hosttest__1.0.0.mjs'), src);
+
+  const calls = [];
+  const onHostRequest = async (name, type, payload) => {
+    calls.push({ name, type, payload });
+    if (type === 'ping') return { pong: true, plugin: name, echo: payload };
+    return { ok: true };
+  };
+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, onHostRequest });
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(dir);
+  await mgr.reconcile();
+
+  const ping = calls.find((c) => c.type === 'ping');
+  ok(!!ping, 'plugin init reached the system via host.request');
+  ok(ping && ping.name === 'hosttest', `the request carries the plugin's identity (got: ${ping && ping.name})`);
+  ok(ping && ping.payload && ping.payload.hello === 'world', 'the request carries the payload');
+  const observed = calls.find((c) => c.type === 'observed');
+  ok(observed && observed.payload.pong && observed.payload.pong.pong === true && observed.payload.pong.echo.hello === 'world',
+    'the system RESULT round-tripped back INTO the plugin (host.request resolves with it)');
+  ok(observed && observed.payload.myName === 'hosttest', 'host.pluginName is the plugin identity');
+
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(null);
+  await mgr.reconcile();   // unload
+}
+
+// [2] an unknown host-request type rejects (the plugin sees the error, run not crashed).
+{
+  const src = [
+    'export default {',
+    '  async init(host) {',
+    '    try { await host.request("nope", {}); host.__err = "no-throw"; }',
+    '    catch (e) { globalThis.__cbHostErr = String(e && e.message || e); }',
+    '  },',
+    '};',
+  ].join('\n');
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'hosterr__1.0.0.mjs'), src);
+  const onHostRequest = async (name, type) => { if (type === 'ping') return { pong: true }; throw new Error(`unknown host request type: "${type}"`); };
+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, onHostRequest });
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(dir);
+  await mgr.reconcile();
+  ok(/unknown host request type/.test(globalThis.__cbHostErr || ''), `an unknown type rejects into the plugin (got: ${globalThis.__cbHostErr})`);
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(null); await mgr.reconcile();
+}

+// [3] a plugin with NO init still loads fine, and a bad init type is rejected at validation.
+{

+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'noinit__1.0.0.mjs'), 'export default { tools: [{ verb: "noop", name: "noop_x", schema: { parameters: { type: "object", properties: {} } }, impl: async () => "ok" }] };');

+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, onHostRequest: async () => ({}) });
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(dir);
+  await mgr.reconcile();

+  ok(snap.some((r) => r.name === 'badinit' && /init must be a function/.test(r.error || '')), 'a non-function init is rejected by validation');
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(null); await mgr.reconcile();
+}
+
+// [4] the channel PERSISTS past init — a request fired later (the real background use case) still round-trips.
+{
+  const d2 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-plughost-defer-'));
+  const src = [
+    'export default {',
+    '  init(host) {',
+    '    globalThis.__cbDeferred = { init: true };',
+    '    setTimeout(() => { host.request("later", { n: 7 }).then((r) => { globalThis.__cbDeferred = { got: r }; }); }, 15);',
+    '  },',
+    '};',
+  ].join('\n');
+  await writeFile(path.join(d2, 'defer__1.0.0.mjs'), src);
+  const calls = [];
+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, onHostRequest: async (name, type, payload) => { calls.push({ type, payload }); return { echoed: payload.n }; } });
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(d2); await mgr.reconcile();   // init returns immediately; the request fires ~15ms LATER
+  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 80));
+  ok(calls.some((c) => c.type === 'later' && c.payload.n === 7), 'host.request works AFTER init returns (persistent channel — the background use case)');
+  ok(globalThis.__cbDeferred && globalThis.__cbDeferred.got && globalThis.__cbDeferred.got.echoed === 7, 'the deferred result round-tripped back to the plugin');
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(null); await mgr.reconcile();
+  await rm(d2, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+
+// [5] unloading the plugin ABORTS an in-flight request (the abort signal reaches the handler → it settles).
+{
+  const d3 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-plughost-abrt-'));
+  const src = [
+    'export default {',
+    '  init(host) {',
+    '    globalThis.__cbAbort = { pending: true };',
+    '    host.request("never", {}).then(() => { globalThis.__cbAbort = { resolved: true }; })',
+    '      .catch((e) => { globalThis.__cbAbort = { rejected: String(e && e.message || e) }; });',
+    '  },',
+    '};',
+  ].join('\n');
+  await writeFile(path.join(d3, 'abrt__1.0.0.mjs'), src);
+  const onHostRequest = (name, type, payload, signal) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    if (type === 'never') signal.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(new Error('cancelled: plugin unloaded')), { once: true });
+    else resolve({});
+  });
+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, onHostRequest });
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(d3); await mgr.reconcile();
+  ok(globalThis.__cbAbort && globalThis.__cbAbort.pending, 'the request is pending while the plugin is loaded');
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(null); await mgr.reconcile();   // unload → the plugin's AbortController fires
+  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 30));

+  await rm(d3, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+
+// [6] no onHostRequest wired → host.request rejects gracefully (never hangs, never throws into the loop).
+{
+  const d4 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-plughost-nohost-'));
+  const src = [
+    'export default {',
+    '  init(host) { host.request("x", {}).then(() => { globalThis.__cbNoHost = "resolved"; }).catch((e) => { globalThis.__cbNoHost = String(e && e.message || e); }); },',
+    '};',
+  ].join('\n');
+  await writeFile(path.join(d4, 'nohost__1.0.0.mjs'), src);
+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {} });   // NO onHostRequest
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(d4); await mgr.reconcile();
+  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 20));
+  ok(/not available|no host/.test(globalThis.__cbNoHost || ''), `host.request rejects gracefully with no host wired (got: ${globalThis.__cbNoHost})`);
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(null); await mgr.reconcile();
+  await rm(d4, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+
+await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

new e053f6feaa301f173f8fc4ec
+/**
+ * PLUGIN ↔ PLUGIN. A plugin may export functions for other plugins (`exports`, reached via host.use(name)) and may
+ * run another plugin's TOOL (host.callTool). Both refuse an absent/disabled partner with the reason.
+ *   [1] host.use(name).fn(...) calls the partner's export
+ *   [2] host.callTool(name, tool, args) runs the partner's tool through the executor — schema validation included
+ *   [3] a partner that is not loaded → a clear error, not a crash
+ *   [4] `exports` with a non-function is refused at load (error row on the panel)
+ *   [5] a declared dependency that is missing → refused at load, with the reason
+ *   [6] the host.use view is LIVE: the partner's file changes, and the next call runs the new function
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-interop.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-interop-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = home;
+const { createPluginManager } = await import('../src/plugins/index.mjs');
+const dir = path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'plugins');
+await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
+
+const ALPHA = (greeting) => `export default {
+  description: 'alpha: exports + a tool',
+  exports: { greet(who) { return '${greeting}, ' + who; }, twice(n) { return n * 2; }, async later(n) { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 30)); return 'later:' + n; } },
+  tools: [{ verb: 'alpha_echo', name: 'alpha_echo', schema: { description: 'echo', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { x: { type: 'number' } }, required: ['x'] } },
+    impl: async (args, ctx) => ({ content: 'alpha saw ' + JSON.stringify(args) + ' for ' + (ctx.host ? ctx.host.pluginName : 'nobody') }) },
+    { verb: 'alpha_fail', name: 'alpha_fail', schema: { description: 'always fails', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } },
+      impl: async () => { throw new Error('the page never loaded'); } },
+    { verb: 'alpha_soft', name: 'alpha_soft', schema: { description: 'reports a failure as its RESULT', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } },
+      impl: async () => ({ content: 'ERROR: 3 tests failed' }) }],
+};
+`;
+const BETA = `let host;
+export default {
+  description: 'beta: calls alpha',
+  dependencies: { alpha: true },
+  init(h) { host = h; },
+  tools: [{ verb: 'beta_run', name: 'beta_run', schema: { description: 'run', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { mode: { type: 'string' } } } },
+    impl: async (args, ctx) => {
+      const h = ctx.host || host;
+      if (args.mode === 'use') return { content: h.use('alpha').greet('beta') };
+      if (args.mode === 'twice') return { content: String(h.use('alpha').twice(21)) };
+      if (args.mode === 'later') return { content: await h.use('alpha').later(7) };
+      if (args.mode === 'call') return { content: await h.callTool('alpha', 'alpha_echo', { x: 7 }) };
+      if (args.mode === 'call-ctx') return { content: await h.callTool('alpha', 'alpha_echo', { x: 8 }, ctx) };
+      if (args.mode === 'call-bad') { try { return { content: 'no error?! ' + await h.callTool('alpha', 'alpha_echo', { y: 1 }) }; } catch (e) { return { content: 'ERR ' + e.message }; } }
+      if (args.mode === 'call-fail') { try { return { content: 'no error?! ' + await h.callTool('alpha', 'alpha_fail', {}) }; } catch (e) { return { content: 'ERR ' + e.message }; } }
+      if (args.mode === 'call-soft') return { content: 'GOT ' + await h.callTool('alpha', 'alpha_soft', {}) };
+      if (args.mode === 'missing') { try { h.use('gamma'); return { content: 'no error?!' }; } catch (e) { return { content: 'ERR ' + e.message }; } }
+      if (args.mode === 'has') return { content: String(h.has('alpha')) + '/' + String(h.has('gamma')) };
+      if (args.mode === 'opt') { const g = h.use('gamma', { optional: true }); const a = h.use('alpha', { optional: true }); return { content: (g ? 'gamma:yes' : 'gamma:none') + ' ' + (a ? a.greet('opt') : 'alpha:none') }; }
+      if (args.mode === 'no-tool') { try { await h.callTool('alpha', 'nope', {}); return { content: 'no error?!' }; } catch (e) { return { content: 'ERR ' + e.message }; } }
+      return { content: '?' };
+    } }],
+};
+`;
+const BAD_EXPORTS = `export default { description: 'bad exports', exports: { notAFunction: 42 }, tools: [] };\n`;
+const NEEDS_MISSING = `export default { description: 'needs a plugin that is not there', dependencies: { nosuchplugin: true }, tools: [{ verb: 'dep_t', name: 'dep_t', schema: { description: 'x', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } }, impl: async () => ({ content: 'x' }) }] };\n`;
+
+const row = (m, name) => m.snapshot().find((p) => p.name === name);
+const run = async (m, tool, args) => { const t = m.enabledTools()[tool]; if (!t) throw new Error(`tool ${tool} not enabled`); const r = await t.impl(args, {}); return typeof r === 'string' ? r : (r && r.content != null ? String(r.content) : JSON.stringify(r)); };
+
+try {
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'alpha__1.mjs'), ALPHA('hello'), 'utf8');
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'beta__1.mjs'), BETA, 'utf8');
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'badex__1.mjs'), BAD_EXPORTS, 'utf8');
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'needs__1.mjs'), NEEDS_MISSING, 'utf8');
+  const m = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, onHostRequest: async () => null, pluginDataRoot: path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'plugin-data') });
+  m.setPluginsDir(dir);
+  await m.detectAll(); await m.reconcile();
+
+  console.log('\n[1] host.use(name).fn(...) reaches the partner\'s export');
+  ok((await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'use' })) === 'hello, beta', 'greet() ran with the argument');
+  ok((await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'twice' })) === '42', 'a second export, a number back');
+  ok((await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'later' })) === 'later:7', 'an ASYNC export returns a Promise the caller awaits — the live view passes it through');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] host.callTool runs the partner\'s tool through the executor');
+  const c = await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'call' });
+  ok(/alpha saw \{"x":7\}/.test(c), `the tool ran with the args (${c})`);
+  ok(/for alpha$/.test(c), 'and with ITS OWN host (not the caller\'s)');
+  const cc = await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'call-ctx' });
+  ok(/for alpha$/.test(cc), `its own host even when the caller passes ITS OWN ctx along - ctx.host is the caller's (${cc})`);
+  // A call that could not be made THROWS — named, so the caller's own error says whose tool it was. A tool that
+  // deliberately RETURNS "ERROR: …" still returns it (its choice of result shape).
+  const bad = await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'call-bad' });
+  ok(/^ERR alpha\.alpha_echo\(\) failed: .*required parameter "x"/.test(bad), `the partner's schema still gates the call, and the refusal THROWS (${bad.slice(0, 90)})`);
+  ok(/ONE args object/.test(bad) && !/tag body|attribute/.test(bad), 'and it says how to fix it in a function call, not in tag syntax that no longer exists');
+  const fail = await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'call-fail' });
+  ok(/^ERR alpha\.alpha_fail\(\) failed: the page never loaded$/.test(fail), `a partner tool that throws makes callTool throw, with its own message (${fail})`);
+  const soft = await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'call-soft' });
+  ok(soft === 'GOT ERROR: 3 tests failed', `a partner tool that RETURNS "ERROR: …" still returns it as data (${soft})`);
+  const noTool = await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'no-tool' });
+  ok(/has no tool "nope"/.test(noTool) && /alpha_echo/.test(noTool), `an unknown tool names what the partner does have (${noTool.slice(0, 80)})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a partner that is not loaded → a clear error');
+  const miss = await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'missing' });
+  ok(/plugin "gamma" is not loaded/.test(miss), `the error says which plugin and who asked (${miss.slice(0, 80)})`);
+  // OPTIONAL partner: check with has(), or ask with { optional: true } and get null instead of a throw.
+  ok((await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'has' })) === 'true/false', 'host.has(name): true for a loaded partner, false for an absent one');
+  ok((await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'opt' })) === 'gamma:none hello, opt', 'use(name, { optional: true }) → null for an absent partner, the live view for a present one');
+
+  console.log('\n[4] `exports` with a non-function is refused at load');
+  const badRow = row(m, 'badex');
+  ok(!!badRow && badRow.enabled === false && /exports\.notAFunction must be a function/.test(badRow.error || ''), `refused with the field named (${badRow && badRow.error})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[5] a declared dependency that is missing → refused at load, with the reason');
+  const needsRow = row(m, 'needs');
+  ok(!!needsRow && needsRow.enabled === false && /nosuchplugin/.test(needsRow.error || ''), `disabled, naming the missing dependency (${needsRow && needsRow.error})`);
+  ok(!m.enabledTools().dep_t, 'its tool is not offered');
+
+  console.log('\n[6] the host.use view is LIVE across the partner\'s hot-reload');
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'alpha__1.mjs'), ALPHA('howdy'), 'utf8');
+  await m.reconcile();
+  ok((await run(m, 'beta_run', { mode: 'use' })) === 'howdy, beta', 'the next call ran the NEW greet — no stale function');
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { await cleanupDir(home); }
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

=== add test/test-plugin-load-row.mjs
new 1e823e311f9cdf0fca9374b1
+/**
+ * Companion to test-agents-reload-row: when a USER plugin LOADS, surface it in the conversation as a tool-use row
+ * — so the developer SEES which plugins loaded. Built-ins never produce a row (always present); an erroring
+ * plugin stays in the warning icon, not here.
+ *
+ * The row is for a plugin that appears or changes WHILE the session runs — the developer just dropped a file or
+ * edited one, and this is how they learn it took (the poll picks it up within ~5s). What was already there when
+ * the session started is not news: it is in Settings, in the status bar and in the prompt, and since a plugin
+ * ships with the app, a row on open would sit at the top of every conversation in every project forever.
+ * Verifies: the load row for a file dropped mid-session, silence while developer-disabled, and a fresh row on
+ * re-enable.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-load-row.mjs
+ */
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { userPluginsDir } from '../src/plugins/paths.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const delay = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PORT = 29870 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30);
+
+async function pluginRows() {
+  const s = await (await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + '/api/state?limit=300')).json();
+  const results = new Map((s.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.verb === 'plugin').map((e) => [e.callId, e]));
+  return (s.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'plugin')
+    .map((c) => ({ summary: c.args?.command || '', result: results.get(c.callId)?.content || '' }));
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-plugrow-'));
+  process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-plugrow-home-'));   // plugins are GLOBAL — isolate the home
+  const pdir = userPluginsDir();
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  const JIRA = `export default { description:'Jira issues', author:'Phil', tools:[{ verb:'jira_create', name:'jira_create', schema:{ description:'create', parameters:{ type:'object', properties:{} } }, impl: async()=>({ content:'created' }) }] };`;
+  const adapter = { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'hi' } }] }; } };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], port: PORT, adapter, initialProject: proj });
+  const get = async (p) => (await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + p)).json();
+  const post = async (p, b) => (await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(b) })).json();
+  try {
+    console.log('=== nothing is announced for what was already there when the session started ===');
+    // The shipped plugin loaded during the first scan. That is not news and must not put a row at the top of
+    // every conversation — the roster is in Settings, the status bar and the prompt.
+    await delay(1200);
+    ok((await pluginRows()).length === 0, `no load row on open (${JSON.stringify((await pluginRows()).map((r) => r.summary))})`);
+
+    console.log('\n=== a plugin dropped MID-SESSION loads by default and a row reports it ===');
+    await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'jira__1.mjs'), JIRA);
+    let j = null;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const s = await get('/api/state'); j = (s.plugins || []).find((r) => r.name === 'jira'); if (j && j.enabled) break; await delay(300); }
+    ok(!!j && j.enabled === true, 'the dropped plugin is LOADED (default-load, no Enable step)');
+    let rows = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { rows = await pluginRows(); if (rows.some((x) => /loaded 1/.test(x.summary))) break; await delay(300); }
+    const loadRow = rows.find((x) => /loaded 1/.test(x.summary) && /plugin/.test(x.summary));
+    ok(!!loadRow, `a "loaded 1 ... plugin" row appeared (${JSON.stringify(rows.map((r) => r.summary))})`);
+    ok(loadRow && /jira v1/.test(loadRow.result), 'the row lists the plugin name + version (jira v1)');
+
+    console.log('\n=== disable → unload (no new load row); re-enable → a fresh load row ===');
+    const rowCountAfterOpen = (await pluginRows()).length;
+    const dr = await post('/api/plugin-toggle', { name: 'jira', disabled: true });
+    ok(dr.ok === true, 'disable returned ok');
+    await delay(400);
+    ok((await pluginRows()).length === rowCountAfterOpen, 'disabling produces no load row (rows are for LOADS)');
+    const er = await post('/api/plugin-toggle', { name: 'jira', disabled: false });
+    ok(er.ok === true, 're-enable returned ok');
+    let rows2 = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { rows2 = await pluginRows(); if (rows2.length > rowCountAfterOpen) break; await delay(200); }
+    const reRow = rows2.slice(rowCountAfterOpen).find((x) => /loaded 1/.test(x.summary));
+    ok(!!reRow && /jira v1/.test(reRow.result), `re-enabling produces a fresh load row (${JSON.stringify(rows2.map((r) => r.summary))})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally { try { server.close(); } catch {} }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });
+

+/**
+ * Runtime plugin loader (USER-PLUGIN-SYSTEM-SPEC §4/§5): exercises createPluginManager's reconcile against a

+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const find = (snap, name) => snap.find((r) => r.name === name);
+const GOOD = (content = 'did it') => `export default { description:'a test plugin', promptAddition:'Use <foo_do/>.', tools:[{ verb:'foo_do', name:'foo_do', schema:{ description:'do', parameters:{ type:'object', properties:{} } }, impl: async()=>({ content:'${content}' }) }] };`;

+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-plugdir-'));

+    'points at the INSTALLED PLUGINS list as the build result, and decodes ENABLED/DISABLED');
+  ok(/ABSENT entirely/i.test(BRIEF), 'covers the plugin that never appears (wrong path/filename)');
+  ok(/SAME reply fails as an unknown function/i.test(BRIEF), 'warns the new verb is only callable from the NEXT turn');
+  ok(/hot-reloads in place/i.test(BRIEF), 'describes iterating by editing the installed file');
+  ok(/NEVER ask the developer to restart/i.test(BRIEF), 'forbids the "please restart / click Enable" dead-end');

+  // named `write` would load fine and be unreachable forever. Refused at load, with the reason, instead.
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'evil__1.mjs'), `export default { tools:[{ verb:'evil_write', name:'write', schema:{ description:'x', parameters:{type:'object',properties:{}} }, impl: async()=>'x' }] };`);

+  await rm(path.join(dir, 'evil__1.mjs'));
+  await mgr.reconcile();

+    await rm(dir2, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+
+  // ── RAW-SPAWN GATE ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+  // A plugin may not start its OWN processes: one started via child_process is invisible to code_boss (no job
+  // id, so <kill-background> cannot stop it, <bg-status> cannot list it, and project-close cannot reap it).
+  // Observed live: a plugin spawned Chrome that way, the agent had no id, and reached for `taskkill /IM
+  // chrome.exe` — which would have closed every browser window the developer had open, including the code_boss
+  // UI. The gate reads the SOURCE TEXT, so the module is never imported and its top-level code never runs.
+  console.log('\n[I] a plugin that imports child_process is REFUSED, with the replacement named');
+  {
+    const dir3 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rawspawn-'));
+    const mgr3 = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {} });
+    mgr3.setPluginsDir(dir3);
+    const TOOL = `tools:[{ verb:'x_go', name:'x_go', schema:{ description:'go', parameters:{ type:'object', properties:{} } }, impl: async()=>({content:'ok'}) }]`;
+    for (const [file, src, label] of [
+      ['imp__1.mjs', `import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';\nexport default { ${TOOL} };`, 'static import'],
+      ['req__1.mjs', `const cp = require('child_process');\nexport default { ${TOOL} };`, 'require'],
+      ['dyn__1.mjs', `export default { async init(){ await import('node:child_process'); }, ${TOOL} };`, 'dynamic import'],
+    ]) {
+      await writeFile(path.join(dir3, file), src);
+      await mgr3.reconcile();
+      const row = find(mgr3.snapshot(), file.split('__')[0]);
+      ok(row && row.enabled === false && /do NOT start your own processes/.test(row.error || ''), `I: a plugin using ${label} is DISABLED`);
+      ok(row && /host\.spawn/.test(row.error || ''), `I: …and the error names host.spawn as the replacement (${label})`);
+      await rm(path.join(dir3, file));
+    }
+    // The sanctioned shape still loads.
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir3, 'okp__1.mjs'), `let HOST=null;\nexport default { init(h){ HOST=h; }, ${TOOL} };`);
+    await mgr3.reconcile();
+    const okRow = find(mgr3.snapshot(), 'okp');
+    ok(okRow && okRow.enabled === true, 'I: a plugin using host.spawn (no child_process import) loads normally');
+    // host.spawn / killSpawned / spawnStatus are wired onto the host object handed to init().
+    const seen = {};
+    const mgr4 = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, onHostRequest: async (name, type, payload) => { seen[type] = payload; return { id: 'bg-9', pid: 42 }; } });
+    const dir4 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-hostspawn-'));
+    mgr4.setPluginsDir(dir4);
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir4, 'hs__1.mjs'), `let H=null;\nexport default { init(h){ H=h; H.spawn('java',['-jar','x.jar'],{cwd:'sub'}); }, ${TOOL} };`);
+    await mgr4.reconcile();
+    ok(seen['spawn-process'] && seen['spawn-process'].file === 'java', `I: host.spawn routes to the spawn-process host request (${JSON.stringify(seen['spawn-process'] || null)})`);
+    ok(Array.isArray(seen['spawn-process'].argv) && seen['spawn-process'].cwd === 'sub', 'I: argv + cwd reach the host untouched');
+    await rm(dir3, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    await rm(dir4, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

+/**
+ * plugin_log — the one read the agent gets inside .code_boss: the plugin-tagged tail of the project log, filtered by
+ * plugin name. An agent authoring a plugin could not otherwise see its own plugin's host.log lines, init errors or
+ * scan-probe failures (the control dir is off-limits; the developer had to paste from Settings → Log).
+ *   [A] through execute(): only [plugin]-scoped lines; name= keeps that plugin's host.log, "plugin X loaded/init
+ *       error" and "[X] scan probe" lines and drops another plugin's; grep= narrows; lines= caps; unknown name and a
+ *       missing log are clear messages; no project is an error; the spine noise is stripped; both spellings
+ *       (plugin_log / pluginLog) are snippet functions, take the same args and stay in the read-only scope
+ *   [B] the ordinary read/search tools STILL refuse the same file (the control-dir guard is untouched)
+ *   [C] through the real server with a scripted model: a log line holding a test-data value reaches the transcript
+ *       MASKED (H3), like any tool result; the tool runs in read-only (Discuss) mode
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-log-tool.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, mkdir, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';

+import path from 'node:path';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';

+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-plog-home-'));

+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');

+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const spine = (level, msg) => `2026-09-16T22:10:00.000Z  ${level}  [plugin] build=1892 pid=4242 proj=proj | ${msg} | status=idle model=m`;

+    ok(!reqs.some((t) => /Okonkwo-Zed/.test(t)), 'and no request to the model carried it');
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness C:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally { try { server && server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(project); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

+// A PLUGIN THAT MASKS ITS OWN RESULT (the developer, 2026-10-14: "if a plugin is already handling all the masking,
+// it can say don't auto-mask my result. Plugins have more info about the data and can be more surgical" - "yes,
+// w/ the approval please"). Through the real server, the shipped filter and a scripted model:
+//   0. THE APPROVAL IS OFF FOR NOW ("let's actually disable the approval right now"): the declaration alone counts,
+//      and a tool without it is masked as before
+//   then with CB_OWN_MASK_APPROVAL=1, the approval as it will be:
+//   1. the tool declares masksOwnResult; until the developer approves it, its result is masked as usual
+//   2. approved: the result reaches the model as the plugin returned it - the redetermination date readable
+//   3. the approval is for that exact file: an edit to the plugin turns it off until approved again
+//   4. withdrawn: masked again
+//   5. a plugin with no such tool cannot be approved; the approval is in prefs
+// Run: node test/test-plugin-own-mask.mjs
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, mkdir, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';

+import path from 'node:path';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-ownmask-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PORT = 29498;
+const DATE = '482-11-9037';   // a value the masker hides (it was a date, and dates have been shown since 2026-10-22)

+  description: 'a record from the eligibility tables, masked by the plugin itself',

+  tools: [

+  ],
+};
+${extra}
+`;
+const plain = `export default { description: 'no own masking', tools: [{ verb: 'plain_row', name: 'plain_row', schema: { parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } }, impl: async () => JSON.stringify({ ENTL_RDTR_DT: '${DATE}' }) }] };\n`;

+// each message: call the tool, then finish
+const adapter = {

+  // (the background agents' calls - the bookkeeper, recall, the skills maintainer - neither counted nor answered with the
+  // agent's turns: between two messages one took a turn, and the next run got the other reply - the flake in 2712)
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' } }] }; },
+};

+const BASE = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+const post = async (p, body) => { const r = await fetch(BASE + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }); return { status: r.status, json: await r.json().catch(() => null) }; };
+const row = async (name) => ((await (await fetch(BASE + '/api/state?limit=1')).json()).plugins || []).find((p) => p.name === name);
+// one message: the tool's result is in the request that follows the snippet
+const ask = async () => {
+  const n = requests.length;
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'get the row' });
+  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) { await sleep(150); const s = await (await fetch(BASE + '/api/state?limit=1')).json(); if (requests.length >= n + 2 && (s.status === 'idle' || s.status === 'done')) break; }
+  const r = requests[n + 1] || '';
+  const turns = [...r.matchAll(/--- developer [0-9a-z]{4} ---\n([\s\S]*?)\n\/--- developer [0-9a-z]{4} ---/g)];
+  return turns.length ? turns[turns.length - 1][1] : '';
+};
+try {
+  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !(await row('elig'))?.enabled; i++) await sleep(250);
+  delete process.env.CB_OWN_MASK_APPROVAL;

+  ok(z && z.ownMaskNeedsApproval === false && z.ownMaskApproved === true, '0: with the approval off, the declaration alone counts');
+  const a0 = await ask();
+  ok(a0.includes(DATE), `0: so the tool's result reaches the model as the plugin returned it (${a0.slice(0, 90).replace(/\s+/g, ' ')})`);
+  SNIPPET = '<remote-workspace><js>return await plain_row({});</js></remote-workspace>';
+  const p0 = await ask();
+  ok(/ENTL_RDTR_DT/.test(p0) && !p0.includes(DATE), '0: a tool that does not declare it is masked as before');
+  SNIPPET = '<remote-workspace><js>return await elig_row({});</js></remote-workspace>';
+  process.env.CB_OWN_MASK_APPROVAL = '1';
+  const r0 = await row('elig');
+  ok(r0 && r0.enabled && JSON.stringify(r0.masksOwnResult) === '["elig_row"]' && r0.ownMaskApproved === false, '1: the plugin loads, its tool says it masks its own result, and it is not approved');
+  const a1 = await ask();
+  ok(/ENTL_RDTR_DT/.test(a1) && !a1.includes(DATE), `1: not approved - the value is masked as usual, the column name shown (exempt) (${a1.slice(0, 90).replace(/\s+/g, ' ')})`);

+  const a2 = await ask();
+  ok(a2.includes(DATE) && a2.includes('"ENTL_RDTR_CD":"N"'), `2: approved - the result reaches the model as the plugin returned it (${a2.slice(0, 90).replace(/\s+/g, ' ')})`);
+  const prefs = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'prefs.json'), 'utf8'));
+  ok(prefs.pluginOwnMask && typeof prefs.pluginOwnMask.elig === 'string' && prefs.pluginOwnMask.elig.length >= 16, '2: the approval is kept in prefs, as the hash of the file approved');
+
+  await writeFile(PFILE, pluginSrc('// edited'));

+  ok((await row('elig')).ownMaskApproved === false, '3: the plugin file changed - the approval no longer applies');
+  const a3 = await ask();
+  ok(!a3.includes(DATE), '3: and its result is masked again');

+  await post('/api/plugin-own-mask', { name: 'elig', approve: true });

+  await post('/api/plugin-own-mask', { name: 'elig', approve: false });
+  ok((await row('elig')).ownMaskApproved === false, '4: and withdrawn');
+  const a4 = await ask();
+  ok(!a4.includes(DATE), '4: withdrawn - masked as usual');
+
+  const bad = await post('/api/plugin-own-mask', { name: 'plainrow', approve: true });
+  ok(bad.status === 400, `5: a plugin with no tool that masks its own result cannot be approved (${bad.status})`);
+  const none = await post('/api/plugin-own-mask', { name: 'nosuch', approve: true });
+  ok(none.status === 404, '5: nor can one that is not loaded');
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { await new Promise((r) => { try { server.closeAllConnections?.(); } catch {} server.close(() => r()); }); await cleanupDir(proj); }

+/**
+ * The panel DSL v2 (PLUGIN-UI-PANEL-SPEC.md §4a) through the REAL UI: a fixture plugin renders every component in
+ * its side-panel tab and in a blocking ask-panel dialog.
+ *   [A] every component renders with its class, drawn by the core (no plugin markup); a link with a non-http href
+ *       is text; an unknown type is a visible warning
+ *   [B] ambient events: a checkbox click → change with a BOOLEAN; a select → change with its value; a textarea →
+ *       one debounced change; a table row → click with the row id; `values` on every event carries the booleans
+ *   [C] blocking dialog: answers come back typed (boolean / string); a required select gates submit until chosen;
+ *       a live repaint keeps the checkbox, textarea and select the developer set
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-panel-components-e2e.mjs
+ */

+};
+`;
+function reply(t) {
+  let top = -1, which = '';
+  for (const m of ['EVENTS', 'ASK', 'LOADING']) { const p = t.lastIndexOf('STEP_' + m + ' '); if (p > top) { top = p; which = m; } }
+  if (top < 0) return 'ready';
+  const fn = which === 'EVENTS' ? 'dsl_events' : which === 'ASK' ? 'dsl_ask' : 'dsl_loading';
+  const call = `<js>return await ${fn}({});</js>`;
+  return t.lastIndexOf('<remote-workspace-result') > top ? `done. ${which}_DONE` : `Calling. <remote-workspace>${call}</remote-workspace>`;
+}
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) { const text = (req && req.messages ? req.messages : []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content))).join('\n'); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: reply(text) } }] }; },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+

+/**
+ * The test-data panel through the real UI: ONE OUTLINE (the developer, 2026-10-10) - a text area with a gutter of
+ * icons. A family is a line at the left; Tab moves a line in to a person and once more to a "field : value" line;
+ * Enter adds a line at the same level; → at the end of a box goes to the start of the next; Backspace on an empty
+ * line removes it; every box is an underline. Everything saves as it is typed, into the store outside the container
+ * (here: a folder under the test's own CB_HOME). Also: a rename, a line with a problem, the empty panel, the live view.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-panel-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, mkdir, readFile, copyFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+// its own home BEFORE the server module loads (the server fixes its home folder when it loads)
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-panel-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+// the test data is in the store OUTSIDE the container (vault.mjs) - for a test, a folder under its own CB_HOME
+const tdFam = () => path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'secure', 'plugins', 'pii-testdata', 'families');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29161;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const readFam = async (n) => { try { return JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(tdFam(), n + '.json'), 'utf8')); } catch { return null; } };
+const until = async (fn, ms = 6000) => { const t = Date.now() + ms; let v; while (Date.now() < t) { v = await fn(); if (v) return v; await sleep(150); } return v; };

=== add test/test-plugin-panel.mjs
new 79ddcebc3cc5baaffea7c5f8
+/**
+ * Plugin UI panel primitive (PLUGIN-UI-PANEL-SPEC.md §3-§5): a plugin declares panel:{title,render,onEvent};
+ * the manager validates it, exposes enabledPanels() + panel meta in the snapshot, and invokes render/onEvent
+ * time-boxed + error-caught. No server, no UI — the manager contract only. Run: node test/test-plugin-panel.mjs
+ */
+import { createPluginManager } from '../src/plugins/index.mjs';
+import { validatePluginShape } from '../src/plugins/runtime-loader.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+// A fixture plugin whose panel render returns a tiny tree and whose onEvent echoes the event into a note. It also
+// keeps a module-level counter so we can prove onEvent actually ran server-side.
+const PLUGIN_SRC = `
+let saves = 0;
+export default {
+  description: 'panel fixture',
+  panel: {
+    title: 'Fixture', icon: '🧪',
+    render: async (ctx) => ({ type: 'stack', children: [
+      { type: 'note', text: 'project=' + (ctx.projectDir || 'none') },
+      { type: 'text', id: 'greeting', label: 'Greeting', value: 'hi' },
+      { type: 'button', id: 'go', label: 'Go' },
+    ] }),
+    onEvent: async (ev, ctx) => {
+      if (ev.event === 'change') saves++;
+      return { toast: { text: ev.event + ':' + ev.id + '=' + ev.value + ' (saves=' + saves + ')', tone: 'ok' } };
+    },
+  },
+  tools: [{ verb: 'fx', name: 'fx', schema: { parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } }, impl: async () => 'ok' }],
+};`;
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[A] validatePluginShape accepts a good panel and rejects malformed ones');
+  ok(validatePluginShape({ panel: { title: 'X', render: () => ({}) } }, 'p').ok, 'A1: {title, render} is valid');
+  ok(validatePluginShape({ panel: { title: 'X', render: () => ({}), onEvent: () => ({}), icon: '🧪' } }, 'p').ok, 'A2: + onEvent + icon valid');
+  ok(!validatePluginShape({ panel: { render: () => ({}) } }, 'p').ok, 'A3: missing title rejected');
+  ok(!validatePluginShape({ panel: { title: 'X' } }, 'p').ok, 'A4: missing render rejected');
+  ok(!validatePluginShape({ panel: { title: 'X', render: () => ({}), onEvent: 42 } }, 'p').ok, 'A5: non-fn onEvent rejected');
+  ok(!validatePluginShape({ panel: [] }, 'p').ok, 'A6: array panel rejected');
+
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-panel-'));
+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {} });
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(dir);
+  try {
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'fixture__1.mjs'), PLUGIN_SRC);
+    await mgr.reconcile();
+
+    console.log('\n[B] the plugin loads and its panel is advertised');
+    const snap = mgr.snapshot().find((r) => r.name === 'fixture');
+    ok(snap && snap.enabled, 'B1: fixture enabled');
+    ok(snap && snap.panel && snap.panel.title === 'Fixture' && snap.panel.icon === '🧪', `B2: snapshot carries panel meta (${JSON.stringify(snap && snap.panel)})`);
+    const panels = mgr.enabledPanels();
+    ok(panels.length === 1 && panels[0].name === 'fixture' && panels[0].title === 'Fixture', 'B3: enabledPanels() lists it');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] renderPanel returns the tree (ctx.projectDir flows through)');
+    const r = await mgr.renderPanel('fixture', { projectDir: '/proj/x' });
+    ok(r.title === 'Fixture' && r.tree && r.tree.type === 'stack', 'C1: render returns { title, tree }');
+    ok(r.tree.children.some((c) => c.type === 'note' && /project=\/proj\/x/.test(c.text)), 'C2: ctx.projectDir reached render');
+    ok(r.tree.children.some((c) => c.type === 'text' && c.id === 'greeting'), 'C3: the bound text field is present');
+
+    console.log('\n[D] panelEvent runs onEvent server-side and returns its result');

+/**
+ * Bug review #12: during a plugin reconcile, a TRANSIENT fs error (stat/realpath/readFile throwing on an
+ * ENOENT/EBUSY blip while the file still exists) must NOT unload an already-loaded, healthy plugin — the catch now
+ * keeps it in `desired` when it's in `runtime`. This is verified STATICALLY: the error is a Windows share/lock blip
+ * that can't be induced in-process (Node's readFile succeeds even under a FileShare.None exclusive lock — verified),
+ * and createPluginManager takes no fs seam, so a behavioral repro would need a module-level fs mock. The static
+ * check still guards against a revert. Plus a behavioral idempotency check (repeat reconcile ≠ churn).
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-reconcile-keep.mjs
+ */
+import { createPluginManager } from '../src/plugins/index.mjs';
+import { readFile, mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+
+console.log('\n[1] STATIC: the transient stat + readFile catches keep an already-loaded plugin (desired.add on runtime.has)');
+{
+  const src = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'plugins', 'index.mjs'), 'utf8');
+  const guards = (src.match(/if \(runtime\.has\(name\)\) desired\.add\(name\)/g) || []).length;
+  ok(guards >= 2, `both transient catches (stat/realpath + readFile) preserve a loaded plugin — found ${guards} guards`);
+  ok(/transient ENOENT\/EBUSY[^\n]*keep an ALREADY-LOADED plugin/.test(src), 'the stat catch documents keeping a loaded plugin on an FS blip');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] BEHAVIORAL: a healthy plugin survives repeated reconciles (no spurious unload/churn)');
+{
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-recon-'));
+  const pdir = path.join(dir, 'plugins');
+  await import('node:fs/promises').then((m) => m.mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true }));
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'keepme__1.mjs'), 'export default { tools: [{ verb: "keepme", name: "keepme_x", schema: { parameters: { type: "object", properties: {} } }, impl: async () => "ok" }] };');
+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, onHostRequest: async () => ({}) });
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(pdir);
+  await mgr.reconcile();
+  const loaded1 = mgr.snapshot().some((r) => r.name === 'keepme' && !r.error);
+  await mgr.reconcile(); await mgr.reconcile();   // unchanged bytes → must stay loaded, no churn
+  const loaded2 = mgr.snapshot().some((r) => r.name === 'keepme' && !r.error);
+  ok(loaded1 && loaded2, 'the plugin stays loaded across repeated reconciles (idempotent, not unloaded)');
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(null); await mgr.reconcile();
+  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}

+/**
+ * A PLUGIN JOINS THE PROJECT WALK (host.scan) — through the real server, alongside the git scan:
+ *   [A] a probe registered in init() finds its folders after the first scan — INSIDE a repo and outside — while the
+ *       git scan still lists the repo; node_modules is never entered
+ *   [B] a second scan with nothing changed does NOT call onChange; a new folder + host.scan.rescan() does, once
+ *   [C] a hot-reload of the plugin re-registers the probe from init, without duplicating hits
+ *   [D] removing the plugin file drops its probe (the next scan has no probe to call)
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-scan-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, mkdir, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-scan-home-'));
+process.env.CB_GIT_SCAN_MS = '1500';   // a fast tick so "a scan with nothing changed" happens within the test
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+
+const PORT = 29242;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const PLUGIN = (tag) => `export default {
+  description: 'scan fixture ${tag}',
+  init(host) {
+    host.state.changes = host.state.changes || 0;
+    host.state.fileChanges = host.state.fileChanges || 0;
+    host.scan.register({ name: 'wl', visit: (dir, entries) => (dir.endsWith('wl-domain') ? 'hit+prune' : 'continue'),
+      onChange: (hits) => { host.state.changes++; host.state.hits = hits.map((h) => h.path); } });
+    // A FILE-oriented probe on the same plugin: *.ds.json anywhere; hits carry the files.
+    host.scan.register({ name: 'ds', visit: (dir, entries) => { const f = entries.filter((e) => e.isFile() && /\\.ds\\.json$/.test(e.name)).map((e) => e.name); return f.length ? { result: 'hit', files: f } : 'continue'; },
+      onChange: (hits) => { host.state.fileChanges++; host.state.files = hits.flatMap((h) => h.files); } });
+  },
+  tools: [
+    { verb: 'scan_report', name: 'scan_report', schema: { description: 'x', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } },
+      impl: async (args, ctx) => ({ content: JSON.stringify({ changes: ctx.host.state.changes || 0, hits: ctx.host.state.hits || [], last: ctx.host.scan.hits('wl').map((h) => h.path), fileChanges: ctx.host.state.fileChanges || 0, files: ctx.host.state.files || [] }) }) },
+    { verb: 'scan_now', name: 'scan_now', schema: { description: 'x', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } },
+      impl: async (args, ctx) => { ctx.host.scan.rescan(); return { content: 'asked' }; } },
+  ],
+};
+`;
+
+// Flat transport: the LAST marker decides; a tool call until its result appears after it, then DONE.
+function reply(t) {
+  let top = -1, which = '';
+  for (const m of ['REPORT', 'NOW', 'BAD']) { const p = t.lastIndexOf('STEP_' + m + ' '); if (p > top) { top = p; which = m; } }
+  if (top < 0) return 'ready';
+  const fn = which === 'REPORT' ? 'scan_report' : which === 'NOW' ? 'scan_now' : 'bad_report';
+  const call = `<js>return await ${fn}({});</js>`;
+  return t.lastIndexOf('<remote-workspace-result') > top ? `done. ${which}_DONE` : `Calling. <remote-workspace>${call}</remote-workspace>`;
+}
+// A probe whose visit THROWS (the WebLogic plugin's report, 2026-09-16: `nodePath` used without an import): the plugin
+// must learn WHY it has no hits, not sit on "0 found". `fixed` swaps in a visit that works, so the same file proves the
+// recovery: warnings clear, hits arrive.
+const BAD_PLUGIN = (fixed) => `${fixed ? "import nodePath from 'node:path';" : ''}
+export default {
+  description: 'scan fixture with a ${fixed ? 'working' : 'throwing'} visit',
+  init(host) {
+    host.state.calls = host.state.calls || [];
+    host.scan.register({ name: 'ds', visit: (dir) => (nodePath.basename(dir) === 'data-sources' ? 'hit' : 'continue'),
+      onChange: (hits, info) => { host.state.calls.push({ n: hits.length, error: (info && info.error) || null }); } });
+    host.scan.rescan();
+  },
+  tools: [{ verb: 'bad_report', name: 'bad_report', schema: { description: 'x', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } },
+    impl: async (args, ctx) => ({ content: JSON.stringify({ calls: ctx.host.state.calls, status: ctx.host.scan.status('ds') }) }) }],
+};
+`;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) { const text = (req && req.messages ? req.messages : []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content))).join('\n'); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: reply(text) } }] }; },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  const pdir = userPluginsDir();
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  const pluginFile = path.join(pdir, 'scanfix__1.mjs');
+  await writeFile(pluginFile, PLUGIN('v1'), 'utf8');
+  const project = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-scan-proj-'));
+  const mk = async (...segs) => { const p = path.join(project, ...segs); await mkdir(p, { recursive: true }); return p; };
+  const repoA = await mk('repoA'); spawnSync('git', ['init', '-q', repoA], { windowsHide: true });
+  await mk('repoA', 'domains', 'wl-domain'); await mk('tools', 'wl-domain'); await mk('node_modules', 'x', 'wl-domain');
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'README.md'), 'x');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<!doctype html><title>m</title>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: project });
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const j = (p) => fetch(B + p).then((r) => r.json());
+  const post = (p, b) => fetch(B + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(b) }).then((r) => r.json());
+  const send = async (marker) => { await post('/api/message', { text: 'STEP_' + marker + ' go', model: 'm' }); for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) { await sleep(150); const s = await j('/api/state'); const evs = s.events || []; const done = evs.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && String(e.content || '').includes(marker + '_DONE')); if (done && s.status !== 'running') return s; } throw new Error('timeout waiting for ' + marker); };
+  const report = async () => { const s = await send('REPORT'); const r = (s.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval').pop(); return JSON.parse(String(r && r.content || '{}').replace(/\n\nWhat the snippet did, in order:\n[\s\S]*$/, '')); };
+  const rel = (p) => path.relative(project, p).split(path.sep).join('/');
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[A] the probe finds its folders — inside the repo and outside — and git still lists the repo');
+    await sleep(2500);   // the initial scan (1.5s after boot) + the register-triggered one
+    const r1 = await report();
+    const hits1 = (r1.hits || []).map(rel).sort();
+    ok(r1.changes >= 1, `onChange ran after the first scan (${r1.changes} time(s))`);
+    ok(hits1.join(',') === 'repoA/domains/wl-domain,tools/wl-domain', `both folders found, the one INSIDE the repo included, node_modules skipped (${hits1.join(',')})`);
+    ok((r1.last || []).length === 2, 'host.scan.hits() reads the same result');
+    const st = await j('/api/state');
+    ok(JSON.stringify(st.gitProjects || st.session?.gitProjects || []).includes('repoA'), 'the git scan still lists repoA from the same walk');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] nothing changed → no onChange; a new folder + rescan → exactly one');
+    const before = r1.changes;
+    await sleep(3500);   // two ticks of the fast interval
+    const r2 = await report();
+    ok(r2.changes === before, `two scans with nothing changed did not call onChange (${before} → ${r2.changes})`);
+    await mk('more', 'wl-domain');
+    await send('NOW');   // host.scan.rescan() → a scan soon
+    await sleep(1500);
+    const r3 = await report();
+    ok(r3.changes === before + 1 && (r3.hits || []).map(rel).sort().join(',') === 'more/wl-domain,repoA/domains/wl-domain,tools/wl-domain', `one onChange with the new folder (${r3.changes}, ${(r3.hits || []).map(rel).sort().join(',')})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[B2] a FILE probe: a file dropped into an already-hit directory is a change — onChange runs once, with the files');
+    // The FIRST scan reports to every probe once, even an empty set — the "scanned, nothing found" signal.
+    ok(r3.fileChanges === 1 && (r3.files || []).length === 0, `no *.ds.json yet → the file probe got its one first-scan report, empty (${r3.fileChanges})`);
+    await writeFile(path.join(project, 'tools', 'one.ds.json'), '{}');
+    await send('NOW'); await sleep(1500);
+    const f1 = await report();
+    ok(f1.fileChanges === 2 && (f1.files || []).map(rel).join(',') === 'tools/one.ds.json', `the first file → one onChange with its ABSOLUTE path (${f1.fileChanges}, ${(f1.files || []).map(rel).join(',')})`);
+    await writeFile(path.join(project, 'tools', 'two.ds.json'), '{}');   // the directory set is unchanged — the FILE set is not
+    await send('NOW'); await sleep(1500);
+    const f2 = await report();
+    ok(f2.fileChanges === 3 && (f2.files || []).map(rel).sort().join(',') === 'tools/one.ds.json,tools/two.ds.json', `a second file in the SAME directory → onChange again, both files (${f2.fileChanges}, ${(f2.files || []).map(rel).sort().join(',')})`);
+    ok(f2.changes === r3.changes, 'the folder probe on the same plugin was not called for it (its set did not change)');
+    await sleep(3500);   // two idle ticks
+    ok((await report()).fileChanges === 3, 'and nothing changed → no further onChange');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] a hot-reload re-registers the probe from init, without duplicate hits');
+    await writeFile(pluginFile, PLUGIN('v2'), 'utf8');
+    await sleep(6500);   // the 5s plugin poll + a scan
+    const r4 = await report();
+    ok((r4.hits || []).length === 3 && (r4.last || []).length === 3, `after the reload the probe still reports three folders, not six (${(r4.hits || []).length}/${(r4.last || []).length})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[D] removing the plugin drops its probe');
+    await rm(pluginFile, { force: true });
+    await sleep(6500);
+    const snap = (await j('/api/state')).plugins || [];
+    ok(!snap.some((p) => p.name === 'scanfix'), 'the plugin is gone from the snapshot (its probe went with it — the next scan has nothing of its to call)');
+
+    console.log('\n[E] a probe whose visit THROWS is told so — not handed "no hits": onChange gets the error, status() carries it, the plugins panel shows it');
+    await mk('data-sources');
+    const badFile = path.join(pdir, 'scanbad__1.mjs');
+    await writeFile(badFile, BAD_PLUGIN(false), 'utf8');
+    await sleep(6500);   // the 5s plugin poll + the register-triggered scan
+    const b1 = await (async () => { const s = await send('BAD'); const r = (s.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval').pop(); return JSON.parse(String(r && r.content || '{}').replace(/\n\nWhat the snippet did, in order:\n[\s\S]*$/, '')); })();
+    const errCall = (b1.calls || []).find((c) => c.error);
+    ok(!!errCall && /threw at .*: nodePath is not defined/.test(errCall.error) && errCall.n === 0, `onChange ran ONCE with the visit error as its second argument and the (empty) last-good hits (${JSON.stringify(b1.calls)})`);
+    ok((b1.calls || []).filter((c) => c.error).length === 1, 'the same error is not repeated on every scan');
+    ok(b1.status && b1.status.scanned === true && /not defined/.test(b1.status.error || '') && b1.status.hits.length === 0, `host.scan.status() says: scanned, error, no hits (${JSON.stringify(b1.status)})`);
+    const rowBad = ((await j('/api/state')).plugins || []).find((p) => p.name === 'scanbad');
+    ok(!!rowBad && rowBad.enabled && Array.isArray(rowBad.warnings) && rowBad.warnings.length === 1 && /threw at/.test(rowBad.warnings[0]), `the plugin's snapshot row is LOADED with a warning the panel shows (${JSON.stringify(rowBad && rowBad.warnings)})`);
+    // Fix the plugin: the warning clears and the folder is found.
+    await writeFile(badFile, BAD_PLUGIN(true), 'utf8');
+    await sleep(6500);
+    const b2 = await (async () => { const s = await send('BAD'); const r = (s.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval').pop(); return JSON.parse(String(r && r.content || '{}').replace(/\n\nWhat the snippet did, in order:\n[\s\S]*$/, '')); })();
+    ok(b2.status && b2.status.error === null && b2.status.hits.length === 1 && /data-sources$/.test(b2.status.hits[0].path), `after the fix the probe reports its folder and no error (${JSON.stringify(b2.status)})`);
+    ok((b2.calls || []).some((c) => c.n === 1 && !c.error), 'and onChange delivered the hit with error:null');
+    const rowOk = ((await j('/api/state')).plugins || []).find((p) => p.name === 'scanbad');
+    ok(!!rowOk && Array.isArray(rowOk.warnings) && rowOk.warnings.length === 0, 'the warning is gone from the snapshot row');
+    await rm(badFile, { force: true });
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(project); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

=== add test/test-plugin-spawn-wake-e2e.mjs
new 2cdc2643e1641962c46c42f8
+/**
+ * A plugin starts a SLOW SERVER and the agent is WOKEN — it never polls. host.spawn(file, argv, { readyWhen,
+ * wakeAfterMs, wakeIntervalMs }) now forwards the wake contract (the spawn-process host request dropped it before):
+ *   [A] the tool returns at once with the job id and the run ends; /api/bg shows the contract ARMED on the process
+ *   [B] at wakeAfterMs the idle agent gets a fresh turn carrying a periodic check-in (status="running")
+ *   [C] the READY line wakes it again with status="ready" (the "CONTINUE, do NOT start it again" message), readyAt set
+ *   [D] no page errors
+ * Drives the real UI with a scripted model. Run: node test/test-plugin-spawn-wake-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, mkdir, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-wlwake-home-'));   // isolate the GLOBAL plugin folder before the server loads
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+const { reapAllBackground } = await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29235;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// The fixture plugin: wl_start spawns a "WebLogic" that announces RUNNING after 5.5s and then runs forever, with the
+// wake contract: first check-in at 1.5s (a real plugin would say 5 min), then every minute, and the READY line.
+const PLUGIN = `const SCRIPT = "setTimeout(()=>console.log('<Notice> <WebLogicServer> <Server started in RUNNING mode>'),5500);setInterval(()=>{},1000)";
+export default {
+  description: 'weblogic-ish fixture',
+  tools: [{
+    verb: 'wl_start', name: 'wl_start',
+    schema: { description: 'start the server in the background', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } },
+    impl: async (args, ctx) => {
+      const { id } = await ctx.host.spawn(process.execPath, ['-e', SCRIPT], { label: 'weblogic', readyWhen: 'RUNNING mode', wakeAfterMs: 1500, wakeIntervalMs: 60000 });
+      return { content: 'started job ' + id + ' — end your turn; you will be woken when it reports RUNNING.' };
+    },
+  }],
+};
+`;
+
+// Scripted model: turn 1 calls the tool, turn 2 (after the tool result) ends the run; every later request is a wake.
+const reqs = [];
+let call = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    reqs.push((req && req.messages ? req.messages : []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content))).join('\n'));
+    call++;
+    const t = call === 1 ? 'Starting WebLogic. <remote-workspace><js>return await wl_start({});</js></remote-workspace>'
+      : call === 2 ? 'Started; I will wait to be woken. WL_TURN_DONE'
+      : `Acknowledged wake ${call - 2}. WL_ACK`;
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const pdir = userPluginsDir();
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'wlish__1.mjs'), PLUGIN, 'utf8');
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-wlwake-proj-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), 'hello\n');
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+
+    console.log('\n[A] the tool returns at once with the job id; the run ends; the wake contract is ARMED on the process');
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    await page.type('#input', 'start weblogic');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('WL_TURN_DONE'), { timeout: 20000 });
+    const tookMs = Date.now() - t0;
+    ok(tookMs < 5000, `the start tool returned immediately and the run ended (${tookMs}ms) — the agent did not sit in the tool`);
+    // The result row paints lazily (its text is not in the DOM until opened) — what matters is that the MODEL saw the id.
+    ok(reqs.length >= 2 && /started job bg-\d+ /.test(reqs[1]), 'the tool result, with the background job id, reached the model on its next turn');
+    const bg = await page.evaluate(async () => (await (await fetch('/api/bg')).json()).processes);
+    const p = (bg || []).find((x) => /weblogic/.test(x.label || ''));
+    ok(!!p && p.alive, `the process is a first-class background job (label ${p && p.label})`);
+    ok(!!p && p.readyWhen === 'RUNNING mode' && p.wakeAfterMs === 1500 && p.wakeIntervalMs === 60000, `readyWhen + wakeAfterMs + wakeIntervalMs reached the registry (${JSON.stringify(p && { readyWhen: p.readyWhen, wakeAfterMs: p.wakeAfterMs, wakeIntervalMs: p.wakeIntervalMs })})`);
+    ok(!!p && !p.readyAt, 'not ready yet');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] at wakeAfterMs the idle agent is woken with a fresh turn carrying a periodic check-in');
+    const callsAfterStart = call;
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('WL_ACK'), { timeout: 8000 }).catch(() => {});
+    const checkin = reqs.slice(callsAfterStart).find((r) => /status="running"/.test(r));
+    ok(!!checkin && /periodic check-in/.test(checkin) && /weblogic/.test(checkin), `a check-in wake reached the model as a fresh turn (${checkin ? 'status="running", periodic check-in' : 'no wake seen; calls=' + call})`);
+    ok(!reqs.slice(callsAfterStart).some((r) => /status="ready"/.test(r)), 'no READY yet — the check-in came first, as scheduled');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] the RUNNING line wakes the agent again with status="ready"');
+    const callsAfterCheckin = call;
+    await page.waitForFunction((n) => (document.body.innerText.match(/WL_ACK/g) || []).length >= n, { timeout: 12000 }, 2).catch(() => {});
+    const ready = reqs.slice(callsAfterCheckin).find((r) => /status="ready"/.test(r));
+    ok(!!ready && /READY\. CONTINUE/.test(ready) && /RUNNING mode/.test(ready), `the READY wake reached the model with the continue-don't-restart message (${ready ? 'yes' : 'no wake seen; calls=' + call})`);
+    const bg2 = await page.evaluate(async () => (await (await fetch('/api/bg')).json()).processes);
+    const p2 = (bg2 || []).find((x) => /weblogic/.test(x.label || ''));
+    ok(!!p2 && p2.alive && !!p2.readyAt, `the process is still running and readyAt is recorded (${p2 && p2.readyAt})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[D] no page errors');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { try { reapAllBackground(); } catch {} await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
