=== add test/test-plugin-sql-testdata-e2e.mjs
new dfc0343c9207dfd0f24f78f5
+/**
+ * A SQL-STYLE PLUGIN LOADS ITS RESULT ROWS AS TEST DATA — through pii-testdata's export, not by writing files —
+ * and the rows reach the agent ONLY as tokens, which the agent can hand to any tool.
+ *   [A] the fixture "sql" plugin (dependencies: pii-testdata) calls host.use('pii-testdata').loadFamily(...) inside its
+ *       tool and returns the raw rows; H3 masks that same result against the store the call just wrote: the model's
+ *       next request carries #@q1.<row>.<column>#<id> tokens and never the values; the row is also tokenized in the UI event
+ *   [B] the agent hands a token to write() inside a snippet → the FILE holds the real value (H2), the transcript still holds the token
+ *   [C] a value the panel would refuse (a common word) was dropped by loadFamily, and reported
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-sql-testdata-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, mkdir, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-sqltd-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+
+const PORT = 29240;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const ROWS = { r1: { last_name: 'Okonkwo-Zed', ssn: '482-11-9037', status: 'first' }, r2: { last_name: 'Vantongeren', ssn: '333-22-1111', status: 'first' } };
+
+const PLUGIN = `export default {
+  description: 'sql fixture: query → test-data family',
+  dependencies: { 'pii-testdata': true },
+  tools: [{ verb: 'sql_query', name: 'sql_query', schema: { description: 'run a query', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { sql: { type: 'string' } } } },
+    impl: async (args, ctx) => {
+      const rows = ${JSON.stringify(ROWS)};
+      const pii = ctx.host.use('pii-testdata');
+      const loaded = await pii.loadFamily({ projectDir: ctx.projectDir, label: 'q1', description: args.sql || 'query', members: rows });
+      ctx.host.state.lastLoad = loaded;
+      const text = Object.entries(rows).map(([k, r]) => k + ': ' + Object.entries(r).map(([c, v]) => c + '=' + v).join(', ')).join('\\n');
+      return { content: 'ROWS\\n' + text + '\\nDROPPED ' + loaded.dropped.length };
+    } },
+  { verb: 'sql_last', name: 'sql_last', schema: { description: 'what the last load reported', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } },
+    impl: async (args, ctx) => ({ content: JSON.stringify(ctx.host.state.lastLoad || null) }) }],
+};
+`;
+
+// Flat transport: route on the LAST marker in the whole conversation.
+const reqs = [];
+function reply(t) {
+  const pos = (s) => t.lastIndexOf(s);
+  // After STEP2 the first request holds no tool result yet; the next one holds the write's result block → done.
+  if (pos('STEP2') >= 0) { if (pos('<remote-workspace-result') > pos('STEP2')) return 'Written. STEP2_DONE'; const m = /#@q1\.r1\.last_name(?:~[^#\s]+)?#[0-9a-z]{7}/.exec(t); return m ? `Writing the name. <remote-workspace><js>const r = await write("out.txt", "${m[0]}"); return r.summary;</js></remote-workspace>` : 'No token found. STEP2_DONE'; }
+  if (pos('STEP1') >= 0) return pos('ROWS') > pos('STEP1') ? 'Got the rows. STEP1_DONE' : 'Querying. <remote-workspace><js>return await sql_query({ sql: "SELECT * FROM enrollees" });</js></remote-workspace>';
+  return 'ready';
+}
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const text = (req && req.messages ? req.messages : []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content))).join('\n');
+    reqs.push(text);
+    let r = reply(text);
+    // ("wrote it" only in the conversation after the step: code_boss's own prompt has come to hold those words, and
+    // this answered "Written. STEP2_DONE" to STEP1)
+    const sinceStep = text.slice(Math.max(text.lastIndexOf('STEP1'), text.lastIndexOf('STEP2')));
+    if (/wrote it/.test(sinceStep) && !/STEP2_DONE/.test(r)) r = 'Written. STEP2_DONE';
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: r } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  const pdir = userPluginsDir();
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'sqlfix__1.mjs'), PLUGIN, 'utf8');
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-sqltd-proj-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), 'hello\n');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<!doctype html><title>m</title>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const j = (p) => fetch(B + p).then((r) => r.json());
+  const post = (p, b) => fetch(B + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(b) }).then((r) => r.json());
+  const send = async (text, marker) => { await post('/api/message', { text, model: 'm' }); for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) { await sleep(150); const s = await j('/api/state'); if ((s.chatEvents || s.events || []).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && String(e.content || '').includes(marker))) return s; } throw new Error('timeout waiting for ' + marker); };
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[A] the query result reaches the agent as tokens — loaded through the export, inside the tool');
+    const s1 = await send('STEP1 run the query', 'STEP1_DONE');
+    // The plugin tool is called by name inside the snippet: its call is a js-step (fn sql_query), and the snippet
+    // RETURNS the tool's content, so the js_eval tool-result is that content (masked by H3 like any result).
+    const step = (s1.chatEvents || s1.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'js-step' && e.fn === 'sql_query').pop();
+    ok(!!step && step.kind === 'plugin', 'the sql_query call is a plugin js-step inside the snippet');
+    const res = (s1.chatEvents || s1.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval' && (!step || e.callId === step.callId)).pop();
+    const content = String(res && res.content || '');
+    ok(!!res && /ROWS/.test(content), 'the tool result is in the transcript');
+    ok(!/Okonkwo-Zed|Vantongeren|482-11-9037|333-22-1111/.test(content), `no raw value in the transcript's result (${content.replace(/\n/g, ' | ').slice(0, 120)})`);
+    ok(/#@q1\.r1\.last_name(?:~[^#\s]+)?#[0-9a-z]{7}/.test(content) && /#@q1\.r2\.ssn(?:~[^#\s]+)?#[0-9a-z]{7}/.test(content), 'the rows read as #@q1.<row>.<column>#<id> tokens');
+    const after = reqs[reqs.length - 1] || '';
+    ok(/#@q1\.r1\.last_name(?:~[^#\s]+)?#[0-9a-z]{7}/.test(after) && !/Okonkwo-Zed|482-11-9037/.test(after), 'the model saw the tokens and never a value');
+    ok(!/status=first/.test(content) || /first/.test(content), 'a dropped (common-word) value stays as plain text, not a token');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] the agent hands a token to write() → the file holds the real value, the transcript the token');
+    await send('STEP2 write the sponsor last name to out.txt', 'STEP2_DONE');
+    const fileText = await readFile(path.join(projectDir, 'out.txt'), 'utf8').catch(() => null);
+    ok(fileText === 'Okonkwo-Zed', `the file holds the REAL value via H2 (${JSON.stringify(fileText)})`);
+    const s2 = await j('/api/state');
+    // The write is a js-step inside the snippet; the tool-call event is the snippet itself, whose code carries the token.
+    const wstep = (s2.chatEvents || s2.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'js-step' && e.fn === 'write').pop();
+    ok(!!wstep && /out\.txt/.test(String(wstep.label || '')), 'the write is a js-step of the snippet (out.txt)');
+    const wcall = (s2.chatEvents || s2.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'js' && /write\(/.test(String(e.args?.code || ''))).pop();
+    ok(!!wcall && /#@q1\.r1\.last_name(?:~[^#\s]+)?#[0-9a-z]{7}/.test(JSON.stringify(wcall.args || {})) && !/Okonkwo-Zed/.test(JSON.stringify(wcall.args || {})), 'the tool-call event keeps the token, not the value');
+    ok(!wstep || !/Okonkwo-Zed/.test(String(wstep.diff || '')), 'the diff under the write step is masked, not the value');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] loadFamily dropped what the panel would refuse, and said so');
+    const dropped = Number((content.match(/DROPPED (\d+)/) || [])[1] || 0);
+    ok(dropped === 2, `both rows' "first" status values were dropped with a note (${dropped})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-plugin-state-persist.mjs
new e2208f01126e37d401753f3f
+/**
+ * A plugin's host.state is an in-memory object that SURVIVES A HOT-RELOAD. A plugin's module scope is thrown away
+ * every time its file changes (re-imported fresh), so a module-level counter resets; anything written to host.state
+ * is still there because the manager holds that object per plugin name and re-attaches the SAME reference to the new
+ * host. Verifies: (1) module scope resets on reload but host.state persists; (2) init() re-runs against the SAME
+ * bag; (3) the bag is DROPPED when the plugin is uninstalled (file removed), not merely reloaded/disabled.
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-state-persist.mjs
+ */
+import { createPluginManager } from '../src/plugins/index.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+// The fixture: a module-level counter (resets on reload) + a host.state counter (survives), and init() bumps a
+// host.state init-count. The trailing marker is swapped to change the file's bytes and force a real re-import.
+const srcFor = (marker) => `let moduleCount = 0;
+export default {
+  description: 'state-persist fixture',
+  init(host) { host.state.inits = (host.state.inits || 0) + 1; },
+  tools: [{
+    verb: 'st_bump', name: 'st_bump',
+    schema: { description: 'bump counters', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } },
+    impl: async (args, ctx) => {
+      moduleCount++;
+      ctx.host.state.persist = (ctx.host.state.persist || 0) + 1;
+      return { content: JSON.stringify({ moduleCount, persist: ctx.host.state.persist, inits: ctx.host.state.inits }) };
+    },
+  }],
+};
+// ${marker}
+`;
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-state-'));
+try {
+  const pdir = path.join(dir, 'plugins');
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  const file = path.join(pdir, 'statefix__1.mjs');
+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {} });
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(pdir);
+  const call = async () => JSON.parse((await mgr.enabledTools().st_bump.impl({}, {})).content);
+
+  console.log('\n[A] before a reload: module scope and host.state both climb together');
+  await writeFile(file, srcFor('v1'));
+  await mgr.reconcile();
+  ok(mgr.snapshot().some((r) => r.name === 'statefix' && r.enabled), 'the fixture plugin loaded');
+  const a1 = await call();
+  const a2 = await call();
+  ok(a1.moduleCount === 1 && a2.moduleCount === 2, `module counter climbs 1→2 (${a1.moduleCount},${a2.moduleCount})`);
+  ok(a2.persist === 2, `host.state.persist climbs to 2 (${a2.persist})`);
+  ok(a2.inits === 1, `init ran once so far (${a2.inits})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[B] a RELOAD (edit the file) resets module scope but KEEPS host.state');
+  await writeFile(file, srcFor('v2'));   // different bytes → new ?v=<hash> → real re-import
+  await mgr.reconcile();
+  const b = await call();
+  ok(b.moduleCount === 1, `module counter RESET to 1 — a real reload happened (${b.moduleCount})`);
+  ok(b.persist === 3, `host.state.persist SURVIVED the reload: 2 → 3 (${b.persist})`);
+  ok(b.inits === 2, `init re-ran against the SAME state bag: inits 1 → 2 (${b.inits})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[C] a DISABLE/edit keeps the bag; only an UNINSTALL (file removed) drops it');
+  await rm(file, { force: true });
+  await mgr.reconcile();
+  ok(!mgr.snapshot().some((r) => r.name === 'statefix' && r.enabled), 'the plugin unloaded after its file was removed');
+  await writeFile(file, srcFor('v3'));   // re-install a fresh copy under the same name
+  await mgr.reconcile();
+  const c = await call();
+  ok(c.persist === 1 && c.inits === 1, `a reinstalled plugin gets a FRESH bag (persist ${c.persist}, inits ${c.inits})`);
+
+  await mgr.setPluginsDir(null); await mgr.reconcile();
+} finally { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+

+/**
+ * A plugin's statusItem renders as a real button in the status bar, LEFT of the built-in Edit/tokens/⚙, and its
+ * content/style/tooltip/click are all driven by the plugin through /api/plugin-status. Drives the real UI: installs
+ * a counter plugin, confirms the button appears (polled, content "● 0"), sits before #editToggle, updates on click
+ * ("● 1" with the plugin's inline style), and shows fresh hover text. Run: node test/test-plugin-status-item-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+// Isolate CB_HOME (plugins load from <CB_HOME>/.code_boss/plugins) BEFORE server.mjs reads the config dir at load.
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-si-home-'));
+const pluginsDir = path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'plugins');
+await mkdir(pluginsDir, { recursive: true });
+await writeFile(path.join(pluginsDir, 'counter__1.mjs'), `export default {
+  description: 'status counter',
+  init(host) { host.state.n ??= 0; },
+  statusItem: {
+    render: (ctx) => ({ icon: '●', label: String(ctx.host.state.n), count: ctx.host.state.n, error: ctx.host.state.n > 1, busy: ctx.host.state.n === 0, title: 'clicks: ' + ctx.host.state.n }),
+    onClick: (ctx) => { ctx.host.state.n++; return { icon: '●', label: String(ctx.host.state.n), count: ctx.host.state.n, error: ctx.host.state.n > 1, busy: false }; },
+    hover: (ctx) => 'hovered at count ' + ctx.host.state.n,
+  },
+};\n`);
+// STILL PROCESSING: busy + disabled — the button pulses, is dimmed, and a click never reaches the plugin (the UI
+// posts nothing; `clicks` must stay 0). The shape a plugin returns while it walks and processes its files.
+await writeFile(path.join(pluginsDir, 'sbwait__1.mjs'), `export default {
+  description: 'processing',
+  init(host) { host.state.clicks = 0; },
+  statusItem: {
+    render: () => ({ icon: '⌛', label: 'files', busy: true, disabled: true, title: 'processing files…' }),
+    onClick: (ctx) => { ctx.host.state.clicks++; return { icon: '⌛', label: 'clicked ' + ctx.host.state.clicks }; },
+  },
+};\n`);
+// A DELIBERATELY huge-content item — a plugin must never be able to shove the built-in Edit/gear off-screen (a bug
+// found in the status-bar bug hunt: the plugin group must cap + clip, keeping the developer's own controls visible).
+await writeFile(path.join(pluginsDir, 'sbhuge__1.mjs'), `export default { description: 'huge', statusItem: { render: () => ({ content: 'H'.repeat(300), title: 'huge' }) } };\n`);

+await writeFile(path.join(pluginsDir, 'sbemoji__1.mjs'), `export default { description: 'emoji icon', statusItem: { render: () => ({ content: '🗄️ 3', title: 'data sources' }) } };\n`);

+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29188;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; } };

+async function main() {

+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });

+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });

+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.querySelector('#pluginStatusItems .plugin-status-btn[data-plugin="counter"]'), { timeout: 8000 });
+    ok(true, 'the counter button rendered from the /api/plugin-status poll');
+    const layout = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const btn = document.querySelector('.plugin-status-btn[data-plugin="counter"]');
+      const edit = document.getElementById('editToggle');
+      const inRight = !!btn.closest('.sc-right');
+      // DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING (4) → edit comes AFTER the button, i.e. the button is to its LEFT.
+      const beforeEdit = !!(btn.compareDocumentPosition(edit) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING);
+      return { text: btn.textContent, title: btn.title, inRight, beforeEdit };
+    });
+    ok(layout.inRight, 'the button is in the right-hand status group (.sc-right)');
+    ok(layout.beforeEdit, 'the button sits to the LEFT of the built-in Edit toggle');
+    ok(layout.text === '● 0', `initial content came from the plugin (${JSON.stringify(layout.text)})`);
+    ok(layout.title === 'clicks: 0', 'initial tooltip came from the plugin');

+      return { cls: b.classList.contains('ps-busy'), anim, otherAnim: other ? getComputedStyle(other).animationName : 'none' };
+    });
+    ok(pulse.cls && pulse.anim === 'ps-breathe', `busy:true pulses the glyph (${JSON.stringify(pulse)})`);
+    ok(pulse.otherAnim === 'none', 'an item that is not busy does not pulse — each on its own clock');

+      b.style.pointerEvents = '';

+    });
+    ok(clip.topRight && clip.topLeft && clip.bottomRight && clip.inViewport && clip.w >= 10 && clip.h >= 10, `the badge is drawn whole — no corner is clipped by the button or the group (${JSON.stringify(clip)})`);

+    ok(hov.bg === hov.color, 'on hover the silhouette follows the accent colour (currentColor)');

+      const b = document.querySelector('.plugin-status-btn[data-plugin="sbwait"]'); if (!b) return null;
+      return { disabledAttr: b.disabled === true, cls: b.classList.contains('ps-disabled'), busy: b.classList.contains('ps-busy'), anim: getComputedStyle(b.querySelector('.ps-text')).animationName, opacity: getComputedStyle(b).opacity, title: b.title };
+    });

+    ok(!!dis && dis.busy && dis.anim === 'ps-breathe' && dis.title === 'processing files…', 'and still pulses with its tooltip');
+    // Force a DOM click anyway (a user cannot, but a stray script could): no event is posted and the plugin's onClick never runs.
+    const posted = await page.evaluate(async () => {

+      window.fetch = (u, o) => { if (String(u).includes('/api/plugin-status/sbwait/event')) n++; return orig(u, o); };
+      document.querySelector('.plugin-status-btn[data-plugin="sbwait"]').click();
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
+      window.fetch = orig; return n;
+    });
+    ok(posted === 0, 'a forced click posts nothing to the server');

+/**
+ * A plugin can add a button to the status bar via statusItem:{render, onClick?, hover?}. The plugin owns the
+ * content/style/tooltip/click; the core renders it. Verifies at the manager level: renderStatusItems() returns the
+ * normalized content/style/title for every enabled item; a click runs onClick and returns the updated render (and
+ * state kept on host.state persists across calls); hover returns fresh tooltip text; the snapshot carries a
+ * statusItem flag; a throwing render degrades to a ⚠ button; and a malformed statusItem disables the plugin with a
+ * clear reason. Run: node test/test-plugin-status-item.mjs
+ */
+import { createPluginManager } from '../src/plugins/index.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-statusitem-'));
+try {
+  const pdir = path.join(dir, 'plugins');
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+
+  // A well-formed item: a click counter kept on host.state (so it survives reloads), a style once it climbs, a live
+  // tooltip, and a "hidden" flag toggled from state. A second, THROWING item proves the ⚠ degrade.
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'counter__1.mjs'), `export default {
+    description: 'status counter',
+    init(host) { host.state.n ??= 0; },
+    statusItem: {
+      render: (ctx) => ({ icon: '●', label: String(ctx.host.state.n), count: ctx.host.state.n, error: ctx.host.state.n > 1, busy: ctx.host.state.n === 0, disabled: ctx.host.state.n === 0, title: 'clicks: ' + ctx.host.state.n, hidden: ctx.host.state.n < 0 }),
+      onClick: (ctx) => { ctx.host.state.n++; return { icon: '●', label: String(ctx.host.state.n), count: ctx.host.state.n, error: ctx.host.state.n > 1 }; },
+      hover: (ctx) => 'hovered at count ' + ctx.host.state.n,
+    },
+  };\n`);
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'boom__1.mjs'), `export default {
+    description: 'throwing status item',
+    statusItem: { render: () => { throw new Error('kaboom'); } },
+  };\n`);
+  // Malformed: render is not a function → should be rejected at load with a clear error.
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'baditem__1.mjs'), `export default { description: 'bad', statusItem: { render: 42 } };\n`);
+
+  const mgr = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {} });
+  mgr.setPluginsDir(pdir);
+  await mgr.reconcile();
+
+  console.log('\n[A] the snapshot flags which plugins contribute a status item, and rejects a malformed one');
+  const snap = mgr.snapshot();
+  ok(snap.find((r) => r.name === 'counter')?.statusItem === true, 'counter is flagged statusItem:true');
+  ok(snap.find((r) => r.name === 'boom')?.statusItem === true, 'boom (throwing render) still flags statusItem:true (it is a valid shape)');
+  const bad = snap.find((r) => r.name === 'baditem');
+  ok(bad && !bad.enabled && /statusItem\.render/.test(bad.error || ''), `baditem is DISABLED with a clear reason (${bad && bad.error})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[B] renderStatusItems() returns normalized content/style/title for every enabled item');
+  let items = await mgr.renderStatusItems({ projectDir: null });
+  const counter = items.find((i) => i.name === 'counter');
+  ok(counter && counter.icon === '●' && counter.label === '0' && counter.count === 0 && counter.error === false, `counter renders icon/label/count/error (${JSON.stringify(counter && { icon: counter.icon, label: counter.label, count: counter.count, error: counter.error })})`);
+  ok(counter && counter.content === '● 0', `and a legacy content string is derived for older consumers (${counter && counter.content})`);
+  ok(counter && counter.busy === true, 'busy:true comes through the contract (the plugin is still initializing — the bar pulses it)');
+  ok(counter && counter.disabled === true, 'disabled:true comes through too (nothing to open yet — the bar dims it and posts no click)');
+  ok(counter && counter.title === 'clicks: 0', 'counter renders its tooltip');
+  ok(counter && counter.style === undefined, 'a plugin style is not part of the contract any more (nothing normalized through)');
+  const boom = items.find((i) => i.name === 'boom');
+  ok(boom && boom.icon === '⚠' && boom.error === true, 'a throwing render degrades to a ⚠ item in the error colour, not a failed batch');
+
+  console.log('\n[C] a click runs onClick, returns the updated render, and host.state persists');
+  let r = await mgr.statusItemEvent('counter', { event: 'click' }, {});
+  ok(r.icon === '●' && r.label === '1' && r.count === 1 && r.error === false, `click 1 → label/count update (${JSON.stringify({ label: r.label, count: r.count })})`);
+  ok(r.busy === false, 'and busy turned off once the plugin had something (the pulse stops on the plugin\'s own clock)');
+  r = await mgr.statusItemEvent('counter', { event: 'click' }, {});
+  ok(r.label === '2' && r.count === 2 && r.error === true, `click 2 → count climbs and error turns on at >1 (${JSON.stringify({ label: r.label, count: r.count, error: r.error })}) — state kept on host.state`);
+  // A fresh render now reflects the accumulated state + the style that turns on once n>0.
+  items = await mgr.renderStatusItems({ projectDir: null });
+  const c2 = items.find((i) => i.name === 'counter');
+  ok(c2.label === '2' && c2.count === 2 && c2.error === true && c2.style === undefined, `a fresh render reflects the accumulated state; no style comes through (${JSON.stringify({ label: c2.label, count: c2.count, error: c2.error })})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[D] hover returns fresh, live tooltip text');
+  const h = await mgr.statusItemEvent('counter', { event: 'hover' }, {});
+  ok(h.title === 'hovered at count 2', `hover returns live text (${h.title})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[E] an unknown / non-status plugin is a clean error, not a crash');
+  let threw = false; try { await mgr.statusItemEvent('nope', { event: 'click' }, {}); } catch { threw = true; }
+  ok(threw, 'statusItemEvent on an unknown plugin throws (the server maps it to { ok:false })');
+
+  await mgr.setPluginsDir(null); await mgr.reconcile();
+
+  console.log('\n[Z] ONE ORDER: statusItem.order, then the plugin\'s name - whatever order they loaded in');
+  {
+    const zdir = path.join(dir, 'ordered'); await mkdir(zdir, { recursive: true });
+    const zm = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {} }); zm.setPluginsDir(zdir);
+    await writeFile(path.join(zdir, 'counter__1.mjs'), `export default { description: 'c', statusItem: { render: () => ({ icon: 'C' }) } };\n`);
+    await zm.reconcile();   // counter loads FIRST
+    await writeFile(path.join(zdir, 'zeta__1.mjs'), `export default { description: 'z', statusItem: { order: 1, render: () => ({ icon: 'Z' }) } };\n`);
+    await writeFile(path.join(zdir, 'alpha__1.mjs'), `export default { description: 'a', statusItem: { render: () => ({ icon: 'A' }) } };\n`);
+    await zm.reconcile();
+    const names = (await zm.renderStatusItems({ projectDir: null })).map((i) => i.name);
+    ok(names[0] === 'zeta', `the one that asked to be first is first, although it loaded last (${names.join(', ')})`);
+    const rest = names.slice(1);
+    ok(rest.join() === [...rest].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b)).join() && rest.includes('alpha') && rest.includes('counter'), `the rest are in name order (${rest.join(', ')})`);
+    const again = (await zm.renderStatusItems({ projectDir: null })).map((i) => i.name);
+    ok(again.join() === names.join(), 'and the same the next time');
+  }
+} finally { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-plugin-strict-schema.mjs
new 30d78365f6e6c1a40df68b67
+/**
+ * strict: true ON A PLUGIN TOOL - code_boss checks its WHOLE schema before the tool runs (the developer, 2026-10-29: "yes"
+ * to the host enforcing a precise schema, the plugin keeping only the rules a schema cannot say). schema-check.mjs.
+ *   [1] schemaCheck: enum, nested objects and arrays (where, by path), unknown keys, lengths, patterns, ranges, anyOf
+ *   [2] strictSchemaProblems: a keyword code_boss does not check is named; notes (description, token) are not rules
+ *   [3] through the plugin manager: a strict tool with an unchecked keyword is refused at load, saying which
+ *   [4] a strict tool's bad call throws before its code runs, naming each problem; a good one runs; a loose tool is as before
+ *   [5] the authoring brief says what strict checks, and what stays in the plugin
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-strict-schema.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-strict-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = home;
+const { schemaCheck, strictSchemaProblems } = await import('../src/agent/schema-check.mjs');
+const { createPluginManager } = await import('../src/plugins/index.mjs');
+const { execute, extractContent } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const PARAMS = {
+  type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, required: ['table', 'rows'],
+  properties: {
+    table: { type: 'string', description: 'which table', pattern: '^[A-Z_]+$' },
+    mode: { type: 'string', enum: ['append', 'replace'] },
+    limit: { type: 'integer', minimum: 1, maximum: 1000 },
+    rows: { type: 'array', minItems: 1, items: { type: 'object', required: ['id'], additionalProperties: false, properties: { id: { type: 'integer' }, name: { type: 'string', maxLength: 5 } } } },
+    key: { anyOf: [{ type: 'string', minLength: 3 }, { type: 'integer' }] },
+  },
+};
+

+/**
+ * host.tokens.reveal (prod, 2026-10-28): a datasource plugin's export handed another plugin a password as its
+ * #@datasource.password~…# token; that plugin sent it to Oracle, the login failed, and resolve() would not give it

+ *   [3] back to the model, the revealed value is masked again (the real filter pipeline)

+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'sqlp__1.mjs'), `let H;
+export default {
+  description: 'fixture: uses the data source',
+  dependencies: { datasource: true },
+  init(h) { H = h; },
+  tools: [{ verb: 'sq_probe', name: 'sq_probe', schema: { description: 'x', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } },
+    impl: async () => {
+      const ds = H.use('datasource').getDataSource('db1');
+      const r = H.tokens.reveal(ds);
+      const plain = { notIssued: H.tokens.reveal('#@datasource.password~xxx#abcdefg'), none: H.tokens.reveal('no token here'), num: H.tokens.reveal(5) };
+      const inst = new (class Box { constructor() { this.p = ds.password; } })();
+      return { content: JSON.stringify({ raw: ds.password, own: H.tokens.resolve(ds.password), revealed: r, inSentence: H.tokens.reveal('pw=' + ds.password + ';'), list: H.tokens.reveal([ds.password]), plain, instSame: H.tokens.reveal(inst) === inst }) };
+    } }],
+};
+`);

+ok(/^#@datasource\.password~[^#]+#[0-9a-z]{7}$/.test(out.raw) && out.own === null, `the export gives the token, and resolve() - not the caller's own - gives null (${out.raw.replace(/#[0-9a-z]{7}$/, '#…')})`);
+ok(out.revealed.password === PW && out.revealed.user === 'app' && out.revealed.id === 'db1', 'reveal(object): the password is the value, the rest as it was');
+ok(Array.isArray(out.revealed.urls) && out.revealed.urls[0] === 'jdbc:x://h/db1', 'and an array inside it is kept');
+ok(out.inSentence === 'pw=' + PW + ';' && out.list[0] === PW, 'a token inside a string, and in an array');

+console.log('\n[2] what it leaves alone');
+ok(out.plain.notIssued === '#@datasource.password~xxx#abcdefg' && out.plain.none === 'no token here' && out.plain.num === 5, 'a token nobody issued, text with none, a number: as they were');

+const pipeline = createFilterPipeline(() => pm.enabledFilters(), { ownNames: () => pm.exemptNames(), namesVersion: () => pm.namesVersion(), tokens: store });
+const shown = await pipeline.mask('connected with ' + PW + ' as app', { hook: 'tool-result', toolName: 'sq_probe', projectDir: home });

+ok(/A SECRET YOUR PLUGIN DECRYPTS OR READS/.test(brief) && /a value you issued is written to code-boss\.log as its token/.test(brief), 'and a secret a plugin decrypts is issued at once; the log masks it');

+/**
+ * host.tokens (the developer, 2026-10-12/13): a plugin issues a REFERENCE for something it computed - a browser
+ * plugin's selector for each control in a snapshot - and the agent passes it back without reading it.

+ *   [3] H2: the tool is given the value in its arguments; an invented ref is refused with "take a fresh snapshot"

+ *   [6] a parameter declared `token` takes an issued reference and nothing else - through the real worker

+  const tryIt = (code) => `try { out.push(await ${code}); } catch (e) { out.push("REFUSED " + e.message); }`;
+  const calls = [
+    `tb_fill({ ref: "${mine}", value: "${person}" })`,          // 0 a ref, and a value token beside it

+    `tb_fill({ ref: "#@ref#zzzzzzz", value: "x" })`,            // 2 an invented token

+    `tb_fill({ ref: "${theirs}", value: "x" })`,                // 6 another plugin's ref

+  await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 't', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'go' }], projectDir: proj, extraTools: pm.enabledTools(), getExtraTools: () => pm.enabledTools(),
+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {}, contentFilter: pipeline });

+  ok(refused(r[3]) && /stands for a value, not a reference/.test(r[3]), 'a value token is refused');
+  ok(refused(r[4]) && /another kind/.test(r[4]), 'a reference issued under another name is refused where the name is given');
+  ok(r[5] === 'any' && got.includes('any row-17'), `and taken where the parameter says any reference (${r[5]})`);
+  ok(r[6] === 'filled' && got.includes('fill #other-btn x'), `a reference another plugin issued is accepted (${r[6]})`);
+  ok(refused(r[7]), 'a token with something added to it is refused');
+  ok(r[8] === 'many' && got.includes('many #save=a #other-btn=b'), `a list of { ref, value } is checked and resolved item by item (${r[8]})`);
+  ok(/tb_fill\(.*\[tokens: \d+ in what tb_fill\(\) itself returned, \d+ in what you were given; 2 tokens in the arguments were passed as their real values\]/.test(res), 'and the call\'s record line says its tokens were passed as their values - on any tool, not only a write');
+  ok(r[11] === 'REFUSED tb_any({ ref }): what was passed is not a reference. Call tb_snapshot first and pass one of its ids.', `a plugin's own tokenError replaces the general advice, after the reason (${r[11]})`);
+  ok(got.length === 4, `the refused calls never reached a tool (${got.length} calls did)`);
+  ok(refused(r[9]) && /fields\[1\]\.ref/.test(r[9]), `one written selector in the list refuses the call, naming where (${String(r[9]).slice(0, 60)})`);
+  ok(r[10] === 'clicked #typed', 'a parameter that declares nothing behaves as before');
+  ok(/DECLARE THE PARAMETER AS ONE/.test(String(typeof authoringBrief === 'function' ? authoringBrief({}) : authoringBrief)), 'the brief tells a plugin author to declare it');
+}
+
+await rm(home, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

new 0d15f21d16530a06440aba40
+/**
+ * DEPLOYING A PLUGIN SAYS WHETHER IT LOADED — through the real UI, scripted model.
+ *
+ * Reported 2026-09-25: the agent edited a plugin and reinstalled it, the developer saw a plugin error in the UI, and
+ * the agent never knew — dropping the file in gave it no feedback, so it carried on as if its plugin were live.
+ * The plugins folder is now closed to the agent's own write/copy (DEPLOYED_PLUGINS_MSG): it writes the plugin in the
+ * project and deployPlugin({ file }) installs it, waits for the reload and returns it.
+ *
+ *   [1] a broken plugin deployed: ok:false, deployed:true, and the load error — in the model's next request, and on
+ *       the row the developer sees
+ *   [2] the fixed file deployed over it: ok, "loaded" with its tool names
+ *   [3] a copy() straight into the folder is refused, and the refusal names deployPlugin
+ *   [4] an ordinary write elsewhere is untouched (no plugin field)
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-write-report-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-plugrep-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29603;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+
+const PDIR = userPluginsDir().split('\\').join('/');
+const BROKEN = 'export default { tools: [ { name: "demo_tool", this is not javascript ';
+const GOOD = 'export default { tools: [{ verb: "demo_tool", name: "demo_tool", schema: { parameters: { type: "object", properties: {} } }, impl: async () => "ok" }] };';
+const GOOD2 = GOOD.split('demo_tool').join('copied_tool');
+const js = (body) => `Doing it.\n<remote-workspace><js>${body}</js></remote-workspace>`;
+
+const requests = [];
+const reqText = (req) => (req.messages || []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : (m.content || []).map((b) => b.text || '').join('\n'))).join('\n');
+function reply(text) {
+  // the developer's LAST marker ("<word> step"), and whether its snippet already ran after it ("finished <word>").
+  // Ordinary lower-case words: the shipped filter shows those, and masks an invented one (BROKENQ, RESULT_MARK_X).
+  const marks = [...text.matchAll(/\b(broken|repair|copy|plain) step\b/g)];
+  const lm = marks[marks.length - 1]; const last = lm && lm[1];
+  const answered = !!lm && text.indexOf('finished ' + last, lm.index) >= 0;
+  if (answered) return 'Done. ' + last + ' is done\n' + C;
+  const mark = `"finished ${last}"`;
+  if (last === 'broken') return js(`await write("build/demo__1.mjs", ${JSON.stringify(BROKEN)}); const w = await deployPlugin({ file: "build/demo__1.mjs" }); return { note: ${mark}, w };`);
+  if (last === 'repair') return js(`await write("build/demo__1.mjs", ${JSON.stringify(GOOD)}); const w = await deployPlugin({ file: "build/demo__1.mjs" }); return { note: ${mark}, w };`);
+  if (last === 'copy') return js(`await write("build/copied__1.mjs", ${JSON.stringify(GOOD2)}); let c; try { c = await copy("build/copied__1.mjs", ${JSON.stringify(PDIR + '/copied__1.mjs')}); } catch (e) { c = String(e.message || e); } return { note: ${mark}, c };`);
+  if (last === 'plain') return js(`const w = await write("notes.txt", "hello"); return { note: ${mark}, w };`);
+  return 'ok\n' + C;
+}
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const text = reqText(req); requests.push(text);
+    const body = reply(text);
+    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i += 60) { if (req.signal?.aborted) return; yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: body.slice(i, i + 60) } }] }; await sleep(3); }
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-plugrep-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), '# plugin report\n');
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const errs = [];
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 900 });
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+    const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+    const status = async () => (await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=1')).json()).status;
+    const idle = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 240; i++) { if ((await status()) !== 'running') return true; await sleep(250); } return false; };
+    const running = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) { if ((await status()) === 'running') return true; await sleep(100); } return false; };
+    const type = async (t) => { await page.evaluate((x) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = x; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); i.focus(); }, t); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); };
+    // the request AFTER the snippet: it carries the snippet's run record
+    const after = (mark) => requests.filter((r) => r.includes('finished ' + mark)).pop() || '';
+    const ask = async (m, t) => { await type(t); await running(); await idle(); await sleep(400); return after(m); };
+
+    console.log('\n[1] a broken plugin deployed');
+    const t1 = await ask('broken', 'broken step install the demo plugin');
+    ok(/FAILED TO LOAD/.test(t1), `the model's next request carries the load failure (${(t1.match(/plugin demo[^\n"]{0,120}/) || [''])[0]})`);
+    ok(/"ok":\s*false/.test(t1) && /"deployed":\s*true/.test(t1), 'as ok:false, deployed:true (the file is there, the plugin is not running)');
+    const row1 = await page.evaluate(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .row, #log .js-step-plugin')].map((r) => r.className + ': ' + r.innerText).filter((t) => /FAILED TO LOAD/.test(t)).join(' | '));
+    ok(/FAILED TO LOAD/.test(row1), `the developer sees it in the chat too (${row1.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 140)})`);
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(__dirname, 'plugin-write-report-1.png') });
+
+    console.log('\n[2] the fixed file deployed over it');
+    const t2 = await ask('repair', 'repair step fix the demo plugin');
+    const rows2 = await page.evaluate(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .js-step-plugin')].map((r) => r.className + ': ' + r.innerText));
+    ok(rows2.some((t) => /^js-step-plugin: .*loaded: demo_tool/.test(t)), `and on the row, muted (${rows2.join(' | ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 200)})`);
+    ok(/plugin demo v1 loaded: demo_tool/.test(t2), `"loaded" with its tools (${(t2.match(/plugin demo[^\n"]{0,80}/) || [''])[0]})`);
+    ok(!/FAILED TO LOAD/.test(t2.slice(t2.lastIndexOf('finished repair') - 400)), 'and no failure in that result');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] a copy straight into the plugins folder is refused');
+    const t3 = await ask('copy', 'copy step build and install the copied plugin');
+    const seg3 = t3.slice(t3.lastIndexOf('<remote-workspace-result'));
+    ok(/BLOCKED: that is where code_boss keeps DEPLOYED plugins/.test(seg3) && /deployPlugin\(\{ file/.test(seg3), `refused, and told to use deployPlugin (${(seg3.match(/BLOCKED[^\n"]{0,80}/) || [''])[0]})`);
+    ok(!existsSync(path.join(userPluginsDir(), 'copied__1.mjs')), 'and nothing landed in the folder');
+
+    console.log('\n[4] an ordinary write is untouched');
+    const t4 = await ask('plain', 'plain step write a note');
+    const seg = t4.slice(t4.lastIndexOf('<remote-workspace-result'));   // this snippet's own result, not the copy's before it
+    ok(/notes\.txt/.test(seg) && !/"plugin"/.test(seg), 'no plugin field on a write outside the plugins folder');
+
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors${errs.length ? ': ' + errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ') : ''}`);
+  } catch (e) { ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e)); }
+  finally { await browser.close().catch(() => {}); server.close(); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+function findChrome() {
+  const candidates = [
+    'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  ];
+  return candidates.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}

+function startMockEndpoint({ modelsDelayMs }) {
+  const srv = createServer((req, res) => {
+    const url = new URL(req.url, `http://${req.headers.host}`);
+    if (url.pathname === '/models') {
+      // Match the user's case: 11s delay, then 200 with models.
+      setTimeout(() => {
+        res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });
+        res.end(JSON.stringify({ data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }));
+      }, modelsDelayMs);
+      return;
+    }
+    res.writeHead(404); res.end();
+  });
+  return new Promise((resolve) => srv.listen(MOCK_PORT, () => resolve(srv)));
+}

+    }, { timeout: 10000 });
+    await page.type('#glCredsUser', 'fake-user');
+    await page.type('#glCredsToken', 'fake-token');
+    await page.click('#glCredsSave');
+
+    // 3. Wait for init to complete (loadModels takes 11s).
+    await page.waitForSelector('.welcome', { timeout: 20000 });
+    ok(true, 'chat appeared after key save + 11s models wait');
+
+    // 4. Inspect modal classes — the freeze symptom suggests an invisible
+    //    overlay is still claiming clicks.
+    const state = await page.evaluate(() => ({
+      initModalShown: document.getElementById('initModalBg')?.classList.contains('show'),
+      mainModalShown: document.getElementById('modalBg')?.classList.contains('show'),
+      settingsModalShown: document.getElementById('settingsModalBg')?.classList.contains('show'),
+      initViewHidden: document.getElementById('initView')?.classList.contains('hidden'),
+      chatHidden: document.getElementById('chat')?.classList.contains('hidden'),
+      // Check what's on top at the settings-icon coordinates.
+      settingsBtn: (() => {
+        const btn = document.getElementById('settingsBtn');
+        if (!btn) return { exists: false };
+        const r = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
+        const cx = r.x + r.width / 2, cy = r.y + r.height / 2;
+        const top = document.elementFromPoint(cx, cy);
+        return {
+          exists: true,
+          rect: { x: r.x, y: r.y, w: r.width, h: r.height },
+          topElementId: top?.id || top?.className || top?.tagName,
+          topIsButton: top === btn || btn.contains(top),
+        };
+      })(),
+    }));
+    console.log('     state:', JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
+    ok(!state.initModalShown, 'initModalBg is not stuck on "show"');
+    ok(!state.mainModalShown, 'modalBg (snapshot) is not stuck on "show"');
+    ok(!state.settingsModalShown, 'settingsModalBg is not stuck on "show"');
+    ok(state.initViewHidden, 'initView is hidden');
+    ok(!state.chatHidden, 'chat is visible');
+    ok(state.settingsBtn?.topIsButton, `settings button is the topmost element at its coordinates (got top=${state.settingsBtn?.topElementId})`);
+
+    // 5. Actually try to click it.
+    let settingsOpened = false;
+    try {
+      await page.click('#settingsBtn');
+      await page.waitForFunction(
+        () => document.getElementById('settingsModalBg')?.classList.contains('show'),
+        { timeout: 3000 }
+      );
+      settingsOpened = true;
+    } catch {}
+    ok(settingsOpened, 'settings modal opens on gear click (THIS IS THE FREEZE SYMPTOM)');
+
+    ok(jsErrors.length === 0, `no JS errors (got ${jsErrors.length}: ${jsErrors.slice(0, 2).join('; ')})`);
+
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    cbServer.close();
+    mockSrv.close();
+    await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/test-preamble-probe.mjs
new eccd67df2b651674cd7fb9fa
+/**
+ * SNAPSHOT #76 — "why does it repeat itself?"
+ *
+ * A production reply stated the same four-item plan four times before its first tool call, in ONE call. Three
+ * things could cause that and they need different fixes:
+ *
+ *   OUR PROMPT    "state a plan first" and "lead with a sentence before every block" are both satisfied by
+ *                 writing the plan and then restating it.
+ *   THE MODEL     gemini-3.1-pro-preview does it unprompted.
+ *   THE ENDPOINT  api.genai.mil may be a Gemini Enterprise AGENT sitting on the model. An agent layer that plans
+ *                 before acting would produce exactly this, and no instruction of ours would ever remove it.
+ *
+ * So the thing under test is the VERDICT logic. A probe that blamed our prompt for a wrapper's output would send
+ * someone to rewrite a prompt that cannot fix it; one that blamed the endpoint for our own rule would send them
+ * to argue with an ops team about a bug we wrote. Each case below is an endpoint that behaves one of those ways.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-preamble-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-preamble-home-'));
+const { runPreambleProbe, formatPreambleProbe, preambleProbeState, resetPreambleProbe } = await import('../src/agent/preamble-probe.mjs');
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const PORT = 29311;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const TIGHT = 'Reading the file first.\n<remote-workspace><read path="index.html"/></remote-workspace>';
+// The production shape: the same plan, four ways, before the block.
+const PADDED = 'I will write a script to apply all of these updates:\n1. Rename the button.\n2. Table the pool.\n'
+  + '3. Highlight columns.\n4. Explain mismatches.\n\n'
+  + 'I will read index.html, update the labels, refactor the pool into a table, highlight the matching columns, and expand the note.\n\n'
+  + 'I will update index.html to address all of those visual and behavioral requests.\n\n'
+  + 'This includes:\n1. Renaming the button.\n2. Converting the pool.\n3. Highlighting columns.\n4. Replacing the note.\n\n'
+  + '<remote-workspace><read path="index.html"/></remote-workspace>';
+
+const reply = (content) => ({ choices: [{ message: { content } }] });
+// The one-word control has to be answered like a one-word control. A mock that returns the same 80-character
+// reply to it looks exactly like a wrapper padding a request with no room to plan - and tripped that verdict
+// on every case, which is the probe working correctly on an unrealistic endpoint.
+const isOneWord = (sent) => /Reply with exactly one word/.test(sent);
+/** Pads only when the request contains `trigger` — so a rung's added rule is what turns it on. */
+const adapterThatPadsWhen = (trigger) => ({
+  async complete(req) {
+    const sent = String(req.messages[0].content);
+    if (isOneWord(sent)) return reply(trigger === 'always' ? PADDED : 'ready');
+    if (trigger === 'always') return reply(PADDED);
+    if (trigger && sent.includes(trigger)) return reply(PADDED);
+    return reply(TIGHT);
+  },
+});
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[1] padding that appears with NO rules at all is not our prompt');
+  {
+    // The rung that matters most. Our prompt cannot cause something that happens without it, and blaming it
+    // would send someone to rewrite a prompt that is not the problem.
+    const r = await runPreambleProbe({ adapter: adapterThatPadsWhen('always'), model: 'm' });
+    const f = formatPreambleProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/^THE ENDPOINT OR THE MODEL/.test(f.body), 'verdict points away from our prompt');
+    ok(/Gemini Enterprise agent/i.test(f.body), 'and names the wrapper possibility explicitly');
+    ok(f.severity === 'critical', 'flagged as critical (' + f.severity + ')');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] padding that starts when "state a plan" is added is OUR rule');
+  {
+    const r = await runPreambleProbe({ adapter: adapterThatPadsWhen('THINK BEFORE YOU ACT'), model: 'm' });
+    const f = formatPreambleProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/state a plan first/i.test(f.body) && /OUR PROMPT/.test(f.body), 'the plan rule is named as the cause');
+    ok(r.rows.find((x) => x.key === 'bare').preamble < 400, 'and the bare rung stayed tight, which is what makes that conclusion safe');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] padding that needs BOTH rules is the interaction, not either rule');
+  {
+    // The suspected mechanism: each rule is harmless alone.
+    const r = await runPreambleProbe({ adapter: adapterThatPadsWhen('lead with one short sentence'), model: 'm' });
+    const f = formatPreambleProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/two rules TOGETHER/i.test(f.body), `the interaction is identified (${f.body.slice(0, 70)})`);
+    ok(r.rows.find((x) => x.key === 'plan').preamble < 400, 'plan-only stayed tight');
+    ok(r.rows.find((x) => x.key === 'leadin').preamble > 400, 'both-rules did not');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] the counter-rule working is reported as the fix');
+  {
+    // Pads once both rules are present, stops once the counter-rule is added — the outcome we want to be able to
+    // recognise, since it is the one that says "ship the prompt change".
+    const adapter = {
+      async complete(req) {
+        const sent = String(req.messages[0].content);
+        if (isOneWord(sent)) return reply('ready');
+        if (sent.includes('A PLAN IS ALSO A LEAD-IN')) return reply(TIGHT);
+        return reply(sent.includes('lead with one short sentence') ? PADDED : TIGHT);
+      },
+    };
+    const r = await runPreambleProbe({ adapter, model: 'm' });
+    const f = formatPreambleProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/COUNTER-RULE WORKS/.test(f.body), 'the fix is reported as confirmed on this endpoint');
+    ok(f.severity === 'ok', 'and not flagged as a problem (' + f.severity + ')');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] a counter-rule that does NOT hold is reported as such');
+  {
+    // The outcome worth knowing about most: instruction is not going to fix it.
+    const adapter = {
+      async complete(req) {
+        const sent = String(req.messages[0].content);
+        if (isOneWord(sent)) return reply('ready');
+        return reply(sent.includes('lead with one short sentence') ? PADDED : TIGHT);
+      },
+    };
+    const r = await runPreambleProbe({ adapter, model: 'm' });
+    const f = formatPreambleProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/COUNTER-RULE DOES NOT HOLD/.test(f.body), 'reported as unfixable by instruction');
+    ok(/different model, or the endpoint/.test(f.body), 'and says what is left to try');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[6] the two controls catch a wrapper our prompting cannot reach');
+  {
+    // A one-word request and an explicit one-sentence ceiling. Padding that survives either is not ours.
+    const chatty = { async complete() { return reply(PADDED); } };
+    const r = await runPreambleProbe({ adapter: chatty, model: 'm' });
+    const oneword = r.rows.find((x) => x.key === 'oneword');
+    const terse = r.rows.find((x) => x.key === 'terse');
+    ok(oneword && oneword.chars > 40, 'the one-word control notices a long reply');
+    ok(terse && terse.preamble > 400, 'and the one-sentence ceiling notices it too');
+    const f = formatPreambleProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/stayed long even when told to answer in one sentence/.test(f.body), 'which is called out separately, as the loudest wrapper signal');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[7] a well-behaved endpoint reproduces nothing, and says so');
+  {
+    // Never invent a finding. "Not reproduced" has to be a reportable outcome or the probe is a rubber stamp.
+    const r = await runPreambleProbe({ adapter: adapterThatPadsWhen(null), model: 'm' });
+    const f = formatPreambleProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/^NOT REPRODUCED/.test(f.body), 'reported as not reproduced');
+    ok(/Compare against #77/.test(f.body), 'with the next step named rather than left implied');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[7b] LONG but not repeating is verbosity, not the reported pattern');
+  {
+    // The first real run: the bare rung came back with 536 characters and ZERO statements of intent — the model
+    // asking for the file it needed — and a length-based threshold called that "the endpoint padding an
+    // unprompted request", which points at whoever operates the endpoint. An expensive place to send someone on
+    // a false positive, and #77 already drew this distinction where this did not.
+    const longNoRepeat = 'I would love to help you update index.html with these new features, but I need your current file first. '.repeat(5);
+    const adapter = { async complete(req) { return reply(isOneWord(String(req.messages[0].content)) ? 'ready' : longNoRepeat); } };
+    const r = await runPreambleProbe({ adapter, model: 'm' });
+    const f = formatPreambleProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/^NOT REPRODUCED/.test(f.body), `not blamed on the endpoint (${f.body.slice(0, 40)})`);
+    ok(/verbosity, not the/.test(f.body), 'and the length is reported as verbosity');
+    ok(!/THE ENDPOINT OR THE MODEL/.test(f.body), 'nobody is sent to argue with the endpoint operators');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[8] the rules are sent where this endpoint will actually read them');
+  {
+    // genai.mil/Gemini ignores role:'system'. A rung whose instruction was dropped on the floor would read as
+    // "the rule did nothing" — the exact wrong conclusion, and an easy mistake to make.
+    let seenRoles = [];
+    const spy = { async complete(req) { seenRoles = req.messages.map((m) => m.role); return reply(TIGHT); } };
+    await runPreambleProbe({ adapter: spy, model: 'm' });
+    ok(!seenRoles.includes('system'), `no role:'system' is used (${seenRoles.join(',')})`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[9] #76 is reachable through the real server');
+  {
+    resetPreambleProbe();
+    const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-preamble-'));
+    const { server } = await startServer({
+      html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+      defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: adapterThatPadsWhen(null), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+    });
+    try {
+      const one = async () => (await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/queries?ids=76`)).json()).tiles?.[0] ?? {};
+      // ONE run: the tile waits for its own probe (see #73).
+      const first = await one();
+      ok(first.id === 76, 'the catalog entry renders (' + JSON.stringify(first.name) + ')');
+      ok(/NOT REPRODUCED/.test(first.body || ''), 'and the FIRST run returns the verdict, not a "come back later"');
+      ok(preambleProbeState().status === 'done', 'and the module state settled');
+    } finally { server.close(); await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n' + pass + ' passed, ' + fail + ' failed');
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
