+  ok(cut.holds === false && /40LY 120LY 300LCUT/.test(cut.measured), `a size that arrives but is CUT is scored apart from one that does not arrive (${cut.measured})`);
+  ok(/the end marker survives up to ~\d+ characters and not beyond/.test(cut.note), 'with the ceiling stated as a number');
+  ok(/a cut-off commit is a file silently truncated rather than a failed one/.test(cut.note), 'and why it is dangerous rather than merely annoying');
+  ok(/sent \d+c, back \d+c/.test(cut.detail), 'sent and returned lengths are both printed, per size');
+
+  // ── we ship an ESCAPE KEY, and a cancelled turn is a daily event ──
+  const cancel = byName['a-cancelled-turn-does-not-poison-the-session'];
+  const cancelEp = (mode) => {
+    let mk = null;
+    return async (messages, o) => {
+      const body = String(messages[messages.length - 1].content || '');
+      const p = body.match(/Remember this marker: (SPEC-[A-Z0-9]{6})/);
+      if (p) { mk = p[1]; return { text: 'noted', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S', assist_token: 'A' } } }; }
+      if (/2000 word essay/.test(body)) {
+        // The rung aborts this one mid-flight; honour the signal the way probeCall does.
+        await new Promise((res, rej) => { const t = setTimeout(res, 5000); o?.signal?.addEventListener('abort', () => { clearTimeout(t); rej(new Error('aborted')); }, { once: true }); });
+        return { text: 'a long essay', raw: {} };
+      }
+      if (mode === 'bleed') return { text: 'As I was saying about distributed systems…', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } };
+      return { text: mode === 'lost' ? 'NONE' : mk, raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } };
+    };
+  };
+  ok((await cancel.plan({ call: cancelEp('ok') })).holds === true, 'a session that survives an aborted turn holds the claim');
+  const bled = await cancel.plan({ call: cancelEp('bleed') });
+  ok(bled.holds === false && /BLEED/.test(bled.measured) && /we cannot take it back/.test(bled.note),
+    'the abandoned turn leaking into the next answer is caught — a cancelled request stays in THEIR transcript');
+  const lost = await cancel.plan({ call: cancelEp('lost') });
+  ok(lost.holds === false && /every Escape a developer presses/.test(lost.note), 'and losing the conversation to an Escape is named as the daily event it would be');
+
+  // ── developers walk away ──
+  const pause = byName['a-session-survives-a-pause'];
+  ok(/PAUSE = 60000/.test(String(pause.plan)), 'the pause is a real minute, not a token gesture');
+  ok(pause.claim.includes('minute'), 'and the claim says how long, because the number is the finding');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[13b] does the sandbox survive a PINNED turn? the old test could not say\n');
+{
+  // The sandbox was written off as "wiped between turns" on the strength of a test that fabricated its own earlier
+  // turn — and a fabricated prior turn never reaches the model, so the file was never written. It measured
+  // nothing. This asks with the session pinned, and it is fraud-proof: the model generates the content itself and
+  // commits to a hash of it before we ever see it, so a matching reply cannot be invented and cannot be
+  // recomputed from the conversation either — the characters were never in the conversation.
+  const s7 = byName['a-pinned-session-keeps-its-cloud-files'];
+  const CHARS = '0123456789abcdef';
+  const DIGEST = createHash('sha256').update(CHARS).digest('hex');
+  const sandbox = (mode) => {
+    const seen = [];
+    return async (messages, o) => {
+      const body = String(messages[messages.length - 1].content || '');
+      seen.push({ pinned: !!o?.extra, body });
+      if (/generate 16 random/.test(body)) return { text: DIGEST, raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S', assist_token: 'A' } } };
+      if (!/Read \/tmp/.test(body)) return { text: 'ok', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } };
+      if (mode === 'persists' && o?.extra) return { text: CHARS, raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } };
+      if (mode === 'liar') return { text: 'ffffffffffffffff', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } };
+      return { text: 'NOFILE', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } };
+    };
+  };
+  const lives = await s7.plan({ call: sandbox('persists') });
+  ok(lives.holds === false && /pinned=PERSISTS/.test(lives.measured), `a sandbox that survives a pinned turn breaks the claim (${lives.measured})`);
+  ok(/check code out once and keep working in there/.test(lives.note), 'and says what it would buy: no re-sending the files every turn');
+  ok(/unpinned=gone/.test(lives.measured), 'with the unpinned arm alongside it, which is the one the old test was really measuring');
+  const wiped = await s7.plan({ call: sandbox('gone') });
+  ok(wiped.holds === true && /so this is the sandbox resetting and not a turn that failed to arrive/.test(wiped.detail),
+    'a wipe under a PINNED session is the first time that result has meant anything');
+  const liar = await s7.plan({ call: sandbox('liar') });
+  ok(liar.holds === true && /pinned=fabricated/.test(liar.measured), `contents that do not hash to the committed digest are scored as fabrication, not persistence (${liar.measured})`);
+  ok(/answered anyway, which is worth knowing on its own/.test(liar.detail), 'and answering confidently about a file that is gone is called out');
+  const noDigest = await s7.plan({ call: async () => ({ text: 'Sure, done!', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } }) });
+  ok(noDigest.holds === null && /never committed to a digest/.test(noDigest.note), 'and with no digest committed up front there is nothing to verify against');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[14b] ticking a group means RUN ONLY THAT GROUP\n');
+{
+  // It used to mean "run these FIRST, then everything else", so "just check the session protocol" was a 130-call
+  // run that happened to answer that part early. A targeted question should cost what it is worth.
+  let calls = 0;
+  const adapter = { baseURL: 'https://x.test/v1', async complete() { calls++; return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'READY' } }] }; } };
+  const only7 = await runProdSpec({ adapter, model: 'm', layers: [7] });
+  ok(only7.results.every((r) => r.layer === 7), `ticking L7 runs ONLY layer 7 (${only7.results.length} rungs)`);
+  // Retired rungs keep their id and their place in the layer but never run, so the expected count excludes them.
+  ok(only7.results.length === SPEC.filter((a) => a.layer === 7 && !a.retired).length, 'and all of it that still runs');
+  ok(!only7.layersDone.includes(0), 'layer 0 does not run just because it is the floor');
+  ok(!only7.results.some((r) => r.id === '0d'), 'and the `first` queue-jumper does not sneak in from an unticked layer either');
+  ok(only7.pending === 0, 'a filtered run reports nothing pending — that IS the whole run');
+  // EVERY RUNG HAS A NUMBER OF ITS OWN (rung-numbers.mjs), handed out once and never changed
+  const { RUNG_NUMBERS, rungIdsFor, rungNumber } = await import('../src/agent/rung-numbers.mjs');
+  const nums = Object.values(RUNG_NUMBERS), allIds = new Set(SPEC.map((a) => a.id));
+  const missing = SPEC.filter((a) => rungNumber(a.id) == null).map((a) => a.id), stale = Object.keys(RUNG_NUMBERS).filter((id) => !allIds.has(id));
+  ok(missing.length === 0, `every rung has a number${missing.length ? ' - give these the next free ones in src/agent/rung-numbers.mjs: ' + missing.join(', ') : ''}`);
+  ok(stale.length === 0 && new Set(nums).size === nums.length && nums.every((n) => Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 200 && n < 1000), `no number is used twice, names a rung that is gone, or leaves 200-999${stale.length ? ' (' + stale.join(',') + ')' : ''}`);
+  ok(rungNumber('0a') === 200 && rungNumber('9k') === 330 && rungNumber('9l') === 331 && rungNumber('9q') === 336 && rungNumber('9ad') === 349, 'and the ones already quoted keep theirs (0a=200, 9k=330, 9l=331)');
+  ok(JSON.stringify(rungIdsFor([330, '331', 999, 'x', null])) === '["9k","9l"]', 'a list of numbers is the rungs it names; anything else is dropped');
+  const one = await runProdSpec({ adapter, model: 'm', layers: rungIdsFor([330]) });
+  ok(one.results.length === 1 && one.results[0].id === '9k', `so typing 330 runs ONE rung (${one.results.map((r) => r.id).join(',')})`);
+
+  const two = await runProdSpec({ adapter, model: 'm', layers: [8, 0] });
+  ok(new Set(two.results.map((r) => r.layer)).size === 2, 'two ticked groups run and nothing else');
+  ok(two.layersDone.filter((l) => l !== 'first').join(',') === '8,0', `in the order they were asked for (${two.layersDone.join(',')})`);
+  ok(two.results.some((r) => r.id === '0d') && two.layersDone[0] === 'first', 'and the queue-jumper DOES run when its own layer is ticked');
+
+  const none = await runProdSpec({ adapter, model: 'm', layers: [] });
+  const live = SPEC.filter((a) => !a.retired).length;
+  ok(none.results.length === live, `ticking nothing still runs everything live (${none.results.length}/${live}) — a spec that runs nothing by default is a spec nobody runs`);
+  ok((await runProdSpec({ adapter, model: 'm', layers: [99] })).results.length === live, 'and a layer that does not exist is ignored rather than running zero assertions');
+  ok(only7.results.length < none.results.length, 'the whole point: the filtered run is smaller than the full one');
+  const hdr = formatProdSpecReport(only7);
+  ok(/layers=7/.test(hdr) && !/layers=first/.test(hdr), 'the report header names the groups that actually ran');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[14a] 7f said the session thins out — 7g finds where\n');
+{
+  // 7f came back 0/4 while 7a recalled at depth 4, 7d kept 19/19 verbs from a prompt four turns old and 7e
+  // survived a tool-result turn. So the only number that matters is where it stops, and 7f cannot say: it varied
+  // the depth AND the shape at once (task-shaped plants, the first buried in a 24k prompt).
+  const g7 = byName['a-pinned-session-has-no-depth-limit'];
+  const window = (depth) => {
+    const said = [];
+    const turns = [];
+    return {
+      turns,
+      fn: async (messages, o) => {
+        turns.push({ n: messages.length, pinned: !!o?.extra });
+        const body = String(messages[messages.length - 1].content || '');
+        const plant = body.match(/^Marker (\d+): (SPEC-[A-Z0-9]{6})/);
+        if (plant) { said[Number(plant[1])] = plant[2]; return { text: 'noted', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S', assist_token: 'A' } } }; }
+        const q = Number((body.match(/What was marker (\d+)/) || [])[1]);
+        // The session reaches back `depth` turns from the ask and no further.
+        const back = 11 - q;
+        return { text: back <= depth ? said[q] : 'NONE', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } };
+      },
+    };
+  };
+  const deep = window(99);
+  const full = await g7.plan({ call: deep.fn });
+  ok(full.holds === true && /1Y 3Y 5Y 7Y 9Y 10Y/.test(full.measured), `a session with no depth limit holds (${full.measured})`);
+  ok(deep.turns.length === 16, `ten plants and six asks, one message each (${deep.turns.length} turns)`);
+  ok(deep.turns.slice(1).every((t) => t.n === 1 && t.pinned), 'every turn after the first carries the handle and one message');
+  const short = await g7.plan({ call: window(5).fn });
+  ok(short.holds === false && /reaches back 5 turn\(s\) and no further/.test(short.note), 'a window is reported as a NUMBER, because that is what a fix designs against');
+  ok(/re-send it before turn 6/.test(short.note), 'and turned straight into the rule the design would need');
+  ok(/1Y 3Y 5Y 7N 9N 10N/.test(short.measured), `with the whole curve visible, not just the cutoff (${short.measured})`);
+  ok(/most-recent-first/.test(short.detail), 'the ask order is stated — answering a shallow question must not push a deep one further away');
+  const none = await g7.plan({ call: window(0).fn });
+  ok(none.holds === false && /contradicts 7a/.test(none.note), 'and nothing at any depth is flagged as contradicting 7a rather than quietly reported');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[15] concurrency, eviction, its own turns, a tampered handle, and how repetitive is too repetitive\n');
+{
+  // ── 7h: THE ONE THAT DECIDES WHETHER ANY OF THIS SHIPS ──
+  // code_boss does not run one conversation at a time — the reviewer forks the worker's prompt and runs alongside
+  // it. Every session rung so far measured a single conversation on a quiet line.
+  const h7 = byName['two-pinned-sessions-do-not-cross-talk'];
+  const twoSessions = (mode) => {
+    const held = {};
+    let n = 0;
+    return async (messages, o) => {
+      const body = String(messages[messages.length - 1].content || '');
+      const plant = body.match(/Remember this marker: (SPEC-[A-Z0-9]{6})/);
+      if (plant) { const id = 'S' + (++n); held[id] = plant[1]; return { text: 'noted', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: id, assist_token: 'A' } } }; }
+      const id = o?.extra?.gemini_enterprise?.session;
+      if (mode === 'shared') return { text: held.S1, raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S1' } } };   // both answer with the first
+      if (mode === 'amnesia') return { text: 'NONE', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: id } } };
+      return { text: held[id] || 'NONE', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: id } } };
+    };
+  };
+  const isolated = await h7.plan({ call: twoSessions('ok') });
+  ok(isolated.holds === true && /own=3\/3 crossed=0/.test(isolated.measured), `two sessions staying separate holds (${isolated.measured})`);
+  const leaked = await h7.plan({ call: twoSessions('shared') });
+  ok(leaked.holds === false && /crossed=[1-9]/.test(leaked.measured), 'a session answering with the OTHER conversation\'s marker breaks it');
+  ok(/a forked reviewer would report on work that never happened/.test(leaked.note), 'and it names the production consequence, not just the symptom');
+  const forgot = await h7.plan({ call: twoSessions('amnesia') });
+  ok(forgot.holds === false && /one conversation at a time/.test(forgot.note), 'and losing recall once a second session exists is its own distinct verdict');
+  const sameSes = await h7.plan({ call: async () => ({ text: 'noted', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'ONE' } } }) });
+  ok(sameSes.holds === false && sameSes.measured === 'ONE-SESSION', 'two unrelated conversations handed the SAME session fails immediately, before any marker is asked for');
+
+  // ── 7i: ours against the traffic around it ──
+  const i7 = byName['unrelated-traffic-does-not-evict-a-session'];
+  const evicting = (survives) => {
+    let mk = null, n = 0;
+    return async (messages, o) => {
+      const body = String(messages[messages.length - 1].content || '');
+      const p = body.match(/Remember this marker: (SPEC-[A-Z0-9]{6})/);
+      if (p) { mk = p[1]; return { text: 'noted', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'MINE', assist_token: 'A' } } }; }
+      if (!o?.extra) return { text: 'A sentence.', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'OTHER' + (++n) } } };
+      return { text: survives ? mk : 'NONE', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'MINE' } } };
+    };
+  };
+  const kept = await i7.plan({ call: evicting(true) });
+  ok(kept.holds === true && /survived=Y across=6 other sessions/.test(kept.measured), `surviving six unrelated conversations holds (${kept.measured})`);
+  const evicted = await i7.plan({ call: evicting(false) });
+  ok(evicted.holds === false && /nothing in the reply would tell us it had gone/.test(evicted.note), 'and being evicted is called out as SILENT — which is what makes it dangerous');
+
+  // ── 7j: 7d proved OUR words survive; this asks whether ITS do ──
+  const j7 = byName['it-remembers-its-own-turns'];
+  const ownTurn = (remembers) => async (messages, o) => {
+    const body = String(messages[messages.length - 1].content || '');
+    if (/random four-digit/.test(body)) return { text: '4471', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S', assist_token: 'A' } } };
+    if (/four-digit number did YOU/.test(body)) return { text: remembers ? '4471' : 'NONE', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } };
+    return { text: 'ok', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } };
+  };
+  ok((await j7.plan({ call: ownTurn(true) })).holds === true, 'a session that keeps its own reply holds the claim');
+  const amnesic = await j7.plan({ call: ownTurn(false) });
+  ok(amnesic.holds === false && /re-issue work we have already paid for/.test(amnesic.note), 'and forgetting its own turn is stated as a LOOP risk — a tool call it already made is not in the transcript either');
+  const noNum = await j7.plan({ call: async () => ({ text: 'I would rather not.', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S' } } }) });
+  ok(noNum.holds === null && /nothing of its own to remember/.test(noNum.note), 'and a turn where it never picked a number measures nothing rather than failing');
+
+  // ── 7k: is the handle VALIDATED? ──
+  // One character of OUR OWN id flipped — never a guessed one. Probing a stranger's conversation is not a thing to
+  // do on this box to answer a design question.
+  const k7 = byName['a-mutated-handle-is-rejected'];
+  let sentIds = [];
+  const tamper = (mode) => async (messages, o) => {
+    const body = String(messages[messages.length - 1].content || '');
+    const p = body.match(/Remember this marker: (SPEC-[A-Z0-9]{6})/);
+    if (p) { sentIds.push(null); return { text: 'noted', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'abc123', assist_token: 'A' } }, _m: p[1] }; }
+    sentIds.push(o?.extra?.gemini_enterprise?.session || null);
+    if (mode === 'reject') throw new Error('HTTP 404 unknown session');
+    if (mode === 'honours') return { text: 'SPEC-KEEP', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'abc123' } } };
+    return { text: 'NONE', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'zzz' } } };
+  };
+  sentIds = [];
+  const refused = await k7.plan({ call: tamper('reject') });
+  ok(refused.holds === true && /rejected \(HTTP 404/.test(refused.measured), `a refused handle holds the claim (${refused.measured})`);
+  ok(sentIds[1] === 'abc12z', `exactly one character was changed, same length (abc123 -> ${sentIds[1]})`);
+  sentIds = [];
+  const ignored7k = await k7.plan({ call: tamper('ignored') });
+  ok(ignored7k.holds === false && /silently ignored rather than refused/.test(ignored7k.note), 'silently ignoring it breaks the claim — a mangled id would look exactly like lost memory');
+  ok(/would look exactly like a conversation that lost its memory/.test(ignored7k.note), 'and that is the reason it matters: the failure is indistinguishable from the normal one');
+
+  // ── 0g: how repetitive is too repetitive ──
+  const g0 = byName['repetition-has-no-threshold-that-mutes-it'];
+  const repetition = (muteAtOrBelow) => async (messages) => {
+    const c = String(messages[0].content);
+    const distinct = new Set(c.split('\n').filter((l) => /^Line \d+ of the reference/.test(l))).size;
+    if (distinct <= muteAtOrBelow) return { text: '', raw: {}, usage: { prompt_tokens: 3000 } };
+    return { text: (c.match(/SPEC-[A-Z0-9]{6}/) || [''])[0], raw: {}, usage: { prompt_tokens: 3000 } };
+  };
+  const fine = await g0.plan({ call: repetition(0) });
+  ok(fine.holds === true && /200Y 50Y 10Y 2Y 1Y/.test(fine.measured), `answering at every level of repetition holds (${fine.measured})`);
+  const threshold = await g0.plan({ call: repetition(2) });
+  ok(threshold.holds === false && /200Y 50Y 10Y 2S 1S/.test(threshold.measured), `and the curve shows where it flips, not just that it does (${threshold.measured})`);
+  ok(/goes silent at 2 distinct line\(s\) and still answers at 10/.test(threshold.note), 'reported as the two numbers a fix has to sit between');
+  ok(/compaction and file dumps have to stay above/.test(threshold.note), 'and tied to the output WE generate — that is why it is worth a rung');
+  ok(/flat tokens would mean the length really was identical/.test(threshold.detail), 'with the control stated: only the number of distinct lines changes, not the length');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[14] the prose spec — generated by the build, and provably not edited\n');
+{
+  // Each assertion's one-sentence `claim` already existed; in layer order they ARE the specification, and the only
+  // way to read them used to be running 130 live calls and photographing the report. The build writes them out, so
+  // the document ships in the same artifact as the code that checks it.
+  const prose = formatProdSpecProse(null);
+  ok(SPEC.every((a) => prose.includes(a.claim)), `every one of the ${SPEC.length} claims appears in the prose spec`);
+  ok(SPEC.every((a) => new RegExp(`\\*\\*${a.id}\\*\\*`).test(prose)), 'each one labelled with the id the report quotes back');
+  for (const l of SPEC_LAYERS) ok(prose.includes(`## ${l.id}. ${l.name} — ${l.claim}`), `layer ${l.id} is a heading carrying its own claim (${l.name})`);
+  const order = SPEC.map((a) => prose.indexOf(`**${a.id}**`));
+  ok(order.every((n, i) => i === 0 || n > order[i - 1]), 'and they are in spec order, so reading top to bottom is reading the dependency order');
+  ok(SPEC.filter((a) => a.expectBroken).every((a) => new RegExp(`\\*\\*${a.id}\\*\\* ${a.claim.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')} †`).test(prose)),
+    'a claim we already know production violates is marked †, so nobody reads it as fact');
+  ok(!/✓ holds/.test(prose), 'with no run there is no verdict legend — symbols that never appear read as an unfinished document');
+
+  // With a run, the note travels with the claim. On a BROKEN claim the claim is the thing that was wrong, so a
+  // document printing it alone would teach the reader the opposite of what the run found.
+  const withRun = formatProdSpecProse({
+    model: 'm', endpoint: 'api.example.mil', started: Date.parse('2026-08-12T18:22:00Z'),
+    results: [
+      { id: '1x', layer: 1, name: 'n', claim: 'our static prompt arrives whether the request is one message or twenty', holds: false, note: 'a multi-message request is billed for its LAST message alone', measured: 'single=19/19@5869t multi=19/19@433t' },
+      { id: '7a', layer: 7, name: 'n', claim: 'we can send ONLY the new message', holds: true, note: '', measured: 'pinned=Y' },
+      { id: '0e', layer: 0, name: 'n', claim: 'a big message comes back with something', holds: null, note: 'empty reply TWICE', measured: 'SILENT' },
+    ],
+  });
+  ok(/✗ our static prompt arrives/.test(withRun), 'a refuted claim is marked ✗ rather than quietly printed as fact');
+  ok(/\*\*found:\*\* a multi-message request is billed/.test(withRun), 'and what we found instead is printed directly under it');
+  ok(/✓ we can send ONLY the new message/.test(withRun) && !/found:.*ONLY the new/.test(withRun), 'a claim that holds needs no correction under it');
+  ok(/\? a big message/.test(withRun) && /\*not measured:\* empty reply TWICE/.test(withRun), 'and an unmeasured one says so, rather than passing or failing');
+  ok(/measured: `single=19\/19@5869t multi=19\/19@433t`/.test(withRun), 'the measurement rides along, because that is the number a fix designs against');
+  ok(/against m via api\.example\.mil/.test(withRun), 'a measured document names the model and endpoint it was measured against');
+
+  // The generated FILE. Pure function of the assertions — no timestamp, no build number — which is the only reason
+  // this comparison can exist at all.
+  const doc = formatProdSpecDoc();
+  ok(/GENERATED FILE — DO NOT EDIT/.test(doc), 'the file opens with a DO-NOT-EDIT banner in the source view');
+  ok(/⚠️ \*\*This file is generated — do not edit it\.\*\*/.test(doc), 'and a visible one for anybody reading it rendered');
+  ok(/scripts\/build\.mjs/.test(doc) && /src\/agent\/prod-spec\.mjs/.test(doc), 'both saying who writes it and where to make the change instead');
+  ok(PROD_SPEC_DOC.includes('.generated.'), `and the filename itself says so (${PROD_SPEC_DOC})`);
+  ok(formatProdSpecDoc() === doc, 'the document is a pure function of the assertions — no timestamp, no build number');
+  let onDisk = null;
+  try { onDisk = await readFile(new URL(`../${PROD_SPEC_DOC}`, import.meta.url), 'utf8'); } catch {}
+  ok(onDisk !== null, `${PROD_SPEC_DOC} is committed, not just generated into thin air`);
+  // THE ENFORCEMENT. An edit to the generated file fails here, and so does forgetting to rebuild after changing a
+  // claim — a generated document nobody verifies is worse than none, because it gets trusted.
+  ok(onDisk === doc, `${PROD_SPEC_DOC} matches the assertions — if this fails, run: node scripts/build.mjs`);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[L9] native tools, per model lane — each verdict against four synthetic endpoints\n');
+{
+  const L9 = SPEC.filter((a) => a.layer === 9);
+  ok(L9.length === 43 && L9.every((a) => typeof a.plan === 'function'), `layer 9 has its forty-three rungs, each driving its own calls (${L9.map((a) => a.id).join(',')})`);
+  const r9 = (name) => byName[name];
+  const SEL = 'gemini-x-pro', WT = SEL + '-withtools', AS = SEL + '-assist';
+  const ids = [SEL, WT, AS, 'gemini-2.5-flash', 'text-embedding-004'];
+
+  // One synthetic endpoint, four temperaments. `lane(model)` says whether THIS model id honours what the docs promise.
+  //   ignores  — every tools array, tool result and response_format accepted and ignored (what 4f measured)
+  //   honours  — every lane does what the docs say
+  //   wt-only  — only the -withtools id honours them (the leading hypothesis)
+  //   rejects  — any tools / response_format field is a 400
+  const endpoint = (kind) => {
+    const sent = [];
+    const honours = (m) => kind === 'honours' || (kind === 'wt-only' && /-withtools$/.test(m || SEL));
+    const reply = (text, extra = {}) => ({ text, finish: 'stop', raw: { choices: [{ message: {} }], gemini_enterprise: { session: 's' } }, ...extra });
+    const call = async (msgs, o = {}) => {
+      const e = o?.extra || {}; const m = o?.model || SEL; const h = honours(m);
+      sent.push({ model: m, extra: e, stream: !!o.stream, roles: msgs.map((x) => x.role) });
+      const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1]; const lt = String(last?.content || '');
+      if (kind === 'rejects' && (e.tools || e.response_format)) throw new Error('HTTP 400 unknown field');
+      if (e.response_format) return reply(h ? '{"colour":"red","count":3}' : 'They are red, and there are 3 of them.');
+      if (/List every tool/.test(lt)) return reply(h && e.tools ? '["cb\\_read\\_workspace\\_file"]' : '["default_api", "transfer_to_agent"]');
+      if (last.role === 'tool') { const tok = h ? (/SPEC-\w+/.exec(lt) || [''])[0] : ''; return reply(tok ? `The token is ${tok}.` : 'I could not read that file.'); }
+      const plain = /contains: token: (SPEC-\w+)/.exec(lt);
+      if (plain) return reply(`The token is ${plain[1]}.`);
+      if (e.tools && h && e.tool_choice !== 'none') {
+        const tcs = e.tools.map((t, i) => ({ id: 'c' + i, type: 'function', function: { name: t.function.name, arguments: '{"path":"src/config.ts"}' } }));
+        const use = /Two things/.test(lt) ? tcs : tcs.slice(0, 1);
+        return o.stream
+          ? { text: '', finish: 'tool_calls', raw: {}, chunks: 4, toolFrames: 2, toolCalls: use.map((c) => ({ id: c.id, name: c.function.name, arguments: c.function.arguments })) }
+          : { text: '', finish: 'tool_calls', raw: { choices: [{ message: { tool_calls: use } }] } };
+      }
+      return reply('It uses port 8443.', { http: { headers: h ? { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-ratelimit-limit-credits-day': '2000000', 'x-ratelimit-remaining-tokens-minute': '199000' } : { 'content-type': 'application/json' } } });
+    };
+    return { call, callStream: (msgs, o = {}) => call(msgs, { ...o, stream: true }), sent };
+  };
+  const run = async (name, kind, listed = ids) => {
+    const ep = endpoint(kind);
+    const v = await r9(name).plan({ call: ep.call, callStream: ep.callStream, model: SEL, listModels: listed ? async () => ({ data: listed.map((id) => ({ id })) }) : null });
+    return { ...v, sent: ep.sent };
+  };
+
+  // the helpers the verdicts rest on
+  ok(JSON.stringify(ntToolTargets(SEL, ids).targets) === JSON.stringify([SEL, WT, AS, 'gemini-2.5-flash']), 'targets: the selected model, then its -withtools and -assist siblings, then a plain vendor id');
+  ok(ntToolTargets(SEL, [SEL]).targets.length === 1 && ntToolTargets(SEL, [SEL]).skipped.includes(WT), 'a sibling the list does not have is skipped, and said so');
+  ok(ntToolTargets(AS, [SEL, AS]).targets.join() === `${AS},${SEL}`, 'selected on the -assist lane, the plain base id is asked too');
+  {
+    // every chat model the list has is asked - five on production (the developer, 2026-10-12) - an embedding id is not
+    const five = [SEL, 'gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-pro', 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite', 'text-embedding-004', AS];
+    const tg = ntToolTargets(SEL, five);
+    ok(tg.targets.join() === [SEL, AS, 'gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-pro', 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite'].join() && tg.skipped.includes('text-embedding-004'),
+      `every listed chat model, the preferred lanes first; the embedding model left out (${tg.targets.join()})`);
+    const labels = tg.targets.map((t) => ntLabel(SEL, t));
+    ok(new Set(labels).size === labels.length, `each lane has its own label (${labels.join(' ')})`);
+  }
+  ok(ntModelIds({ data: [{ id: 'a' }, { id: 'b' }] }).join() === 'a,b' && ntModelIds({ models: ['c'] }).join() === 'c' && ntModelIds(null).length === 0, 'the model list is read from any envelope');
+  ok(ntNamesFromText('["cb\\_read\\_workspace\\_file", "default_api"]').join() === 'cb_read_workspace_file,default_api', 'names read out of an escaped JSON array');
+  ok(ntNamesFromText('I have:\n- `default_api`\n2. **google_search** — searches\n').join() === 'default_api,google_search', 'and out of a bulleted list when it ignores the JSON ask');
+
+  // 9a
+  {
+    const a = await run('the-model-list-has-no-tool-lanes', 'ignores');
+    ok(a.holds === false && /1 -withtools and 1 -assist/.test(a.note) && /wt=1 as=1 docs=1/.test(a.measured), `lanes in the list break the claim (${a.measured})`);
+    ok(a.detail.includes(WT) && a.detail.includes('text-embedding-004'), 'and every id is printed — model names are the finding, not identifiers');
+    const plain = await run('the-model-list-has-no-tool-lanes', 'ignores', [SEL, 'gemini-2.5-flash']);
+    ok(plain.holds === true, 'a list with no tool lanes holds it');
+    const none = await run('the-model-list-has-no-tool-lanes', 'ignores', null);
+    ok(none.holds === null && /cannot list/.test(none.note), 'no listModels on the adapter = not measured, never a verdict');
+  }
+  // 9b — the developer's question
+  {
+    const ig = await run('declaring-a-function-changes-nothing-it-reports', 'ignores');
+    ok(ig.holds === true && /sel:2>2-/.test(ig.measured), `ignored: ours never named, its own two stay (${ig.measured})`);
+    ok(/none\[default_api,transfer_to_agent\]/.test(ig.detail), 'the name sets are printed side by side');
+    ok(ig.sent.filter((s) => s.model === SEL).length === 6, 'two asks per arm, three arms: none, declared+none, declared+auto');
+    ok(ig.sent.some((s) => s.extra?.tool_choice === 'none') && ig.sent.some((s) => s.extra?.tool_choice === 'auto'), 'with tool_choice none and auto both tried');
+    const ho = await run('declaring-a-function-changes-nothing-it-reports', 'honours');
+    ok(ho.holds === false && /SEES our declared function/.test(ho.note) && /VANISH/.test(ho.note), `honoured: ours seen and its own vanish (${ho.note.slice(0, 80)})`);
+    const wt = await run('declaring-a-function-changes-nothing-it-reports', 'wt-only');
+    ok(wt.holds === false && /on wt \(/.test(wt.note) && /sel:2>2- wt:2>0\+/.test(wt.measured), `only -withtools sees it, and the report says which lane (${wt.measured})`);
+    const rj = await run('declaring-a-function-changes-nothing-it-reports', 'rejects');
+    ok(rj.holds === null && /err=HTTP 400/.test(rj.detail), 'a 400 on every declared arm cannot measure this — not a verdict');
+  }
+  // 9c
+  {
+    const ig = await run('a-declared-function-is-never-called', 'ignores');
+    ok(ig.holds === true && ig.measured === 'sel:NNN wt:NNN as:NNN g2.5fl:NNN', `ignored on every lane, every shape (${ig.measured})`);
+    ok(/\/ent/.test(ig.detail), 'and the Enterprise envelope is noted per call');
+    const ho = await run('a-declared-function-is-never-called', 'honours');
+    ok(ho.holds === false && /NATIVE FUNCTION CALLING WORKS on sel/.test(ho.note) && /auto\+req\+named/.test(ho.note), 'honoured: the lane and the shapes that work are named');
+    ok(/\/json/.test(ho.detail), 'with the arguments checked to be JSON');
+    const wt = await run('a-declared-function-is-never-called', 'wt-only');
+    ok(wt.holds === false && /on wt \(gemini-x-pro-withtools\)/.test(wt.note) && /sel:NNN wt:CCC/.test(wt.measured), `only on -withtools — exactly what the run would show if the hypothesis is right (${wt.measured})`);
+    ok(wt.sent.some((s) => s.extra?.tool_choice?.function?.name === 'cb_read_workspace_file'), 'the named tool_choice form is sent');
+    const rj = await run('a-declared-function-is-never-called', 'rejects');
+    ok(rj.holds === true && /rejected outright on every lane/.test(rj.detail) && /sel:EEE/.test(rj.measured), 'a 400 everywhere is a different fact, recorded as one');
+  }
+  // 9d
+  {
+    const ig = await run('a-tool-result-does-not-reach-it', 'ignores');
+    ok(ig.holds === true && /sel:NNY/.test(ig.measured), `ignored: the result never arrives, while the positive control echoes (${ig.measured})`);
+    const rt = ig.sent.find((s) => s.roles.includes('tool'));
+    ok(rt && rt.roles.join() === 'user,assistant,tool', 'the round trip is user → assistant tool_calls → role:tool, as the docs show');
+    const ho = await run('a-tool-result-does-not-reach-it', 'honours');
+    ok(ho.holds === false && /REACHES the model/.test(ho.note) && /sel:YNY/.test(ho.measured), `honoured: the marker comes back and the negative control stays clean (${ho.measured})`);
+    const wt = await run('a-tool-result-does-not-reach-it', 'wt-only');
+    ok(wt.holds === false && /on wt/.test(wt.note), 'only -withtools: named');
+    const rj = await run('a-tool-result-does-not-reach-it', 'rejects');
+    ok(rj.holds === null && /rejected a tool message/.test(rj.note), 'a 400 on the tool message is not a verdict');
+  }
+  // 9e
+  {
+    const ig = await run('streamed-tool-calls-never-arrive', 'ignores');
+    ok(ig.holds === true && /sel:N/.test(ig.measured) && ig.sent.every((s) => s.stream), `ignored: no frames, and every call streamed (${ig.measured})`);
+    const ho = await run('streamed-tool-calls-never-arrive', 'honours');
+    ok(ho.holds === false && /frames ARRIVE/.test(ho.note) && /naming our function/.test(ho.note) && /sel:F2/.test(ho.measured), `honoured (${ho.measured})`);
+    const wt = await run('streamed-tool-calls-never-arrive', 'wt-only');
+    ok(wt.holds === false && /on wt/.test(wt.note), 'only -withtools: named');
+  }
+  // 9f
+  {
+    const ig = await run('it-never-calls-two-functions-at-once', 'ignores');
+    ok(ig.holds === true && /sel:0/.test(ig.measured), `ignored (${ig.measured})`);
+    const ho = await run('it-never-calls-two-functions-at-once', 'honours');
+    ok(ho.holds === false && /BOTH calls in one reply/.test(ho.note) && /sel:2/.test(ho.measured), `honoured: two calls in one reply (${ho.measured})`);
+    ok(ho.sent[0].extra.tools.length === 2, 'two functions declared');
+  }
+  // 9g
+  {
+    const ig = await run('json-schema-output-is-ignored', 'ignores');
+    ok(ig.holds === true && /sel:P/.test(ig.measured), `ignored: prose (${ig.measured})`);
+    ok(ig.sent[0].extra.response_format?.json_schema?.strict === true, 'strict:true, as the docs show');
+    const ho = await run('json-schema-output-is-ignored', 'honours');
+    ok(ho.holds === false && /STRUCTURED OUTPUT WORKS/.test(ho.note) && /sel:J/.test(ho.measured), `honoured: exactly the schema (${ho.measured})`);
+    const rj = await run('json-schema-output-is-ignored', 'rejects');
+    ok(rj.holds === true && /sel:E/.test(rj.measured), 'rejected is recorded per lane');
+  }
+  // 9h — the budget headers (the positive claim since 2026-10-12: six headers on every production reply)
+  {
+    const ig = await run('replies-carry-the-budget-headers', 'ignores');
+    ok(ig.holds === false && ig.measured === 'rl=0 dep=0' && /nothing to read/.test(ig.note), `no headers BREAKS it - the pie would be blind (${ig.measured})`);
+    const ho = await run('replies-carry-the-budget-headers', 'honours');
+    ok(ho.holds === true && /x-ratelimit-limit-credits-day=2000000/.test(ho.detail) && ho.measured === 'rl=2 dep=0', `the limits are printed and the claim holds (${ho.measured})`);
+    const none = await r9('replies-carry-the-budget-headers').plan({ call: async () => ({ text: 'READY', raw: {} }) });
+    ok(none.holds === null, 'no headers captured at all = not measured');
+  }
+  // 9i — is anything cached (the developer, 2026-10-12)
+  {
+    ok(ntCacheOf({ prompt_tokens: 9, prompt_tokens_details: { cached_tokens: 7 } }).tokens === 7 && ntCacheOf({ prompt_tokens: 9, prompt_tokens_details: { cached_tokens: 7 } }).keys.join() === 'prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens', 'a cache field is found at any depth');
+    ok(ntCacheOf({ prompt_tokens: 9, completion_tokens: 1, total_tokens: 10 }).tokens === null && ntCacheOf(null).keys.length === 0, 'no cache field reads as null — not as 0');
+    ok(ntCacheOf({ cached_content_token_count: 0 }).tokens === 0, 'a field that says 0 reads as 0');
+    ok(JSON.stringify(ntRemaining({ 'X-RateLimit-Remaining-Credits': '1999000', 'x-ratelimit-limit-credits': '2000000', 'content-type': 'x' })) === '{"x-ratelimit-remaining-credits":1999000}', 'only the numeric remaining-budget headers are kept');
+    const d1 = ntCacheDoc('SPEC-AAAAAA'), d2 = ntCacheDoc('SPEC-BBBBBB');
+    ok(d1.startsWith('Reference SPEC-AAAAAA') && d1.length > 35000 && d1.length < 60000 && d1.slice(30) === d2.slice(30), `the opening is ~10k tokens and starts with its own marker (${d1.length} chars)`);
+
+    // A synthetic gateway per behaviour. Each lane remembers the openings it has seen: a repeat is "cached".
+    const gw = ({ field, faster, billed }) => {
+      const seen = new Set(); let left = 2000000, inflight = 0, maxIn = 0; const sent = [];
+      const call = async (msgs, o = {}) => {
+        inflight++; maxIn = Math.max(maxIn, inflight);
+        try {
+          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1));
+          const m = o?.model || SEL; const c = String(msgs[0].content); const key = m + (/Reference (SPEC-\w+)/.exec(c) || [])[1];
+          sent.push({ model: m, n: msgs.length, head: c.slice(0, 16) });
+          const warm = seen.has(key); seen.add(key);
+          const cached = warm && field === 'reports' ? 9000 : 0;
+          left -= billed && warm ? 1000 : 10000;
+          return { text: 'READY', finish: 'stop', ms: warm && faster ? 1500 : 4000, raw: {},
+            usage: { prompt_tokens: 10000, completion_tokens: 1, ...(field ? { prompt_tokens_details: { cached_tokens: cached } } : {}) },
+            http: { headers: { 'x-ratelimit-remaining-tokens-minute': String(left) } } };
+        } finally { inflight--; }
+      };
+      return { call, sent, maxIn: () => maxIn };
+    };
+    const run9i = async (opts) => { const g = gw(opts); const v = await r9('a-repeated-opening-is-not-cached').plan({ call: g.call, model: SEL, listModels: async () => ({ data: ids.map((id) => ({ id })) }) }); return { ...v, g }; };
+    const none = await run9i({});
+    ok(none.holds === true && none.measured === 'sel:-1.0 wt:-1.0 as:-1.0 g2.5fl:-1.0', `no cache field and no speed-up holds it (${none.measured})`);
+    ok(none.g.sent.length === 20 && none.g.sent.every((s) => s.n === 1 && s.head.startsWith('Reference SPEC-')), 'five calls a lane, each ONE message opening on its marker (1n: only the last message is billed)');
+    ok(none.g.maxIn() === 1, 'one call at a time - five lanes at once would trip the token limit');
+    ok(/order A1 A2 B1 B2 A3/.test(none.detail) && /sel ms=4000\/4000\/4000\/4000\/4000 p=10000/.test(none.detail), 'the five timings and prompt sizes are printed per lane');
+    ok(/cost tokens-minute:10000\/10000\/10000\/10000/.test(none.detail), 'and what each call cost, from the remaining-budget header');
+    const rep = await run9i({ field: 'reports', faster: true });
+    ok(rep.holds === false && /IS reported cached on sel/.test(rep.note) && /^sel:R0\.4/.test(rep.measured), `a lane that reports a cached repeat breaks it (${rep.measured})`);
+    ok(/cached=0\/9000\/0\/9000\/9000/.test(rep.detail), 'with the cached tokens per call: the repeats only');
+    const zero = await run9i({ field: 'zero' });
+    ok(zero.holds === true && /^sel:r1\.0/.test(zero.measured), `a cache field that stays at 0 is told apart from no field (${zero.measured})`);
+    const fast = await run9i({ faster: true });
+    ok(fast.holds === false && /no cache field, but repeats came back in 0\.4x/.test(fast.note), 'no field, but repeats much faster: said, with the caveat');
+    const cheap = await run9i({ billed: true });
+    ok(/cost tokens-minute:1000\/10000\/1000\/1000/.test(cheap.detail), `a repeat billed for less shows as a smaller drop (${(/cost [^|]*/.exec(cheap.detail) || [''])[0]})`);
+    ok(r9('a-repeated-opening-is-not-cached').solo === true, 'and it runs alone, so its cost column is its own calls\' cost');
+  }
+  // 9j — a growing conversation against a cold control, and what a credit is
+  {
+    ok(Math.abs(ntSlope([{ x: 0, y: 1 }, { x: 10, y: 21 }, { x: 20, y: 41 }]) - 2) < 1e-9 && ntSlope([{ x: 5, y: 1 }]) === null, 'the slope helper');
+    ok(ntCacheDoc('SPEC-AAAAAA', 832).startsWith(ntCacheDoc('SPEC-AAAAAA', 416).slice(0, -1)), 'a bigger opening starts with the smaller one, word for word - each growing request contains the last');
+    // A synthetic gateway. Every request costs 10 credits a prompt token and 40 a completion token, and takes
+    // 1 ms per 10 tokens on top of a base — except the part of a request this lane has seen before, when it caches.
+    const gw9j = ({ cacheCost, cacheTime, field, noCost }) => {
+      let left = 1e9; const seen = []; const sent = [];
+      const call = async (msgs) => {
+        const t = String(msgs[0].content); sent.push(t.length);
+        const p = Math.round(t.length / 4), c = 30;
+        const prev = seen.filter((s) => t.startsWith(s.replace(/\n\nIgnore[\s\S]*$/, ''))).map((s) => Math.round(s.replace(/\n\nIgnore[\s\S]*$/, '').length / 4)).sort((a, b) => b - a)[0] || 0;
+        seen.push(t);
+        const reused = Math.min(prev, p);
+        left -= (cacheCost ? (p - reused) * 10 + reused * 1 : p * 10) + c * 40;
+        const ms = 1500 + Math.round((cacheTime ? p - reused : p) / 10);
+        return { text: 'READY', ms, raw: {}, usage: { prompt_tokens: p, completion_tokens: c, ...(field ? { prompt_tokens_details: { cached_tokens: reused } } : {}) }, http: { headers: noCost ? {} : { 'x-ratelimit-remaining-credits-day': String(left) } } };
+      };
+      return { call, sent };
+    };
+    const run9j = async (o) => { const g = gw9j(o); const v = await r9('a-growing-conversation-is-not-cached').plan({ call: g.call, model: SEL }); return { ...v, g }; };
+    const plain = await run9j({});
+    ok(plain.holds === true && /slope G\/C=1\.00/.test(plain.measured), `nothing cached: both arms climb together, the claim holds (${plain.measured})`);
+    ok(/fit in=10 out=40/.test(plain.measured), `the fit recovers what a credit is: 10 a prompt token, 40 a completion token (${plain.measured})`);
+    ok(plain.g.sent.length === 12 && /header ran 0 call\(s\) behind/.test(plain.detail) &&/G208=\d+\/\d+\/30\/\d+ C208=/.test(plain.detail), 'one budget read, then G and C interleaved at four sizes, each call\'s ms/prompt/completion/credits printed');
+    const billed = await run9j({ cacheCost: true });
+    ok(billed.holds === false && /BILLED for less/.test(billed.note), `a reused prefix billed cheaper breaks it (${billed.measured})`);
+    // BILLING IS DECISIVE (2026-10-12): answered faster but billed the same is noise on a thinking model, not a cache
+    const timed = await run9j({ cacheTime: true });
+    ok(timed.holds === true && /billed the same/.test(timed.detail) && !timed.note, `a reused prefix answered faster but billed the same HOLDS, saying so (${(/time grew[^|]*/.exec(timed.detail) || [''])[0]})`);
+    const timedOnly = await run9j({ cacheTime: true, noCost: true });
+    ok(timedOnly.holds === false && /time grows/.test(timedOnly.note), `with no cost to read, time alone still breaks it, as the weaker signal (${timedOnly.measured})`);
+    const said = await run9j({ cacheCost: true, field: true });
+    ok(said.holds === false && /REPORTED cached/.test(said.note) && / CACHED$/.test(said.measured), 'a cache field above zero on the growing arm is said first');
+    ok(r9('a-growing-conversation-is-not-cached').solo === true, 'it runs alone too');
+  }
+  // 9k — the double call: a second call pinned to the first one's handle (the developer, 2026-10-12)
+  {
+    // A gateway with a handle. The opening's reply carries gemini_enterprise; a call that sends it back has the
+    // opening's transcript (unless it `forgets`) and is billed for its own message alone (unless it `rebills`).
+    const gw9k = ({ forgets, rebills, noHandle, lag = 0, lastOnly, masks, deaf, seenFree, thin, masksAnswers, frozen } = {}) => {
+      let left = 1e9; const hist = [1e9]; const memory = new Map(); let n = 0; const sent = []; const had = new Set();
+      const call = async (msgs, opts) => {
+        const env = opts?.extra?.gemini_enterprise;
+        const text = msgs.map((m) => String(m.content)).join('\n');
+        const own = Math.round(String(msgs[msgs.length - 1].content).length / 4), all = Math.round(text.length / 4);
+        // a `thin` session keeps a summary of what came before: the marker and the file, not the records
+        const known = env ? (thin ? (memory.get(env.session) || '').split('\n').filter((l) => !/^Record \d+:/.test(l)).join('\n') : memory.get(env.session) || '') : '';
+        // a gateway that `rebills` charges a pinned call for its transcript AND the new message, as a resend would
+        // `seenFree`: an earlier message is billed only the first time the gateway is sent it
+        const unseen = own + msgs.slice(0, -1).reduce((t, m) => t + (had.has(String(m.content)) ? 0 : Math.round(String(m.content).length / 4)), 0);
+        for (const m of msgs) had.add(String(m.content));
+        const p = env ? (rebills ? Math.round(known.length / 4) + own : own) : (seenFree ? unseen : lastOnly && msgs.length > 1 ? own : all);
+        sent.push({ pinned: !!env, msgs: msgs.length, p });
+        left -= p * 10 + 30 * 40; hist.push(left);
+        const shown = frozen ? hist[0] : hist[Math.max(0, hist.length - 1 - lag)];   // a header that runs `lag` calls behind
+        // a `deaf` gateway reads only the last message of a request
+        const seen = (env && !forgets ? known + '\n' : '') + (deaf ? String(msgs[msgs.length - 1].content) : text);
+        const id = env ? env.session : 's' + (++n);
+        memory.set(id, seen);
+        const mk = /deployment marker is (SPEC-\w+)/.exec(seen), port = /PORT = (\d+)/.exec(seen);
+        // `masks`: a masking plugin hid the marker on the way out, so the model answers its token - # backslash-escaped
+        const mkOut = mk ? (masks ? 'SPEC-\\#' + mk[1].slice(5).replace(/[0-9]/g, '0').replace(/[A-Z]/g, 'X') + '\\#abc1234' : mk[1]) : 'NONE';
+        const lastQ = String(msgs[msgs.length - 1].content);
+        const recQ = /record (\d+) hold/.exec(lastQ), rec = recQ && new RegExp(`Record ${recQ[1]}: .*? holds (\\d+) units`).exec(seen);
+        // 9k's lookups: a choice of letters per record - answered from what this gateway has SEEN, '?' when it has not
+        const letters = [...lastQ.matchAll(/lookup, the units that record (\d+) holds: ([^\n]+)/g)].map((m) => {
+          const has = new RegExp(`Record ${m[1]}: .*? holds (\\d+) units`).exec(seen);
+          const opt = has && [...m[2].matchAll(/write ([a-z]+) for (\d+)/g)].find((o) => o[2] === has[1]);
+          const w = opt ? opt[1] : 'engine';   // a summary guesses
+          return masksAnswers ? '\\#X\\#abc' + (w.length + 1000) : w;   // a box that masks the option names too
+        });
+        const said = [/What is the deployment marker/.test(lastQ) ? mkOut : null, /what port/.test(lastQ) ? (port ? port[1] : 'NONE') : null, recQ ? (rec ? rec[1] : 'NONE') : null, letters.length ? 'answers: ' + letters.join(' ') : null].filter((x) => x != null);
+        const reply = said.length ? said.join(' ') : /READY/.test(lastQ) ? 'READY' : '0';
+        return { text: reply, ms: 1500, raw: noHandle ? {} : { gemini_enterprise: { session: id, assist_token: 't' + (++n) } }, usage: { prompt_tokens: p, completion_tokens: 30 }, http: { headers: { 'x-ratelimit-remaining-credits-day': String(shown), 'x-ratelimit-remaining-tokens-minute': String(1e6 - p) } } };
+      };
+      return { call, sent };
+    };
+    const run9k = async (o) => { const g = gw9k(o); const v = await r9('a-pinned-second-call-is-billed-for-the-whole-conversation').plan({ call: g.call, model: SEL }); return { ...v, g }; };
+    const real = await run9k();
+    ok(real.holds === false && /ride the first one's handle/.test(real.note), `cheap AND it still has the marker and the file: the double call can pin (${real.measured})`);
+    ok(real.g.sent.length === 8 && real.g.sent[3].pinned && real.g.sent[3].msgs === 1 && !real.g.sent[4].pinned && real.g.sent[4].msgs === 3, 'a budget read, a calibration call, the opening, ONE pinned message, the whole conversation resent as the control, three settling reads');
+    ok(/credits opening=\d+ pinned=\d+ resent=\d+ lag=0 \| pinned recall marker=Y file=Y record=Y \| resent marker=Y file=Y record=Y/.test(real.measured), `every cost and both recalls are printed (${real.measured})`);
+    const thinned = await run9k({ thin: true });
+    ok(thinned.holds === false && /pinned recall marker=Y file=Y record=N/.test(thinned.measured) && /NOT a record from the middle/.test(thinned.note) && /for what a summary is worth/.test(thinned.note), `a session that summarised the opening keeps the marker and loses the record - said (${thinned.measured})`);
+    const maskedAns = await run9k({ masksAnswers: true });
+    ok(/pinned recall marker=Y file=Y record=MASKED/.test(maskedAns.measured), `an answer that comes back as tokens is reported as MASKED, never as not recalled (${maskedAns.measured})`);
+    const frozenHdr = await run9k({ frozen: true });
+    ok(frozenHdr.holds === null && /the budget header did not move once/.test(frozenHdr.note) && /What the follow-up on the handle still had: marker=Y file=Y middle-record=Y/.test(frozenHdr.note), `a budget header that never moves leaves the cost unknown, and what the follow-up had is still said (${frozenHdr.note.slice(0, 120)})`);
+    const forgot = await run9k({ forgets: true });
+    ok(forgot.holds === true && /conversation going missing/.test(forgot.note) && /marker=N file=N/.test(forgot.measured), `cheap WITHOUT recall holds the claim and says why (${forgot.measured})`);
+    const rebilled = await run9k({ rebills: true });
+    ok(rebilled.holds === true && /saves nothing/.test(rebilled.note), `billed the same as resending holds it (${rebilled.measured})`);
+    // THE HEADER LAGS (probe #118): each reply's budget is two calls stale. The costs must still land on their calls.
+    const late = await run9k({ lag: 2 });
+    const cr = (v) => /credits opening=(\d+) pinned=(\d+) resent=(\d+) lag=(\d+)/.exec(v.measured).slice(1).map(Number);
+    ok(late.holds === false && cr(late)[3] === 2 && cr(late).slice(0, 3).join() === cr(real).slice(0, 3).join(), `a header two calls behind gives the same three costs, and says lag=2 (${late.measured})`);
+    ok(/each 2 call\(s\) late: /.test(late.detail), 'and the raw drops are printed in call order');
+    const lateRebilled = await run9k({ lag: 2, rebills: true });
+    ok(lateRebilled.holds === true && /saves nothing/.test(lateRebilled.note), 'the lagged header still tells a rebilled pin from a cheap one');
+    {
+      const L = ntLagCosts([1000, 1000, 992, 992 - 5675, 992 - 5675 - 11295, null], [null, 5675, 11295, null, null, null]);
+      ok(L.lag === 2 && L.costs[1] === 5675 && L.costs[2] === 11295 && L.costs[3] == null, 'ntLagCosts: the lag is where drop/tokens agrees across the sized calls; a cost past the end of the series is unknown');
+      ok(ntLagCosts([100, 90, 80], [null, null, null]).lag === 0, 'no sized call: no lag assumed');
+      // the second #118 run's 9k: one behind, then two quick calls outran the header (38/0/38/8)
+      const dr = [8, 5684, 11370, 38, 0, 38, 8];
+      const lefts = [100000]; for (const d of dr) lefts.push(lefts[lefts.length - 1] - d);
+      const S = ntLagCosts(lefts, [null, 5682, 11370, null, null, null, null, null]);
+      // the third run: two sized calls folded into one drop, either side of it
+      const M1 = ntLagCosts([200000, 200000, 199992, 199992 - 17046, 199992 - 17046, 199992 - 17082], [null, 5676, 11364, null, null, null]);
+      ok(M1.merged === 1 && Math.abs(M1.costs[1] - 5680) <= 6 && Math.abs(M1.costs[2] - 11374) <= 6, `ntLagCosts: 8 then 17046 for calls of 5676 and 11364 is one drop for both, split by size (${M1.costs[1]}/${M1.costs[2]})`);
+      const md = [14120, 8, 5683, 11303, 68324, 0, 4];
+      const ml = [900000]; for (const d of md) ml.push(ml[ml.length - 1] - d);
+      const M2 = ntLagCosts(ml, [null, 5683, 11303, 22573, 45751, null, null, null]);
+      ok(M2.lag === 2 && M2.merged === 1 && M2.costs[1] === 5683 && M2.costs[2] === 11303 && M2.costs[3] === 22573 && M2.costs[4] === 45751, `ntLagCosts: 68324 then 0 is two calls in one drop (${M2.costs.slice(1, 5).join('/')})`);
+      ok(S.lag === 1 && S.costs[2] === 11370 && S.costs[3] === 38 && S.costs[4] === 38 && S.costs[5] === 8 && S.stalls === 1, 'ntLagCosts: a reply that shows no drop is the header standing still, not a free call - the next drop is the next call\'s');
+    }
+    // THE MARKER COMES BACK MASKED (a masking plugin hid it on the way out; the endpoint backslash-escapes the #)
+    const masked = await run9k({ masks: true });
+    ok(masked.holds === false && /pinned recall marker=Y file=Y record=Y \| resent marker=Y/.test(masked.measured) && /masked token on both arms/.test(masked.detail), `a marker answered as its token still counts as recalled (${masked.measured})`);
+    // THE CONTROL IS CHEAP ITSELF: a multi-message request billed for its last message alone
+    const lastOnly = await run9k({ lastOnly: true });
+    ok(lastOnly.holds === true && /billed for its last message alone/.test(lastOnly.note) && /still had the marker and the file/.test(lastOnly.note), `a resend billed for its last message alone is said: nothing for pinning to save (${lastOnly.measured})`);
+    const bare = await run9k({ noHandle: true });
+    ok(bare.holds === null && bare.measured === 'no-handle', 'no handle from the opening = not measured');
+    ok(r9('a-pinned-second-call-is-billed-for-the-whole-conversation').solo === true, 'it runs alone: its cost column is the budget header\'s drop');
+    // 9l — the same question with history NOBODY has sent: a cache, or is only the last message ever billed?
+    const run9l = async (o) => { const g = gw9k(o); const v = await r9('earlier-messages-never-sent-before-are-billed').plan({ call: g.call, model: SEL }); return { ...v, g }; };
+    const billed = await run9l();
+    ok(billed.holds === true && /paid for once/.test(billed.note), `new history billed in full holds it (${billed.measured})`);
+    ok(billed.g.sent.length === 10 && billed.g.sent[3].msgs === 3 && billed.g.sent[4].msgs === 5 && billed.g.sent[5].msgs === 3 && billed.g.sent[6].msgs === 3 && billed.g.sent.every((s) => !s.pinned), 'a budget read, two calibration calls, the fresh three-message request, it extended by a turn, it with one record changed, a big fresh one, three settling reads');
+    ok(/recall marker=Y file=Y record=Y \| extended=\d+ record=Y \| edited=\d+ read=new \| big=\d+ of \d+ marker=Y/.test(billed.measured), `the middle record, the extended turn, the edit and the big history are all printed (${billed.measured})`);
+    ok(/tokens-minute drops in the same order \(R = the window refilled\): [\dR/]+ \|/.test(billed.detail), 'the per-minute token header is read off the same calls');
+    // history already sent is free, new history is not: fresh and edited in full, the extended turn for its new part
+    const seen = await run9l({ seenFree: true });
+    const c9l = (v) => /credits fresh=(\d+) .*extended=(\d+) .*edited=(\d+) .*big=(\d+) of (\d+)/.exec(v.measured).slice(1).map(Number);
+    ok(seen.holds === true && c9l(seen)[1] < c9l(seen)[0] * 0.2 && c9l(seen)[2] > c9l(seen)[0] * 0.8, `seen history free: the extended turn is cheap, one changed record is billed again in full (${seen.measured})`);
+    const never = await run9l({ lastOnly: true });
+    ok(never.holds === false && /seen before or not/.test(never.note) && /recall marker=Y file=Y/.test(never.measured), `new history billed for the question alone, and read: only the last message is ever billed (${never.measured})`);
+    ok(/read exactly/.test(never.note) && c9l(never)[3] < c9l(never)[4] * 0.1, 'and the middle record and the big history are said');
+    const neverLate = await run9l({ lastOnly: true, lag: 2 });
+    ok(neverLate.holds === false && /lag=2/.test(neverLate.measured) && /credits fresh=(\d+)/.exec(neverLate.measured)[1] === /credits fresh=(\d+)/.exec(never.measured)[1], 'a header two calls behind gives the same cost');
+    const deaf = await run9l({ lastOnly: true, deaf: true });
+    ok(deaf.holds === false && /neither billed nor read/.test(deaf.note), `cheap because the earlier messages were dropped is said as that (${deaf.measured})`);
+    ok(r9('earlier-messages-never-sent-before-are-billed').solo === true, 'it runs alone too');
+  }
+  // 9m-9q — WHAT THE SESSION KEEPS, AND IN WHAT FORM (the developer, 2026-10-13)
+  {
+    // A gateway with sessions that keep each message it was sent, and each reply. `exactBack`: how many messages back
+    // a message is still held word for word (older ones lose their Record/Entry lines - a summary keeps the gist);
+    // `stale`: a restated value does not replace the first; `blind`: it will not describe what it holds;
+    // `ownExact`: whether its own replies are held; `sizeBound`: a big message in front pushes the one before it out.
+    const gwS = ({ exactBack = 1, stale = false, blind = false, ownExact = true, sizeBound = false } = {}) => {
+      const sessions = new Map(); let n = 0; const sent = [];
+      const WORDS12 = ['harbour', 'lantern', 'cabinet', 'orchard', 'pebbles', 'thunder', 'blanket', 'compass', 'feather', 'granite', 'journey', 'kitchen'];
+      const call = async (msgs, opts) => {
+        const env = opts?.extra?.gemini_enterprise;
+        const id = env ? env.session : 's' + (++n);
+        const hist = sessions.get(id) || []; sessions.set(id, hist);
+        const q = String(msgs[msgs.length - 1].content);
+        sent.push({ pinned: !!env, chars: q.length });
+        // what it can still read exactly: the current message, and earlier ones within reach
+        const thin = (t) => t.split('\n').filter((l) => !/^(Record|Entry) \d+:/.test(l)).join('\n');
+        const big = (t) => t.length > 8000;
+        const seenUser = hist.filter((h) => h.role === 'user').map((h, i, arr) => { const back = arr.length - i; const pushed = sizeBound && big(q) && back === 1; return back <= exactBack && !pushed ? h.text : thin(h.text); });
+        const view = [...seenUser, q].join('\n');
+        let reply = '0';
+        const looks = [...q.matchAll(/the units that (record|entry) (\d+) holds: ([^\n]+)/g)];
+        if (looks.length) {
+          const words = looks.map((m) => { const has = new RegExp(`^${m[1] === 'record' ? 'Record' : 'Entry'} ${m[2]}: .*? holds (\\d+) units`, 'm').exec(view); const opt = has && [...m[3].matchAll(/write ([a-z]+) for (\d+)/g)].find((o) => o[2] === has[1]); return opt ? opt[1] : 'engine'; });
+          const mk = /deployment marker is (SPEC-\w+)/.exec(hist.map((h) => h.text).join('\n'));
+          reply = `${/What is the deployment marker/.test(q) ? (mk ? mk[1] : 'NONE') + ' ' : ''}answers: ${words.join(' ')}`;
+        } else if (/Make up a list/.test(q)) reply = ['table', 'window', 'pencil', 'candle', 'ladder', 'bottle', 'carpet', 'engine'].map((l, i) => `${l}: ${WORDS12[i]}`).join('\n');
+        else if (/which word did you put after the label ([a-z]+), and which after the label ([a-z]+)/.test(q)) {
+          const L8 = ['table', 'window', 'pencil', 'candle', 'ladder', 'bottle', 'carpet', 'engine'];
+          const m = /after the label ([a-z]+), and which after the label ([a-z]+)/.exec(q);
+          reply = ownExact ? `answers: ${WORDS12[L8.indexOf(m[1])]} ${WORDS12[L8.indexOf(m[2])]}` : 'answers: something else';
+        } else if (/What word is written on the shelf sign/.test(q)) {
+          const all = hist.filter((h) => h.role === 'user').map((h) => /shelf sign is (?:now )?([a-z]+)/.exec(h.text)).filter(Boolean).map((m) => m[1]);
+          reply = stale ? `now ${all[0]}, before none` : `now ${all[all.length - 1]}, before ${all.length > 1 ? all[all.length - 2] : 'none'}`;
+        } else if (/what you actually have in front of you/.test(q)) {
+          const u = hist.filter((h) => h.role === 'user');
+          reply = blind ? 'I do not have access to earlier messages.' : `I have the full text of each. First: "${u[0].text.split(/\s+/).slice(0, 12).join(' ')}". Second: "${u[1].text}"`;
+        }
+        hist.push({ role: 'user', text: q }, { role: 'assistant', text: reply });
+        return { text: reply, ms: 900, raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: id, assist_token: 't' + (++n) } }, usage: { prompt_tokens: 10, completion_tokens: 5 } };
+      };
+      return { call, sent };
+    };
+    const runS = async (name, o) => { const g = gwS(o); const v = await r9(name).plan({ call: g.call, model: SEL }); return { ...v, g }; };
+    const M = 'a-lookup-in-the-opening-is-exact-only-one-message-back';
+    const oneBack = await runS(M);
+    ok(oneBack.holds === true && /exact at 1\.\.5 back=YNNNN \| marker=YYYYY/.test(oneBack.measured) && /One follow-up per full send/.test(oneBack.note) && /a thing stated by name, still came back/.test(oneBack.note), `9m: exact one message back and no further holds the claim, and says the named marker still came back (${oneBack.measured})`);
+    ok(oneBack.g.sent.length === 6 && oneBack.g.sent.slice(1).every((s) => s.pinned), 'an opening, then five follow-ups on its handle');
+    const deep = await runS(M, { exactBack: 3 });
+    ok(deep.holds === false && /=YYYNN/.test(deep.measured) && /still exact 3 messages back/.test(deep.note), `exact three back breaks it: more than one cheap follow-up (${deep.measured})`);
+    const none = await runS(M, { exactBack: 0 });
+    ok(none.holds === null && /not exact this time/.test(none.note), 'not exact even one back concludes nothing about further back');
+    const N = 'a-value-restated-later-replaces-the-earlier-one';
+    const repl = await runS(N);
+    ok(repl.holds === true && /at once=candle\/window three on=candle\/window/.test(repl.measured) && /both are kept, in order/.test(repl.note), `9n: the later value is current and the earlier one is still held (${repl.measured})`);
+    const old = await runS(N, { stale: true });
+    ok(old.holds === false && /gave the OLD value as current/.test(old.note), `the first value given as current breaks it: it cannot hold changing state (${old.measured})`);
+    const O = 'the-model-can-say-what-it-holds-of-earlier-messages';
+    const tells = await runS(O);
+    ok(tells.holds === true && /quoted first=Y second=Y/.test(tells.measured) && /it says: full/.test(tells.measured) && /what it said \(start\): I have the full text/.test(tells.detail), `9o: its own account, with both quotes checked against what was sent (${tells.measured})`);
+    const wont = await runS(O, { blind: true });
+    ok(wont.holds === false && /will not, or cannot, look/.test(wont.note), 'a refusal to look is said as that');
+    const P = 'the-models-own-reply-is-held-exactly';
+    const own = await runS(P);
+    ok(own.holds === true && /own words exact at 1\.\.3 back=YYY \| list=8\/8/.test(own.measured) && /held as exactly as what we send/.test(own.note), `9p: its own words come back (${own.measured})`);
+    const notOwn = await runS(P, { ownExact: false });
+    ok(notOwn.holds === false && /of OUR message, not of its reply/.test(notOwn.note), 'and when they do not, the word-for-word copy is ours alone');
+    const Q = 'a-big-follow-up-pushes-the-opening-out';
+    const rides = await runS(Q, { exactBack: 2 });
+    ok(rides.holds === false && /with the big follow-up: opening=Y itself=Y \| one later: opening=Y big-follow-up=Y/.test(rides.measured) && /can ride the handle without costing the opening/.test(rides.note), `9q: a big follow-up that still finds the opening breaks the claim (${rides.measured})`);
+    const pushed = await runS(Q, { sizeBound: true });
+    ok(pushed.holds === true && /opening=N itself=Y/.test(pushed.measured) && /only a SMALL follow-up can ride the handle/.test(pushed.note), `one that pushes it out holds it (${pushed.measured})`);
+    ok(rides.g.sent[1].chars > 20000, `the big follow-up really is as big as the opening (${rides.g.sent[1].chars} chars)`);
+    // PRE-FLIGHT, kept as a test: every word these rungs depend on, in the sentences they send, goes through the
+    // SHIPPED masker unmasked. Three runs on the box were lost to a number, a letter and "word = value" being masked.
+    const { createShapeMasker } = await import('../examples/plugins/piishape__1.mjs');
+    const masker = createShapeMasker({});
+    const outgoing = [];
+    const capture = async (msgs) => { outgoing.push(String(msgs[msgs.length - 1].content)); return { text: /Make up a list/.test(msgs[0].content) ? Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => `item ${i + 1}: lantern`).join('\n') : 'x', raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: 's', assist_token: 't' } } }; };
+    for (const nm of [M, N, O, P, Q]) await r9(nm).plan({ call: capture, model: SEL });
+    const NEED = ['table', 'window', 'pencil', 'candle', 'ladder', 'bottle', 'carpet', 'engine', 'answers', 'record', 'entry', 'item', 'shelf', 'sign', 'now', 'before', 'write', 'units', 'holds', 'first', 'second', 'message', 'summarized', 'full'];
+    const hiddenNeed = new Set();
+    for (const t of outgoing) for (const r of masker.analyze(t.replace(/^(Reference|A second set)[\s\S]*?\n\n/, '').slice(0, 5000), {}).runs) if (!r.show && NEED.includes(r.t)) hiddenNeed.add(r.t);
+    ok(outgoing.length >= 20 && hiddenNeed.size === 0, `none of the words these rungs turn on is masked by the shipped masker in what they send${hiddenNeed.size ? ' - MASKED: ' + [...hiddenNeed].join(', ') : ''} (${outgoing.length} messages)`);
+  }
+  // 9r-9ad — the twelve from the probe-ideas workflow (2026-10-13)
+  {
+    // A cooperative gateway: sessions that hold everything sent on them, word for word, and a model that follows the
+    // tool protocol. Switches turn one behaviour off at a time. `leaky`: a send with no handle can still see the
+    // last session; `invents`: a key it does not hold gets a made-up answer; `noBare`: a result with no turn
+    // headings around it gets prose instead of a tool call; `forgetsRule`; `staleRefused`; `thinks`.
+    const gwT = ({ leaky = false, invents = false, noBare = false, forgetsRule = false, staleRefused = false, thinks = false, rewrites = false, idleDead = false } = {}) => {
+      const sessions = new Map(); let n = 0, lastId = null; const sent = [];
+      const used = new Set();
+      const call = async (msgs, opts) => {
+        const env = opts?.extra?.gemini_enterprise;
+        const q = String(msgs[msgs.length - 1].content);
+        if (env && staleRefused && env.assist_token && used.has(env.assist_token)) return { err: 'HTTP 400: this handle was already used' };
+        if (env && idleDead && sessions.get(env.session)?.planted) return { err: 'HTTP 404: session expired' };
+        if (env && env.assist_token) used.add(env.assist_token);
+        const id = env ? env.session : 's' + (++n);
+        const hist = sessions.get(id) || { texts: [], planted: false }; sessions.set(id, hist);
+        const prior = [...hist.texts, ...(!env && leaky && lastId && sessions.get(lastId) ? sessions.get(lastId).texts : [])].join('\n');
+        const view = prior + '\n' + q;
+        sent.push({ pinned: !!env, effort: opts?.extra?.reasoning_effort || null });
+        const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{3,8}) ---/.exec(view) || [])[1];
+        const close = tag ? `\n/--- you ${tag} ---` : '';
+        const block = (js) => `<remote-workspace>\n<js>${js}</js>\n</remote-workspace>${close}`;
+        const framed = /--- developer [0-9a-z]{3,8} ---[\s\S]*remote-workspace-result/.test(q) || /=== THE CONVERSATION SO FAR/.test(q);
+        const door = (key) => (new RegExp(`For the ${key} task the door is ([a-z]+)(?: and the check is ([a-z]+))?`).exec(view) || null);
+        let reply = '0', usage = { prompt_tokens: Math.round(q.length / 4), completion_tokens: 2, total_tokens: Math.round(q.length / 4) + 2 };
+        let m;
+        if (/Which object is fourth from the left/.test(q)) { reply = 'engine'; if (thinks) { const c = opts?.extra?.reasoning_effort === 'low' ? 60 : 900; usage = { prompt_tokens: 200, completion_tokens: c, total_tokens: 200 + c }; } }
+        else if (/Reply with only the word candle/.test(q)) reply = 'candle';
+        else if ((m = /what is the door for the ([a-z]+) task, and for the ([a-z]+) task/.exec(q))) { const a = door(m[1]), b = door(m[2]); reply = a && b ? `answers: ${a[1]} ${b[1]}` : invents ? 'answers: table window' : 'answers: nothing nothing'; }
+        else if ((m = /For the ([a-z]+) task: what is the door, and what is the check/.exec(q))) { const a = door(m[1]); reply = a ? `answers: ${a[1]} ${a[2]}` : invents ? 'answers: table window' : 'nothing'; }
+        else if ((m = /For the ([a-z]+) task, the ([a-z]+) task and the ([a-z]+) task: what is the door/.exec(q))) reply = 'answers: ' + [m[1], m[2], m[3]].map((k) => (door(k) || [, 'nothing'])[1]).join(' ');
+        else if (/Was a word for today given after my first message/.test(q)) reply = 'answers: ' + ((/A word for today is ([a-z]+)/.exec(prior) || [, 'none'])[1]);
+        else if (/Which words for today were you given after bottle/.test(q)) reply = 'answers: ' + ([...prior.matchAll(/Another word for today is ([a-z]+)/g)].map((x) => x[1]).join(' ') || 'none');
+        else if (/What was the first word for today/.test(q)) reply = 'answers: ' + ((/A word for today is ([a-z]+)/.exec(prior) || [, 'none'])[1]);
+        else if (/Four questions about the tasks above/.test(q)) {
+          const names = /In the ([a-z]+) task, which object did we decide to keep\? \(two\) In the file read during the ([a-z]+) task[\s\S]*?\(four\) Which object is kept in the ([a-z]+) storeroom now/.exec(q);
+          const keptIn = (nm) => { const all = [...q.matchAll(new RegExp(`(?:Decided: the|the) ([a-z]+) is kept there(?= instead|\\.)|We decided to keep the ([a-z]+) in the ${nm} storeroom`, 'g'))]; const sec = q.split(new RegExp(`Task ${nm}[ :(]`))[1] || ''; const chunk = sec.split(/\n\nTask /)[0]; const c = /the ([a-z]+) is kept there instead/.exec(chunk) || /Decided: the ([a-z]+) is kept there/.exec(chunk) || /We decided to keep the ([a-z]+) in the/.exec(chunk); return c ? c[1] : 'unknown'; };
+          const key = (() => { const sec = q.split(new RegExp(`Task ${names[2]}[ :(]`))[1] || ''; const c = /the spare key is under the ([a-z]+)/.exec(sec.split(/\n\nTask /)[0]); return c ? c[1] : (invents ? 'zebra' : 'unknown'); })();
+          const changed = (() => { for (const s of q.split(/\n\n(?=Task )/)) if (/file called bottle was changed/.test(s) && !/No file was changed/.test(s.split('file called bottle')[0].slice(-40))) { const nm = /^Task ([a-z]+)/.exec(s); if (nm) return nm[1]; } return 'unknown'; })();
+          reply = `answers: ${keptIn(names[1])} ${key} ${changed} ${keptIn(names[3])}`;
+        }
+        else if (/recallTask\(name\) returns/.test(q) && !/Answer with one word[^\n]*\n[\s\S]*remote-workspace-result/.test(q)) {
+          const need = /In the file read during the ([a-z]+) task, the spare key/.exec(q), has = /In the ([a-z]+) task, which object did we decide to keep/.exec(q);
+          if (need) reply = invents ? 'window' + close : block(`return await recallTask("${need[1]}");`);
+          else if (has) reply = ((new RegExp(`Task ${has[1]} \\(finished, summary\\)[^\\n]*Decided: the ([a-z]+) is kept`).exec(q) || [, 'unknown'])[1]) + close;
+          else reply = 'unknown' + close;
+        }
+        else if (/Answer with one word[^\n]*\n[\s\S]*remote-workspace-result/.test(q)) reply = ((/the spare key is under the ([a-z]+)/.exec(q.slice(q.lastIndexOf('<remote-workspace-result'))) || [, 'unknown'])[1]) + close;
+        else if (/Write the summary of the task that was just finished/.test(q) || /end your reply with a block of exactly three lines/.test(q)) {
+          const f = /We changed three files: the ones called ([a-z]+), ([a-z]+) and ([a-z]+)/.exec(view), k = /keep the retry in the ([a-z]+) module/.exec(view), o = /Still open: the ([a-z]+) test/.exec(view);
+          const sum = `files: ${f ? f.slice(1).join(', ') : '?'}\ndecided: keep the retry in the ${k ? k[1] : '?'} module\nopen: the ${o ? o[1] : '?'} test is skipped`;
+          reply = /Write the summary/.test(q) ? sum : block('return await read("notes");').replace(close, '') + '\n' + sum + close;
+        }
+        else if (/The file called window does not exist/.test(q)) reply = (!forgetsRule || /Apply the project convention/.test(q) || /=== THE CONVERSATION SO FAR/.test(q)) ? block('return await read("ladder");') : block('return await write("window", "");');
+        else if ((m = /the next file to read is the one called ([a-z]+)/.exec(q.split('remote-workspace-result').slice(-2).join('')) || /is in the file called ([a-z]+)/.exec(q.split('remote-workspace-result').slice(-2).join('')))) reply = noBare && !framed ? `I would read the file called ${m[1]} next.` : block(`return await read("${m[1]}");`);
+        else if (/the trail has ended/.test(q)) reply = `The color was ${(/the color to remember is ([a-z]+)/.exec(view) || [, 'unknown'])[1]}.${close}`;
+        else if (/start by listing the files|Read the file called|read the file called window and tell me/.test(q)) reply = block(`return await ${/listing/.test(q) ? 'list(".")' : 'read("' + ((/[Rr]ead the file called ([a-z]+)/.exec(q) || [, 'window'])[1]) + '")'};`);
+        else if (/Repeat the three things back now/.test(q)) { const s = [/The storage path is: (\S+)/.exec(q)[1], /The helper name is: (\S+)/.exec(q)[1], /The call line is: ([^\n]+)/.exec(q)[1]]; reply = rewrites ? s.map((x) => x.replace(/[a-z]{3}/, 'xxx')).join('\n') : s.join('\n'); }
+        else if (/What is the storage path/.test(q)) reply = /The storage path is: (\S+)/.exec(prior)[1];
+        else if (/What is the helper name/.test(q)) reply = /The helper name is: (\S+)/.exec(prior)[1];
+        else if (/What is the call line/.test(q)) reply = /The call line is: ([^\n]+)/.exec(prior)[1];
+        else if ((m = [...q.matchAll(/the units that record (\d+) holds: ([^\n]+)/g)]).length) reply = 'answers: ' + m.map((x) => { const has = new RegExp(`^Record ${x[1]}: .*? holds (\\d+) units`, 'm').exec(prior); const opt = has && [...x[2].matchAll(/write ([a-z]+) for (\d+)/g)].find((o) => o[2] === has[1]); return opt ? opt[1] : 'nothing'; }).join(' ');
+        if (/^Reference SPEC-/.test(q) && /Reply with exactly: 0/.test(q) && q.length < 20000) hist.planted = true;
+        hist.texts.push(q); lastId = id;
+        return { text: reply, ms: 900, raw: { gemini_enterprise: { session: id, assist_token: 't' + (++n) } }, usage };
+      };
+      return { call, sent };
+    };
+    const runT = async (name, o) => { const g = gwT(o); const v = await r9(name).plan({ call: g.call, model: SEL }); return { ...v, g }; };
+    const R = 'a-tool-result-alone-on-the-handle-gets-a-well-formed-next-call';
+    const bareOk = await runT(R);
+    ok(bareOk.holds === true && /bare=YYYN framed=YYYN full=YYYN/.test(bareOk.measured), `9r: a result alone on the handle gets a clean next call for the right file, and the turn is closed (${bareOk.measured})`);
+    const needsFrame = await runT(R, { noBare: true });
+    ok(needsFrame.holds === false && /bare=N/.test(needsFrame.measured) && /must carry the turn headings/.test(needsFrame.note), `when only the framed form works, a follow-up must carry the framing (${needsFrame.measured})`);
+    const S = 'thinking-does-not-show-in-what-a-reply-is-billed';

+    const billed = await runT(S, { thinks: true });
+    ok(billed.holds === false && /thinking shows in the usage/.test(billed.note) && /mechanical calls can ask for low/.test(billed.note), 'more tokens on the hard question, and far fewer at low effort, is said as both');
+    const T = 'a-full-send-with-no-handle-starts-clean';

+    ok(clean.holds === true && /on handle=R \| no handle: cut=N grown=N top-changed=N unrelated=N \| original still=R/.test(clean.measured), `9t: no handle, no memory of the earlier session (${clean.measured})`);
+    const leak = await runT(T, { leaky: true });
+    ok(leak.holds === false && /cut=R/.test(leak.measured) && /piling|adding to/.test(leak.note), `a handle-less send answered from the old session breaks it (${leak.measured})`);
+    const U = 'a-handle-already-used-is-refused';
+    const shared = await runT(U);
+    ok(shared.holds === false && /stale=bottle/.test(shared.measured) && /ONE line of conversation/.test(shared.note) && /first=table window second=table window/.test(shared.measured), `9u: a used handle that sees what came after it is one shared line (${shared.measured})`);
+    const refused = await runT(U, { staleRefused: true });
+    ok(refused.holds === true && /stale=refused/.test(refused.measured), 'a used handle that is refused holds the claim');
+    const V = 'task-summaries-answer-as-well-as-the-full-text';
+    const sumOk = await runT(V);

+    const liar = await runT(X, { invents: true });
+    ok(liar.holds === false && /never planted=WW/.test(liar.measured) && /INVENTED/.test(liar.note), 'an invented answer for a key never planted breaks it');

+    // idle gap: plant, then check - on the same running process
+    const gIdle = gwT();

+    ok(plant.holds === true && /planted=3/.test(plant.measured), `9ab: three sessions planted (${plant.measured})`);
+    const check = await r9('a-handle-is-still-good-after-an-idle-gap').plan({ call: gIdle.call, model: SEL });
+    ok(check.holds === true && /idle 0 min: accepted=Y same session=Y opening exact=R control=N \| \d+ left to check/.test(check.measured), `9ac: the planted handle is checked with two exact lookups against a handle-less control (${check.measured})`);

+    // PRE-FLIGHT for all thirteen: the words they turn on go through the shipped masker unmasked
